# Folio M0~M2 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Folio 코어의 저장소(M1)와 단계 실행기(M2)를 fixture 4개 위에서 TDD로 세운다. 모델 없이 가짜 팩·가짜 호스트로 큰 틀부터/바로 쓰기가 끝까지 돌고, 잠금 규칙은 어떤 경로로도 우회되지 않는다.

**Architecture:** 작품 폴더의 파일이 정본이고 `.folio/ledger/*.jsonl`(해시 체인, 추가만)이 기록장이다. `core/store`가 파일을 읽어 트리·엔티티·자료·그림 모델과 읽는 순서 위치를 만들고, `core/index`는 그걸 `index.db`로 투영한다(지워도 재생성). `core/run`의 실행기는 팩이 준 단계 정의를 데이터로 받아 `needs_model`/`needs_user`를 돌려주고, 진행 상태를 `.folio/runs/*.json`에 매번 저장해 프로세스가 죽어도 이어간다. 커밋은 `core/run/commit.js` 한 곳에서만 일어나며 hash·영수증·hard 잠금을 검사한다.

**Tech Stack:** Node ESM + JSDoc, `node:test`, `node:sqlite`, `yaml`(런타임 의존성 1개), ESLint 9 + TypeScript(`checkJs`, 개발용).

**Spec:** 착수 계획서 아티팩트 https://claude.ai/artifact/WjJzcsjnQZnVxyyJZaxvcX (유일한 설계 정본). 이식·판정 기준: `/path/to/vibelore` (읽기 전용).

## Global Constraints

- 이름 `Folio`, MCP 도구 접두어 `folio_`, 작품 내부 폴더 `.folio/`.
- `package.json` `"engines": { "node": "^22.13.0 || ^24.0.0 || ^26.0.0" }`, `"type": "module"`.
- 런타임 의존성은 `yaml` 하나. MCP SDK는 M3에서 추가. 네이티브 모듈 금지.
- `core/`는 모델·네트워크를 부르지 않는다 (`fetch(`, `node:http`, `node:https`, `node:net` import 금지 — Task 1 가드 테스트).
- 잠금 규칙(설정으로 끌 수 없음): 커밋 원고 hash = 검사 원고 hash · 영수증 없이 커밋 금지 · hard 충돌이면 커밋 차단 · 기록장은 추가만 · 같은 호스트 자기검토를 독립 평가로 표기 금지 · soft·참고 의견은 자동 수정 사유 아님 · 엔진은 `needs_model`/`needs_user`를 돌려주고 resume으로 이어감.
- 테스트 데이터는 전부 합성 fixture. 실제 작품·생성 결과물 금지. vibelore 파일은 읽기만 한다.
- 파일 하나에 한 책임. 300줄을 넘기면 나눈다.
- 문서·주석 설명은 한국어 가능, 코드 식별자는 영어. 커밋 메시지는 영어 Conventional Commits, 끝에 `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- 라이선스 Apache-2.0 (첫 커밋 전에 사용자 확인 — Task 1 Step 1).
- 코드를 storyai·story-skills(MIT)에서 가져오면 해당 파일 머리와 `NOTICE`에 저작권 고지를 남긴다. 이 계획 범위(M0~M2)에서는 코드를 가져오지 않는다.

## 계획서와 vibelore를 대조해 정한 것

| # | 어긋난 곳 | 이 계획의 처리 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | vibelore의 hard 판정은 모델 추출(`extractDelta`) 뒤에 나온다. 계획서 단계표는 `draft → facts(deterministic)` | 맨 아래 칸 기본 단계에 `extract`(generate, `produces: events`)를 넣고 `facts`가 `requires: [extract]`. 잠금 단계가 요구하는 단계는 자동으로 잠긴다 |
| 2 | vibelore hook 단계 `planted/advancing/paid/parked`. 계획서는 심기→강화→속임수→해소→닫힘 | 코어 약속 단계 `planted, advanced, misdirected, resolved, closed, parked` (parked는 vibelore 호환) |
| 3 | vibelore는 critic 미완료 시 auto 커밋을 막는다 | M2는 계획서대로 건너뜀을 영수증에 남기고 진행. M5 parity에서 재판단 (판정 맞추기 3항목엔 안 걸림) |
| 4 | vibelore는 검사 뒤 계약이 바뀌면 검사를 무효화한다 | 커밋 직전 참고한 버전 목록을 다시 계산해 다르면 검사부터 다시 돈다 |
| 5 | vibelore 영수증은 한 번만 쓴다 | `LOCK_RECEIPT_CONSUMED` 잠금 추가 |
| 6 | FTS5 없는 SQLite 대응 | M1 index.db는 FTS 없이. 검색은 M4 |
| 7 | 계획서 M1 "fixture 3개" | 4개로 통일 |
| 8 | 로컬 Node 23.11은 engines 범위 밖 | 로컬 테스트는 그대로 돌고, 범위 검증은 CI 매트릭스(22.13.0/22/24/26) |

## 코어 자리 4개 (v0.1 기본값)

| 자리 | 저장 | v0.1 동작 |
|---|---|---|
| `order` | `work.yaml` `order: linear` 또는 `{ default, levels: { <level>: mode } }`, 노드 frontmatter `links` | `linear`만 앞뒤 판정. `none`/`branching`은 앞뒤 검사가 `not_applicable`. 연결 표는 저장·조회만 |
| 판본 | `work.yaml` `variants: [main]`, 영수증·커밋 기록 `variant` | `main` 외 값은 설정 오류 `VARIANT_UNSUPPORTED` |
| 블록 id | 본문 `{#fig-3-2}` 앵커, 참조 `[[block:fig-3-2]]` | 앵커 읽기·index 저장, 깨진 참조·중복 앵커 탐지 |
| `role` | 노드 frontmatter `role: front|body|back` (기본 body) | front/back과 그 자손은 읽는 순서 판정에서 빠짐 |

**데이터 흐름 한 줄:** `openWork(root)` → `{ config, tree, positions, entities, sources, assets, blocks }` → 실행기는 이 모델 + 기록장(`receipts`, `events`)만 보고 판단하고, 파일 쓰기는 `structure/ops.js`(구조)와 `run/commit.js`(본문 확정) 두 곳뿐이다.

```yaml
name: CI
on:
  push:
    branches: [main]

    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    timeout-minutes: 15

      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - { os: ubuntu-latest, node: '22.13.0' }
          - { os: ubuntu-latest, node: '22' }
          - { os: ubuntu-latest, node: '24' }
          - { os: macos-latest, node: '24' }
          - { os: windows-latest, node: '24' }
          - { os: ubuntu-latest, node: '26' }
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ matrix.node }}
      - run: npm ci
      - run: npm run check
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/core/no-model-calls.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const FORBIDDEN = [
  /\bfetch\s*\(/,
  /from\s+['"]node:https?['"]/,
  /from\s+['"]node:net['"]/,
  /@anthropic-ai|openai/,
];

async function* walk(dir) {
  for (const entry of await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const path = join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) yield* walk(path);
    else if (path.endsWith('.js')) yield path;
  }
}

test('core never calls a model or the network', async () => {
  const offenders = [];
  let files = 0;
  for await (const file of walk('core')) {
    files += 1;
    const text = await readFile(file, 'utf8');
    for (const pattern of FORBIDDEN) if (pattern.test(text)) offenders.push(`${file}: ${pattern}`);
  }
  assert.ok(files > 0, 'core/ has no .js files');
  assert.deepEqual(offenders, []);
});
```

`test/core/errors.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { FolioError } from '../../core/errors.js';

test('FolioError carries a code and details', () => {
  const error = new FolioError('LOCK_NO_RECEIPT', 'commit requires a receipt', { nodeId: 'a' });
  assert.ok(error instanceof Error);
  assert.equal(error.code, 'LOCK_NO_RECEIPT');
  assert.deepEqual(error.details, { nodeId: 'a' });
  assert.match(error.message, /^LOCK_NO_RECEIPT: /);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 실패 확인**

Run: `node --test test/core/`
Expected: FAIL — `core/` 없음, `Cannot find module '../../core/errors.js'`.

- [ ] **Step 5: 구현**

`core/errors.js`:
```js
/** 코어의 모든 오류. `code`로 판정하고 message는 사람용이다. */
export class FolioError extends Error {
  /**
   * @param {string} code
   * @param {string} message
   * @param {Record<string, unknown>} [details]
   */
  constructor(code, message, details = {}) {
    super(`${code}: ${message}`);
    this.name = 'FolioError';
    this.code = code;
    this.details = details;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 통과 확인**

Run: `npm run check`
Expected: lint 0 errors, typecheck 0 errors, 2 tests PASS.

### Task 2: 합성 fixture 작품 4개 + 사건 시드

각 작품에 위반을 일부러 하나 이상 심는다: 소설(심기 전 해소, 사망 뒤 등장), 연재(사망 뒤 등장), 지식 책(정의 전 사용, 깨진 상호 참조), 게임북(도달 불가 결말).

**Files:**
- Create: `test/fixtures/novel-3level/**`, `test/fixtures/serial-2level/**`, `test/fixtures/knowledge-book/**`, `test/fixtures/gamebook/**`, `test/fixtures/seeds/*.events.yaml`
- Test: `test/fixtures.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: fixture 폴더 규약 — `work.yaml`, `tree/`(폴더 = 가지 노드, `_node.md` 필수, 그 외 `*.md` = 잎 노드), `entities/<kind>/<id>.md`, `sources/*.yaml`, `assets/*.yaml`, `checks/*.md`. 모든 md는 `---\n<yaml>---\n<본문>` 형식이고 YAML은 `yaml` 라이브러리 `stringify({lineWidth:0})` 출력과 바이트 단위로 같아야 한다(Task 5에서 검증).
- Produces: 시드 형식 `test/fixtures/seeds/<fixture>.events.yaml` = `[{ nodeId, entityId, kind, data? }]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/fixtures.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readdir, readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const FIXTURES = ['novel-3level', 'serial-2level', 'knowledge-book', 'gamebook'];

async function* markdown(dir) {
  for (const entry of await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const path = join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) yield* markdown(path);
    else if (entry.name.endsWith('.md')) yield path;
  }
}

for (const name of FIXTURES) {
  test(`fixture ${name} has a work.yaml, a tree and unique node ids`, async () => {
    const root = join('test/fixtures', name);
    assert.ok((await stat(join(root, 'work.yaml'))).isFile());
    const ids = [];
    for await (const file of markdown(join(root, 'tree'))) {
      const text = await readFile(file, 'utf8');
      assert.ok(text.startsWith('---\n'), `${file} has no frontmatter`);
      const id = /\nid: ([^\n]+)\n/.exec(`\n${text}`)?.[1];
      assert.ok(id, `${file} has no id`);
      ids.push(id);
    }
    assert.ok(ids.length >= 6, `${name} has only ${ids.length} nodes`);
    assert.equal(new Set(ids).size, ids.length, `${name} has duplicate ids`);
    assert.ok((await stat(join('test/fixtures/seeds', `${name}.events.yaml`))).isFile());
  });
}
```

# M1 저장소

M1 완료 기준(계획서 + 요청):
1. fixture 4개 모두 읽고 쓰기 왕복이 동일 (모델 동일 + md/yaml 파일 바이트 동일)
2. 서로 다른 가지·층의 두 노드 앞뒤 판정이 맞음
3. `index.db`를 지우고 다시 만들어도 조회 결과 동일
4. 파일을 손으로 고치면 감지

### Task 3: 기본 유틸 — hash, 원자적 쓰기, YAML, frontmatter

**Files:**
- Create: `core/util/hash.js`, `core/util/fs.js`, `core/util/yaml.js`, `core/store/frontmatter.js`
- Test: `test/core/util.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `sha256(value: string|Uint8Array): string` → `'sha256:<64 hex>'`
  - `canonicalJson(value): string` — 모든 깊이에서 키 정렬, `undefined` 키 제거
  - `shortRev(hash: string): string` — hex 앞 12자
  - `writeFileAtomic(path, data): Promise<void>` — 폴더 생성 + tmp 파일 후 rename
  - `readTextOrNull(path): Promise<string|null>` — ENOENT면 null
  - `listEntries(dir): Promise<Dirent[]>` — `.`으로 시작하는 항목 제외, 이름 코드포인트 순, 없는 폴더는 `[]`
  - `parseYaml(text): any` (빈 문서 → `{}`), `stringifyYaml(value): string` (`lineWidth: 0`)
  - `parseDoc(text): { data: object, body: string }`, `serializeDoc({ data, body }): string`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/core/util.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtemp, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { sha256, canonicalJson, shortRev } from '../../core/util/hash.js';
import { writeFileAtomic, readTextOrNull, listEntries } from '../../core/util/fs.js';
import { parseYaml, stringifyYaml } from '../../core/util/yaml.js';
import { parseDoc, serializeDoc } from '../../core/store/frontmatter.js';

test('sha256 returns a prefixed hex digest for text and bytes', () => {
  assert.match(sha256('a'), /^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  assert.equal(sha256('a'), sha256(Buffer.from('a')));
  assert.notEqual(sha256('a'), sha256('b'));
});

test('canonicalJson sorts keys at every depth and drops undefined', () => {
  assert.equal(
    canonicalJson({ b: 1, a: { d: [2, { z: 1, y: 2 }], c: undefined } }),
    '{"a":{"d":[2,{"y":2,"z":1}]},"b":1}',
  );
  assert.equal(canonicalJson(null), 'null');
});

test('shortRev keeps the first 12 hex characters', () => {
  assert.equal(shortRev(sha256('a')).length, 12);
});

test('yaml helpers parse empty documents and write without line folding', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseYaml(''), {});
  const long = 'x'.repeat(200);
  assert.equal(stringifyYaml({ a: long }), `a: ${long}\n`);
});

test('parseDoc splits frontmatter and body and serializeDoc restores the bytes', () => {
  const text = '---\nid: a\nlevel: scene\n---\n본문\n';
  const doc = parseDoc(text);
  assert.deepEqual(doc.data, { id: 'a', level: 'scene' });
  assert.equal(doc.body, '본문\n');
  assert.equal(serializeDoc(doc), text);
});

test('parseDoc without frontmatter keeps the whole text as body', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseDoc('그냥 글\n'), { data: {}, body: '그냥 글\n' });
});

test('parseDoc rejects an unclosed fence', () => {
  assert.throws(() => parseDoc('---\nid: a\n'), { code: 'FRONTMATTER_UNCLOSED' });
});

test('file helpers write atomically, read missing files as null and list sorted entries', async () => {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'folio-util-'));
  await writeFileAtomic(join(dir, 'deep', 'x.txt'), 'hi');
  assert.equal(await readTextOrNull(join(dir, 'deep', 'x.txt')), 'hi');
  assert.equal(await readTextOrNull(join(dir, 'missing.txt')), null);
  await mkdir(join(dir, 'list'));
  for (const name of ['b.md', 'a.md', '.hidden', '10-x.md', '02-y.md']) await writeFile(join(dir, 'list', name), '');
  assert.deepEqual((await listEntries(join(dir, 'list'))).map((e) => e.name), ['02-y.md', '10-x.md', 'a.md', 'b.md']);
  assert.deepEqual(await listEntries(join(dir, 'nope')), []);
});
```

`core/util/hash.js`:
```js
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

/** @param {string|Uint8Array} value */
export function sha256(value) {
  const input = typeof value === 'string' || value instanceof Uint8Array ? value : String(value);
  return `sha256:${createHash('sha256').update(input).digest('hex')}`;
}

/** 키를 정렬한 JSON. 해시의 입력으로만 쓴다. */
export function canonicalJson(value) {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map((item) => canonicalJson(item ?? null)).join(',')}]`;
  const keys = Object.keys(value).filter((key) => value[key] !== undefined).sort();
  return `{${keys.map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(value[key])}`).join(',')}}`;
}

export const shortRev = (hash) => String(hash).replace(/^sha256:/, '').slice(0, 12);
```

`core/util/fs.js`:
```js
import { mkdir, readdir, readFile, rename, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname } from 'node:path';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';

/** 같은 폴더의 임시 파일에 쓰고 rename 한다. 중간에 죽어도 반쯤 쓴 파일이 남지 않는다. */
export async function writeFileAtomic(path, data) {
  await mkdir(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
  const tmp = `${path}.${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(tmp, data);
  await rename(tmp, path);
}

export async function readTextOrNull(path) {
  try { return await readFile(path, 'utf8'); }
  catch (error) { if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error; }
}

/** 숨김 항목을 뺀 폴더 목록. 정렬은 코드포인트 순이라 OS와 무관하게 같다. */
export async function listEntries(dir) {
  try {
    const entries = await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
    return entries
      .filter((entry) => !entry.name.startsWith('.'))
      .sort((a, b) => (a.name < b.name ? -1 : a.name > b.name ? 1 : 0));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return [];
    throw error;
  }
}
```

`core/util/yaml.js`:
```js
import YAML from 'yaml';

export const parseYaml = (text) => YAML.parse(text) ?? {};
export const stringifyYaml = (value) => YAML.stringify(value, { lineWidth: 0 });
```

`core/store/frontmatter.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { parseYaml, stringifyYaml } from '../util/yaml.js';

const OPEN = '---\n';
const CLOSE = '\n---\n';

/** `---\n<yaml>---\n<body>` 를 나눈다. frontmatter가 없으면 전체가 본문이다. */
export function parseDoc(text) {
  if (!text.startsWith(OPEN)) return { data: {}, body: text };
  const close = text.indexOf(CLOSE, OPEN.length - 1);
  if (close === -1) throw new FolioError('FRONTMATTER_UNCLOSED', 'frontmatter closing fence is missing');
  return { data: parseYaml(text.slice(OPEN.length, close + 1)), body: text.slice(close + CLOSE.length) };
}

export function serializeDoc({ data, body }) {
  if (!data || Object.keys(data).length === 0) return body;
  return `${OPEN}${stringifyYaml(data)}---\n${body}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/work/config.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { readTextOrNull } from '../util/fs.js';
import { parseYaml } from '../util/yaml.js';

export const ORDER_MODES = ['linear', 'none', 'branching'];
export const SUPPORTED_VARIANTS = ['main'];
const LEVEL_NAME = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/;
const isPlainObject = (value) => value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
const isRange = (value) => Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 2
  && value.every((n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0) && value[0] <= value[1];

function readLevels(input, errors) {
  if (!Array.isArray(input) || input.length === 0) {
    errors.push({ code: 'LEVELS_REQUIRED', message: 'levels must be a non-empty list' });
    return [];
  }
  const seen = new Set();
  return input.map((level, index) => {
    const name = level?.name;
    const leaf = index === input.length - 1;
    if (typeof name !== 'string' || !LEVEL_NAME.test(name)) errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_NAME_INVALID', index });
    else if (seen.has(name)) errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_NAME_DUPLICATE', name });
    seen.add(name);
    if (level?.leaf === true && !leaf) errors.push({ code: 'LEAF_NOT_LAST', name });
    let children = null;
    if (level?.children !== undefined && level?.children !== null) {
      if (!leaf && isRange(level.children)) children = [level.children[0], level.children[1]];
      else errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_CHILDREN_INVALID', name });
    }
    return { name, leaf, children };
  });
}

function readOrder(input, levelNames, errors) {
  if (input === undefined) return { default: 'linear', levels: {} };
  if (typeof input === 'string') {
    if (!ORDER_MODES.includes(input)) errors.push({ code: 'ORDER_MODE_INVALID', value: input });
    return { default: input, levels: {} };
  }
  if (!isPlainObject(input)) {
    errors.push({ code: 'ORDER_MODE_INVALID', value: input });
    return { default: 'linear', levels: {} };
  }
  const order = { default: input.default ?? 'linear', levels: {} };
  if (!ORDER_MODES.includes(order.default)) errors.push({ code: 'ORDER_MODE_INVALID', value: order.default });
  for (const [level, mode] of Object.entries(input.levels ?? {})) {
    if (!levelNames.includes(level)) errors.push({ code: 'ORDER_LEVEL_UNKNOWN', level });
    else if (!ORDER_MODES.includes(mode)) errors.push({ code: 'ORDER_MODE_INVALID', value: mode });
    else order.levels[level] = mode;
  }
  return order;
}

function readList(input, code, errors) {
  if (input === undefined) return [];
  if (!Array.isArray(input) || !input.every((item) => typeof item === 'string')) {
    errors.push({ code });
    return [];
  }
  return [...input];
}

function readMap(input, code, errors) {
  if (input === undefined) return {};
  if (!isPlainObject(input)) {
    errors.push({ code });
    return {};
  }
  return structuredClone(input);
}

/** work.yaml 원본 객체를 정규화한다. 예외를 던지지 않고 오류 목록을 돌려준다. */
export function validateWorkConfig(raw) {
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];
  const input = isPlainObject(raw) ? raw : {};
  const levels = readLevels(input.levels, errors);
  const order = readOrder(input.order, levels.map((l) => l.name), errors);
  const variants = input.variants === undefined ? [...SUPPORTED_VARIANTS] : readList(input.variants, 'VARIANTS_INVALID', errors);
  if (variants.some((v) => !SUPPORTED_VARIANTS.includes(v))) errors.push({ code: 'VARIANT_UNSUPPORTED', variants });
  const config = {
    title: typeof input.title === 'string' ? input.title : '',
    levels,
    order,
    variants: [...SUPPORTED_VARIANTS],
    packs: readList(input.packs, 'PACKS_INVALID', errors),
    checkSets: readList(input.checkSets, 'CHECK_SETS_INVALID', errors),
    checks: readMap(input.checks, 'CHECKS_INVALID', errors),
    overrides: readMap(input.overrides, 'OVERRIDES_INVALID', errors),
  };
  return { config, errors, warnings };
}

export async function loadWorkConfig(root) {
  const text = await readTextOrNull(join(root, 'work.yaml'));
  if (text === null) throw new FolioError('WORK_NOT_FOUND', `no work.yaml in ${root}`);
  const { config, errors, warnings } = validateWorkConfig(parseYaml(text));
  if (errors.length) throw new FolioError('CONFIG_INVALID', errors.map((e) => e.code).join(', '), { errors });
  return { config, warnings };
}

export const levelIndex = (config, name) => config.levels.findIndex((level) => level.name === name);
export const orderModeFor = (config, levelName) => config.order.levels[levelName] ?? config.order.default;
```

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdir, readFile, rename, rm, writeFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { readTree, writeTree, preorder, ancestorsOf } from '../../core/store/tree.js';
import { validateTreeLevels } from '../../core/store/tree-levels.js';
import { loadWorkConfig } from '../../core/work/config.js';
import { FIXTURES, fixturePath, copyFixture, tempDir } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function mdFiles(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true, recursive: true })) {
    if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(relative(dir, join(entry.parentPath, entry.name)));
  }
  return out.sort();
}

test('readTree builds nested nodes with stable ids, kinds and parents', async () => {
  const tree = await readTree(fixturePath('novel-3level'));
  assert.deepEqual(tree.errors, []);
  assert.deepEqual(tree.rootIds, ['book-one', 'book-two']);
  assert.deepEqual(tree.nodes.get('book-one').children, ['the-fall', 'the-letter']);
  const scene = tree.nodes.get('rooftop-scene');
  assert.equal(scene.kind, 'leaf');
  assert.equal(scene.parentId, 'the-fall');
  assert.equal(scene.path, '01-book-one/01-the-fall/01-rooftop.md');
  assert.equal(scene.role, 'body');
  assert.equal(scene.status, 'draft');
  assert.deepEqual(scene.entities, ['mara', 'rooftop']);
  assert.deepEqual(scene.assets, ['img-rooftop']);
  assert.equal(tree.nodes.get('the-fall').kind, 'branch');
  assert.equal(tree.nodes.get('the-fall').path, '01-book-one/01-the-fall/_node.md');
  assert.deepEqual(preorder(tree), [
    'book-one', 'the-fall', 'rooftop-scene', 'stairwell', 'the-letter', 'post-office', 'last-night',
    'book-two', 'after', 'funeral', 'market',
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(ancestorsOf(tree, 'funeral').map((n) => n.id), ['after', 'book-two']);
});

test('gamebook links are read as a table of choices', async () => {
  const tree = await readTree(fixturePath('gamebook'));
  assert.deepEqual(tree.nodes.get('gate').links, [
    { to: 'left-path', kind: 'choice', label: '왼쪽 길' },
    { to: 'right-path', kind: 'choice', label: '오른쪽 길' },
  ]);
});

  assert.equal(tree.nodes.get('rooftop-scene').parentId, 'the-fall');

  const root = await tempDir('folio-tree-');
  await mkdir(join(root, 'tree/01-empty'), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(join(root, 'tree/02-a.md'), '---\nid: a\nlevel: scene\n---\nx\n');
  await writeFile(join(root, 'tree/03-a.md'), '---\nid: a\nlevel: scene\n---\ny\n');

for (const name of FIXTURES) {

  const { config } = await loadWorkConfig(root);

  await writeFile(join(root, 'tree/03-ghost.md'), '---\nid: ghost\nlevel: ghost\n---\nx\n');
  await rm(join(root, 'tree/02-book-two/01-after'), { recursive: true });
  const { errors, warnings } = validateTreeLevels(await readTree(root), config);
  assert.deepEqual(errors.map((e) => `${e.code}:${e.id}`).sort(), [
    'LEVEL_KIND_MISMATCH:loose', 'LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID:deep', 'LEVEL_UNKNOWN:ghost',
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(warnings, []);
});
```

`core/store/tree.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { listEntries, readTextOrNull, writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';
import { parseDoc, serializeDoc } from './frontmatter.js';

export const NODE_FILE = '_node.md';
export const TREE_DIR = 'tree';
export const ROLES = ['front', 'body', 'back'];
export const NODE_STATUSES = ['draft', 'planned', 'committed', 'imported'];
const ID = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/;

/** @typedef {{ to: string, kind: string, label: string }} NodeLink */
/**
 * @typedef {{ id: string, level: string, role: string, status: string, title: string,
 *   kind: 'branch'|'leaf', path: string, parentId: string|null, siblingIndex: number,
 *   children: string[], links: NodeLink[], assets: string[], entities: string[],
 *   data: Record<string, any>, body: string }} TreeNode
 */
/** @typedef {{ nodes: Map<string, TreeNode>, rootIds: string[], errors: Array<Record<string, any>> }} Tree */

function readLinks(raw, path, errors) {
  if (raw === undefined) return [];
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) { errors.push({ code: 'LINK_INVALID', path }); return []; }
  const links = [];
  for (const link of raw) {
    if (typeof link?.to !== 'string' || !link.to) { errors.push({ code: 'LINK_INVALID', path }); continue; }
    links.push({ to: link.to, kind: typeof link.kind === 'string' ? link.kind : 'see-also', label: typeof link.label === 'string' ? link.label : '' });
  }
  return links;
}

const stringList = (value) => (Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item) => typeof item === 'string') : []);

function toNode(text, path, kind, parentId, errors) {
  const { data, body } = parseDoc(text);
  if (typeof data.id !== 'string' || !ID.test(data.id)) { errors.push({ code: 'NODE_ID_INVALID', path }); return null; }
  if (typeof data.level !== 'string') { errors.push({ code: 'NODE_LEVEL_MISSING', path, id: data.id }); return null; }
  const role = data.role ?? 'body';
  if (!ROLES.includes(role)) errors.push({ code: 'NODE_ROLE_INVALID', path, id: data.id });
  const status = data.status ?? 'draft';
  if (!NODE_STATUSES.includes(status)) errors.push({ code: 'NODE_STATUS_INVALID', path, id: data.id });
  return {
    id: data.id, level: data.level, role, status, title: typeof data.title === 'string' ? data.title : '',
    kind, path, parentId, siblingIndex: 0, children: [],
    links: readLinks(data.links, path, errors), assets: stringList(data.assets), entities: stringList(data.entities),
    data, body,
  };
}

/** tree/ 를 읽는다. 폴더는 가지(_node.md 필수), 그 밖의 .md 파일은 잎이다. */
export async function readTree(root) {
  /** @type {Tree} */
  const tree = { nodes: new Map(), rootIds: [], errors: [] };
  const register = (node, siblings) => {
    if (tree.nodes.has(node.id)) { tree.errors.push({ code: 'DUPLICATE_ID', id: node.id, path: node.path }); return false; }
    node.siblingIndex = siblings.length;
    tree.nodes.set(node.id, node);
    siblings.push(node.id);
    return true;
  };
  async function visit(dir, relDir, parentId, siblings) {
    for (const entry of await listEntries(dir)) {
      const rel = relDir ? `${relDir}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        const text = await readTextOrNull(join(dir, entry.name, NODE_FILE));
        if (text === null) { tree.errors.push({ code: 'NODE_FILE_MISSING', path: rel }); continue; }
        const node = toNode(text, `${rel}/${NODE_FILE}`, 'branch', parentId, tree.errors);
        if (node && register(node, siblings)) await visit(join(dir, entry.name), rel, node.id, node.children);
      } else if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith('.md') && entry.name !== NODE_FILE) {
        const node = toNode(await readFile(join(dir, entry.name), 'utf8'), rel, 'leaf', parentId, tree.errors);
        if (node) register(node, siblings);
      }
    }
  }
  await visit(join(root, TREE_DIR), '', null, tree.rootIds);
  return tree;
}

export const nodeFilePath = (root, node) => join(root, TREE_DIR, node.path);

export async function writeNode(root, node) {
  await writeFileAtomic(nodeFilePath(root, node), serializeDoc({ data: node.data, body: node.body }));
}

export async function writeTree(root, tree) {
  for (const node of tree.nodes.values()) await writeNode(root, node);
}

/** frontmatter 필드와 본문을 바꿔 쓴다. 모델 필드(status/title/role)도 같이 맞춘다. */
export async function updateNode(root, node, { data = {}, body } = {}) {
  Object.assign(node.data, data);
  for (const key of ['status', 'title', 'role']) if (key in data) node[key] = data[key];
  if (body !== undefined) node.body = body;
  await writeNode(root, node);
  return node;
}

export function preorder(tree) {
  const out = [];
  const walk = (ids) => { for (const id of ids) { out.push(id); walk(tree.nodes.get(id).children); } };
  walk(tree.rootIds);
  return out;
}

export function ancestorsOf(tree, id) {
  const out = [];
  let current = tree.nodes.get(id)?.parentId ?? null;
  while (current) {
    const node = tree.nodes.get(current);
    out.push(node);
    current = node.parentId;
  }
  return out;
}
```

`core/store/tree-levels.js`:
```js
import { levelIndex } from '../work/config.js';

/** 레벨 정의와 트리가 맞는지 본다. 개수 범위는 경고만 한다(계획서: 막지 않음). */
export function validateTreeLevels(tree, config) {
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];
  for (const node of tree.nodes.values()) {
    const index = levelIndex(config, node.level);
    if (index < 0) { errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_UNKNOWN', id: node.id, level: node.level }); continue; }
    const level = config.levels[index];
    if (level.leaf !== (node.kind === 'leaf')) errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_KIND_MISMATCH', id: node.id, level: node.level, kind: node.kind });
    const parent = node.parentId ? tree.nodes.get(node.parentId) : null;
    if (parent && levelIndex(config, parent.level) >= index) errors.push({ code: 'LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID', id: node.id, parent: parent.id });
    if (!parent && index !== 0 && node.role === 'body') warnings.push({ code: 'ROOT_LEVEL_NOT_TOP', id: node.id, level: node.level });
    if (node.kind === 'branch' && level.children && node.children.length > 0) {
      const count = node.children.length;
      if (count < level.children[0] || count > level.children[1]) {
        warnings.push({ code: 'LEVEL_CHILDREN_OUT_OF_RANGE', id: node.id, count, range: level.children });
      }
    }
  }
  return { errors, warnings };
}
```

### Task 6: 읽는 순서 위치와 앞뒤 판정

**Files:**
- Create: `core/store/position.js`
- Test: `test/core/position.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Tree`, `WorkConfig`, `orderModeFor`
- Produces:
  - `Position = { ordinal: number /* 전위 순번, 0부터 */, key: string /* '0001.0003.0002' */, orderMode: string, ordered: boolean }`
  - `computePositions(tree, config): Map<string, Position>` — `ordered = orderMode === 'linear' && role === 'body' && (부모 없음 || 부모.ordered)`
  - `compareNodes(positions, aId, bId): 'before'|'after'|'same'|'not_applicable'` — 없는 id면 `FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND')`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { rename } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { readTree } from '../../core/store/tree.js';
import { loadWorkConfig, validateWorkConfig } from '../../core/work/config.js';
import { computePositions, compareNodes } from '../../core/store/position.js';
import { fixturePath, copyFixture } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function positionsOf(root, configPatch) {
  let { config } = await loadWorkConfig(root);
  if (configPatch) config = validateWorkConfig({ ...config, ...configPatch }).config;
  return computePositions(await readTree(root), config);
}

test('different branches and levels compare in reading order', async () => {
  const p = await positionsOf(fixturePath('novel-3level'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'rooftop-scene', 'funeral'), 'before');   // 1권 장면 vs 2권 장면
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'funeral', 'stairwell'), 'after');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'the-letter', 'stairwell'), 'after');     // 장 vs 앞 장의 장면
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'book-two', 'last-night'), 'after');      // 책 vs 앞 책의 장면
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'book-one', 'rooftop-scene'), 'before');  // 계획이 자식보다 앞
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'post-office', 'post-office'), 'same');
  assert.equal(p.get('rooftop-scene').key, '0001.0001.0001');
  assert.equal(p.get('funeral').key, '0002.0001.0001');
});

test('front and back matter stay outside the reading order', async () => {
  const p = await positionsOf(fixturePath('knowledge-book'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'preface', 'membranes'), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'tissues', 'glossary'), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'membranes', 'atp-section'), 'before');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'tissues', 'what-is-a-cell'), 'after');
});

test('branching and unordered works are not_applicable', async () => {
  const p = await positionsOf(fixturePath('gamebook'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'gate', 'ending-key'), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(p.get('gate').orderMode, 'branching');
});

test('a level with order none drops itself and its descendants out', async () => {
  const p = await positionsOf(fixturePath('novel-3level'), { order: { default: 'linear', levels: { chapter: 'none' } } });
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'rooftop-scene', 'funeral'), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'book-one', 'book-two'), 'before');
});

test('positions are recomputed after nodes move', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await rename(join(root, 'tree/01-book-one'), join(root, 'tree/03-book-one'));
  const p = await positionsOf(root);
  assert.equal(compareNodes(p, 'funeral', 'rooftop-scene'), 'before');
});

test('unknown ids throw', async () => {

  assert.throws(() => compareNodes(p, 'ghost', 'funeral'), { code: 'NODE_NOT_FOUND' });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패 확인**

Run: `node --test test/core/position.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — `Cannot find module '../../core/store/position.js'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/store/position.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { orderModeFor } from '../work/config.js';

/**
 * 깊이와 상관없는 한 줄 순번. 가지 노드(계획)는 자식보다 앞에 온다.
 * front/back 역할, linear가 아닌 레벨, 그리고 그 자손은 앞뒤 판정에서 빠진다.
 */
export function computePositions(tree, config) {
  const positions = new Map();
  let ordinal = 0;
  const walk = (ids, prefix, parentOrdered) => {
    ids.forEach((id, index) => {
      const node = tree.nodes.get(id);
      const key = [...prefix, String(index + 1).padStart(4, '0')];
      const orderMode = orderModeFor(config, node.level);
      const ordered = parentOrdered && orderMode === 'linear' && node.role === 'body';
      positions.set(id, { ordinal: ordinal++, key: key.join('.'), orderMode, ordered });
      walk(node.children, key, ordered);
    });
  };
  walk(tree.rootIds, [], true);
  return positions;
}

export function compareNodes(positions, a, b) {
  const pa = positions.get(a);
  const pb = positions.get(b);
  if (!pa || !pb) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', !pa ? a : b);
  if (!pa.ordered || !pb.ordered) return 'not_applicable';
  if (pa.ordinal === pb.ordinal) return 'same';
  return pa.ordinal < pb.ordinal ? 'before' : 'after';
}
```

Run: `node --test test/core/blocks.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — module not found.

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/store/blocks.js`:
```js
const ANCHOR = /\{#([A-Za-z0-9][\w-]*)\}/g;
const REF = /\[\[block:([A-Za-z0-9][\w-]*)\]\]/g;

/** 본문 속 그림·표·수식·각주 앵커와 그것을 가리키는 참조. */
export function parseBlocks(body) {
  const anchors = [];
  const refs = [];
  String(body).split('\n').forEach((text, index) => {
    for (const match of text.matchAll(ANCHOR)) anchors.push({ id: match[1], line: index + 1 });
    for (const match of text.matchAll(REF)) refs.push({ target: match[1], line: index + 1 });
  });
  return { anchors, refs };
}

/** 앵커 id는 작품 전체에서 하나. 깨진 참조와 중복 앵커는 문제로 돌려준다(오류로 막지 않음). */
export function validateBlocks(tree) {
  const anchors = new Map();
  const problems = [];
  const parsed = [...tree.nodes.values()].map((node) => ({ node, ...parseBlocks(node.body) }));
  for (const { node, anchors: found } of parsed) {
    for (const anchor of found) {
      if (anchors.has(anchor.id)) problems.push({ code: 'BLOCK_DUPLICATE', nodeId: node.id, id: anchor.id, line: anchor.line });
      else anchors.set(anchor.id, { nodeId: node.id, line: anchor.line });
    }
  }
  const refs = [];
  for (const { node, refs: found } of parsed) {
    for (const ref of found) {
      const valid = anchors.has(ref.target);
      refs.push({ nodeId: node.id, target: ref.target, line: ref.line, valid });
      if (!valid) problems.push({ code: 'BLOCK_REF_BROKEN', nodeId: node.id, target: ref.target, line: ref.line });
    }
  }
  return { anchors, refs, problems };
}
```

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';

test('entities load with kind, name, aliases and a content rev', async () => {
  const { entities, errors } = await readEntities(fixturePath('novel-3level'));
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  assert.deepEqual([...entities.keys()], ['junho', 'mara', 'red-letter', 'rooftop']);
  const mara = entities.get('mara');
  assert.equal(mara.kind, 'character');
  assert.equal(mara.name, '마라');
  assert.deepEqual(mara.aliases, ['마라 언니']);
  assert.equal(mara.path, 'entities/character/mara.md');
  assert.match(mara.rev, /^sha256:/);
  assert.equal(entities.get('red-letter').kind, 'promise');
});

for (const name of FIXTURES) {
  test(`entity round trip of ${name} is byte-identical`, async () => {
    const { entities } = await readEntities(fixturePath(name));
    const target = await tempDir();
    for (const entity of entities.values()) await writeEntity(target, entity);
    for (const entity of entities.values()) {
      assert.equal(await readFile(join(target, entity.path), 'utf8'), await readFile(join(fixturePath(name), entity.path), 'utf8'));
    }
  });
}

test('kind folder must match the kind field and ids must be unique', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await writeFile(join(root, 'entities/location/mara.md'), '---\nid: mara\nkind: location\nname: 마라\n---\n');

  await writeFile(join(root, 'entities/item/x.md'), '---\nid: x\nkind: character\nname: X\n---\n');
  const codes = (await readEntities(root)).errors.map((e) => e.code).sort();
  assert.deepEqual(codes, ['DUPLICATE_ENTITY_ID', 'ENTITY_KIND_MISMATCH']);
});
```

```js
  await addSourceFile(root, 'new.txt', Buffer.from('fresh'));
  assert.equal(await readFile(join(root, 'sources/new.txt'), 'utf8'), 'fresh');
```

`core/store/entities.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { listEntries, writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';
import { sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { parseDoc, serializeDoc } from './frontmatter.js';

export const ENTITIES_DIR = 'entities';
const ID = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/;

/** @typedef {{ id: string, kind: string, name: string, aliases: string[], data: Record<string, any>, body: string, path: string, rev: string }} Entity */

/** entities/<kind>/<file>.md 를 읽는다. 폴더 이름과 kind 필드가 같아야 한다. */
export async function readEntities(root) {
  const found = [];
  const errors = [];
  for (const kindDir of await listEntries(join(root, ENTITIES_DIR))) {
    if (!kindDir.isDirectory()) continue;
    for (const file of await listEntries(join(root, ENTITIES_DIR, kindDir.name))) {
      if (!file.isFile() || !file.name.endsWith('.md')) continue;
      const path = `${ENTITIES_DIR}/${kindDir.name}/${file.name}`;
      const text = await readFile(join(root, path), 'utf8');
      const { data, body } = parseDoc(text);
      if (typeof data.id !== 'string' || !ID.test(data.id)) { errors.push({ code: 'ENTITY_ID_INVALID', path }); continue; }
      if (data.kind !== kindDir.name) { errors.push({ code: 'ENTITY_KIND_MISMATCH', path, id: data.id }); continue; }
      if (typeof data.name !== 'string' || !data.name) { errors.push({ code: 'ENTITY_NAME_MISSING', path, id: data.id }); continue; }
      const aliases = Array.isArray(data.aliases) ? data.aliases.filter((a) => typeof a === 'string' && a) : [];
      found.push({ id: data.id, kind: data.kind, name: data.name, aliases, data, body, path, rev: sha256(text) });
    }
  }
  const entities = new Map();
  for (const entity of found.sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0))) {
    if (entities.has(entity.id)) errors.push({ code: 'DUPLICATE_ENTITY_ID', id: entity.id, path: entity.path });
    else entities.set(entity.id, entity);
  }
  return { entities, errors };
}

export async function writeEntity(root, entity) {
  const path = entity.path ?? `${ENTITIES_DIR}/${entity.kind}/${entity.id}.md`;
  await writeFileAtomic(join(root, path), serializeDoc({ data: entity.data, body: entity.body ?? '' }));
}
```

`core/store/sources.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { listEntries, readTextOrNull, writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';
import { sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { parseYaml, stringifyYaml } from '../util/yaml.js';

export const SOURCES_DIR = 'sources';

/** 자료 설명서(yaml)를 읽고 원본 파일의 hash를 rev에 넣는다. 원본은 읽기만 한다. */
export async function readSources(root) {
  const sources = new Map();
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];
  for (const entry of await listEntries(join(root, SOURCES_DIR))) {
    if (!entry.isFile() || !entry.name.endsWith('.yaml')) continue;
    const path = `${SOURCES_DIR}/${entry.name}`;
    const text = await readFile(join(root, path), 'utf8');
    const meta = parseYaml(text);
    if (typeof meta.id !== 'string' || typeof meta.file !== 'string') { errors.push({ code: 'SOURCE_META_INVALID', path }); continue; }
    let original;
    try { original = await readFile(join(root, SOURCES_DIR, meta.file)); }
    catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; errors.push({ code: 'SOURCE_FILE_MISSING', id: meta.id, file: meta.file }); continue; }
    if (sources.has(meta.id)) { errors.push({ code: 'DUPLICATE_SOURCE_ID', id: meta.id }); continue; }
    sources.set(meta.id, {
      id: meta.id, title: meta.title ?? '', file: meta.file, date: meta.date ?? null,
      reliability: meta.reliability ?? null, spans: Array.isArray(meta.spans) ? meta.spans : [],
      meta, path, rev: sha256(`${text}\u0000${sha256(original)}`),
    });
  }
  return { sources, errors, warnings };
}

export async function writeSourceMeta(root, source) {
  await writeFileAtomic(join(root, source.path ?? `${SOURCES_DIR}/${source.id}.yaml`), stringifyYaml(source.meta));
}

/** 새 원본 파일만 넣을 수 있다. 같은 이름이 있으면 거부한다(원본 불변). */
export async function addSourceFile(root, name, bytes) {
  if (name.includes('/') || name.includes('\\') || name.startsWith('.')) throw new FolioError('SOURCE_NAME_INVALID', name);
  const path = join(root, SOURCES_DIR, name);
  if ((await readTextOrNull(path)) !== null) throw new FolioError('SOURCE_IMMUTABLE', `${name} already exists`);
  await mkdir(join(root, SOURCES_DIR), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(path, bytes, { flag: 'wx' });
}
```

`core/store/assets.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { listEntries, writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';
import { sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { parseYaml, stringifyYaml } from '../util/yaml.js';

export const ASSETS_DIR = 'assets';
const INLINE = /!\[\[asset:([A-Za-z0-9][\w-]*)\]\]/g;

export const assetRefsInBody = (body) => [...String(body).matchAll(INLINE)].map((match) => match[1]);

/** 그림 설명서(yaml). 이미지 파일이 없으면 경고만 한다(생성 전 상태가 정상일 수 있음). */
export async function readAssets(root) {
  const assets = new Map();
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];
  for (const entry of await listEntries(join(root, ASSETS_DIR))) {
    if (!entry.isFile() || !entry.name.endsWith('.yaml')) continue;
    const path = `${ASSETS_DIR}/${entry.name}`;
    const text = await readFile(join(root, path), 'utf8');
    const meta = parseYaml(text);
    if (typeof meta.id !== 'string') { errors.push({ code: 'ASSET_META_INVALID', path }); continue; }
    let fileHash = null;
    if (typeof meta.file === 'string') {
      try { fileHash = sha256(await readFile(join(root, ASSETS_DIR, meta.file))); }
      catch (error) { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; warnings.push({ code: 'ASSET_FILE_MISSING', id: meta.id, file: meta.file }); }
    }
    const brief = meta.brief && typeof meta.brief === 'object' ? meta.brief : {};
    if (assets.has(meta.id)) { errors.push({ code: 'DUPLICATE_ASSET_ID', id: meta.id }); continue; }
    assets.set(meta.id, {
      id: meta.id, file: meta.file ?? null, brief,
      entities: Array.isArray(brief.entities) ? brief.entities.filter((e) => typeof e === 'string') : [],
      meta, path, rev: sha256(`${text}\u0000${fileHash ?? ''}`),
    });
  }
  return { assets, errors, warnings };
}

export async function writeAssetMeta(root, asset) {
  await writeFileAtomic(join(root, asset.path ?? `${ASSETS_DIR}/${asset.id}.yaml`), stringifyYaml(asset.meta));
}
```

`core/store/work.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { loadWorkConfig } from '../work/config.js';
import { readTree } from './tree.js';
import { validateTreeLevels } from './tree-levels.js';
import { computePositions } from './position.js';
import { validateBlocks } from './blocks.js';
import { readEntities } from './entities.js';
import { readSources } from './sources.js';
import { readAssets } from './assets.js';

/** @typedef {Awaited<ReturnType<typeof openWork>>} Work */

/** 작품 폴더 전체를 읽어 한 모델로 묶는다. 파일이 정본이고 이 객체는 매번 새로 만든다. */
export async function openWork(root) {
  const { config, warnings: configWarnings } = await loadWorkConfig(root);
  const tree = await readTree(root);
  const levels = validateTreeLevels(tree, config);
  const entities = await readEntities(root);
  const sources = await readSources(root);
  const assets = await readAssets(root);
  const errors = [...tree.errors, ...levels.errors, ...entities.errors, ...sources.errors, ...assets.errors];
  if (errors.length) throw new FolioError('WORK_INVALID', errors.map((e) => e.code).join(', '), { errors });
  return {
    root,
    config,
    tree,
    positions: computePositions(tree, config),
    entities: entities.entities,
    sources: sources.sources,
    assets: assets.assets,
    blocks: validateBlocks(tree),
    warnings: [...configWarnings, ...levels.warnings, ...sources.warnings, ...assets.warnings],
  };
}
```

### Task 9: 기록장(추가만, 해시 체인) + 사건 + 약속 + 영수증 형식

**Files:**
- Create: `core/ledger/ledger.js`, `core/ledger/events.js`, `core/ledger/receipts.js`, `core/promise.js`
- Modify: `test/support/fixtures.js` (seedEvents 추가)
- Test: `test/core/ledger.test.js`, `test/core/events.test.js`, `test/core/receipts.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sha256`, `canonicalJson`, `readTextOrNull`, `Position`
- Produces:
  - `openLedger(root, name, { now? }): Readonly<{ name, path, append(entry), readAll(), verify() }>` — 레코드 `{ ...entry, seq, at, prev, hash }`, `hash = sha256(canonicalJson(hash를 뺀 레코드))`, 첫 `prev = 'genesis'`. 수정·삭제 API 없음. 체인이 깨지면 `readAll`이 `FolioError('LEDGER_TAMPERED', ..., { line })`.
  - 기록장 이름: `'receipts'`(type `receipt`/`commit`), `'events'`(type `event`/`retraction`)
  - `EventRecord = { type: 'event', eventId, receiptId, nodeId, entityId, kind, data?, quote?, seq }`
  - `activeEvents(records): EventRecord[]` — 어떤 레코드의 `supersedes: string[]`에 든 eventId는 제외
  - `eventsBefore({ events, positions, nodeId }): { applicable: boolean, events: EventRecord[] }` — 대상이 ordered가 아니면 `{applicable:false, events:[]}`; 읽는 순서·seq 순 정렬
  - `PROMISE_KIND = 'promise'`, `PROMISE_STAGES = ['planted','advanced','misdirected','resolved','closed','parked']`, `promiseStageOf(event): string|null`, `foldPromise(eventsInOrder): { stage, history, problems: {code, severity, nodeId, eventId}[] }` — `PROMISE_RESOLVED_BEFORE_PLANT`(hard), `PROMISE_MOVED_BEFORE_PLANT`(hard), `PROMISE_RESOLVED_AFTER_CLOSE`(warning)
  - `Finding = { checkId, severity: 'hard'|'block'|'warning'|'advisory', code, message, quote?, nodeId? }`, `isBlocking(finding): boolean` (hard 또는 block)
  - `Receipt` (아래 typedef), `STEP_STATUSES`, `validateReceipt(receipt): {code, ...}[]`, `lastCommits(records): Map<nodeId, commitRecord>`, `receiptsById(records): Map`
  - 테스트 지원: `seedEvents(root, fixtureName): Promise<EventRecord[]>`

`test/core/ledger.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { tempDir } from '../support/fixtures.js';

const clock = () => { let n = 0; return () => `2026-09-26T00:00:0${n++}.000Z`; };

test('append chains records and readAll returns them in order', async () => {
  const ledger = openLedger(await tempDir(), 'receipts', { now: clock() });
  const a = await ledger.append({ type: 'receipt', receiptId: 'r1' });
  const b = await ledger.append({ type: 'commit', receiptId: 'r1' });
  assert.equal(a.seq, 1);
  assert.equal(a.prev, 'genesis');
  assert.equal(b.prev, a.hash);
  assert.deepEqual((await ledger.readAll()).map((r) => r.type), ['receipt', 'commit']);
  assert.deepEqual(await ledger.verify(), { ok: true });
});

test('the ledger API offers no way to edit or delete', async () => {
  const ledger = openLedger(await tempDir(), 'events');
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(ledger).sort(), ['append', 'name', 'path', 'readAll', 'verify']);
  assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(ledger));
});

test('editing, deleting or reordering lines is detected', async () => {
  for (const tamper of [
    (lines) => [lines[0].replace('"r1"', '"rX"'), lines[1]],
    (lines) => [lines[1]],
    (lines) => [lines[1], lines[0]],
  ]) {
    const ledger = openLedger(await tempDir(), 'receipts', { now: clock() });
    await ledger.append({ type: 'receipt', receiptId: 'r1' });
    await ledger.append({ type: 'receipt', receiptId: 'r2' });
    const lines = (await readFile(ledger.path, 'utf8')).trim().split('\n');
    await writeFile(ledger.path, `${tamper(lines).join('\n')}\n`);
    await assert.rejects(ledger.readAll(), { code: 'LEDGER_TAMPERED' });
    assert.equal((await ledger.verify()).ok, false);
    await assert.rejects(ledger.append({ type: 'receipt', receiptId: 'r3' }), { code: 'LEDGER_TAMPERED' });
  }
});

test('entries need a type and ledger names are plain', async () => {
  const root = await tempDir();
  await assert.rejects(openLedger(root, 'events').append({}), { code: 'LEDGER_ENTRY_INVALID' });
  assert.throws(() => openLedger(root, '../x'), { code: 'LEDGER_NAME_INVALID' });
});
```

`test/core/events.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents, eventsBefore } from '../../core/ledger/events.js';
import { foldPromise } from '../../core/promise.js';
import { copyFixture, seedEvents } from '../support/fixtures.js';

test('eventsBefore collects earlier events across branches and levels', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await seedEvents(root, 'novel-3level');
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const events = activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll());
  const before = eventsBefore({ events, positions: work.positions, nodeId: 'funeral' });
  assert.equal(before.applicable, true);
  assert.deepEqual(before.events.map((e) => `${e.nodeId}:${e.kind}`), [
    'rooftop-scene:promise.resolved', 'post-office:promise.planted', 'last-night:state',
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(eventsBefore({ events, positions: work.positions, nodeId: 'rooftop-scene' }).events, []);
});

test('eventsBefore is not applicable outside a linear order', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('gamebook');
  await seedEvents(root, 'gamebook');
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const events = activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll());
  assert.deepEqual(eventsBefore({ events, positions: work.positions, nodeId: 'ending-trap' }), { applicable: false, events: [] });
});

test('superseded events drop out', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const seeded = await seedEvents(root, 'novel-3level');
  const ledger = openLedger(root, 'events');
  await ledger.append({ type: 'retraction', nodeId: 'last-night', receiptId: 'r9', supersedes: [seeded[2].eventId] });
  assert.deepEqual(activeEvents(await ledger.readAll()).map((e) => e.eventId), [seeded[0].eventId, seeded[1].eventId]);
});

test('foldPromise flags a payoff before the plant', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await seedEvents(root, 'novel-3level');
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const events = activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll());
  const ordered = events.filter((e) => e.entityId === 'red-letter')
    .sort((a, b) => work.positions.get(a.nodeId).ordinal - work.positions.get(b.nodeId).ordinal);
  const folded = foldPromise(ordered);
  assert.deepEqual(folded.problems.map((p) => [p.code, p.severity, p.nodeId]), [['PROMISE_RESOLVED_BEFORE_PLANT', 'hard', 'rooftop-scene']]);
  assert.equal(folded.stage, 'planted');
});

test('foldPromise accepts a clean arc and warns on payoff after close', () => {
  const e = (kind, nodeId) => ({ kind: `promise.${kind}`, nodeId, eventId: `${nodeId}-${kind}` });
  assert.deepEqual(foldPromise([e('planted', 'a'), e('advanced', 'b'), e('resolved', 'c')]).problems, []);
  const late = foldPromise([e('planted', 'a'), e('resolved', 'b'), e('closed', 'c'), e('resolved', 'd')]);
  assert.deepEqual(late.problems.map((p) => [p.code, p.severity]), [['PROMISE_RESOLVED_AFTER_CLOSE', 'warning']]);
});
```

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { sha256 } from '../../core/util/hash.js';
import { validateReceipt, lastCommits, isBlocking } from '../../core/ledger/receipts.js';

const draftHash = sha256('본문');
const validReceipt = () => ({
  receiptVersion: 1, receiptId: 'rcpt-1', runId: 'run-1', nodeId: 'n1', variant: 'main', level: 'scene',
  draftHash, pipelineDigest: sha256('p'), hard: 0, attempts: 0, refs: [], advisories: [], overridden: [], events: [],
  steps: [
    { id: 'draft', kind: 'generate', status: 'passed' },
    { id: 'facts', kind: 'check', status: 'passed', draftHash, checks: [] },
    { id: 'critic', kind: 'check', status: 'skipped', reason: '초고 속도 우선', source: 'work.yaml#overrides.critic' },
    { id: 'approve', kind: 'gate', status: 'auto', source: 'work.yaml#overrides.approve' },
  ],
});

test('a well-formed receipt is valid', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(validateReceipt(validReceipt()), []);
});

const broken = {
  VARIANT_UNSUPPORTED: (r) => { r.variant = 'en'; },
  DRAFT_HASH_INVALID: (r) => { r.draftHash = 'abc'; },
  STEP_SKIP_REASON_MISSING: (r) => { delete r.steps[2].reason; },
  CHECK_HASH_MISMATCH: (r) => { r.steps[1].draftHash = sha256('다른 본문'); },
  STEP_NOT_RUN: (r) => { r.steps[1].status = 'not_run'; },
  SELF_REVIEW_MISLABELED: (r) => { r.steps[1].checks = [{ checkId: 'c', status: 'passed', findings: [], reviewer: { sameHost: true, independent: true } }]; },
  RECEIPT_FIELD_MISSING: (r) => { delete r.nodeId; },
};
for (const [code, mutate] of Object.entries(broken)) {
  test(`validateReceipt reports ${code}`, () => {
    const receipt = validReceipt();
    mutate(receipt);
    assert.ok(validateReceipt(receipt).some((e) => e.code === code), JSON.stringify(validateReceipt(receipt)));
  });
}

test('lastCommits keeps the latest commit per node', () => {
  const commits = lastCommits([
    { type: 'commit', nodeId: 'a', receiptId: 'r1', seq: 1 },
    { type: 'receipt', nodeId: 'a', receiptId: 'r2', seq: 2 },
    { type: 'commit', nodeId: 'a', receiptId: 'r2', seq: 3 },
  ]);
  assert.equal(commits.get('a').receiptId, 'r2');
});

test('only hard and block findings block', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(['hard', 'block', 'warning', 'advisory'].map((severity) => isBlocking({ severity })), [true, true, false, false]);
});
```

```js
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { parseYaml } from '../../core/util/yaml.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';

/** seeds/<name>.events.yaml 을 events 기록장에 넣는다(테스트 전용 경로). */
export async function seedEvents(root, name) {
  const seeds = parseYaml(await readFile(join(import.meta.dirname, '..', 'fixtures', 'seeds', `${name}.events.yaml`), 'utf8'));
  const ledger = openLedger(root, 'events', { now: () => '2026-09-26T00:00:00.000Z' });
  const records = [];
  for (const [index, seed] of seeds.entries()) {
    records.push(await ledger.append({ type: 'event', eventId: `seed-${index + 1}`, receiptId: 'seed', ...seed }));
  }
  return records;
}
```

`core/ledger/ledger.js`:
```js
import { appendFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { readTextOrNull } from '../util/fs.js';
import { canonicalJson, sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';

const GENESIS = 'genesis';
const NAME = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/;

/**
 * 추가만 되는 JSON Lines 기록장. 각 줄은 앞 줄의 hash를 품는다.
 * 고치거나 지우는 함수는 없다. 파일을 손으로 고치면 readAll/append가 거부한다.
 */
export function openLedger(root, name, { now = () => new Date().toISOString() } = {}) {
  if (!NAME.test(name)) throw new FolioError('LEDGER_NAME_INVALID', name);
  const path = join(root, '.folio', 'ledger', `${name}.jsonl`);

  async function readAll() {
    const text = await readTextOrNull(path);
    if (!text) return [];
    const records = [];
    let prev = GENESIS;
    const lines = text.split('\n').filter((line) => line.length > 0);
    for (const [index, line] of lines.entries()) {
      let record;
      try { record = JSON.parse(line); }
      catch { throw new FolioError('LEDGER_TAMPERED', `${name}:${index + 1} is not JSON`, { line: index + 1 }); }
      const { hash, ...rest } = record;
      if (rest.seq !== index + 1 || rest.prev !== prev || hash !== sha256(canonicalJson(rest))) {
        throw new FolioError('LEDGER_TAMPERED', `${name}:${index + 1} breaks the hash chain`, { line: index + 1 });
      }
      records.push(record);
      prev = hash;
    }
    return records;
  }

  async function append(entry) {
    if (!entry || typeof entry.type !== 'string') throw new FolioError('LEDGER_ENTRY_INVALID', 'entry needs a type');
    const last = (await readAll()).at(-1);
    const { seq: _s, prev: _p, hash: _h, ...payload } = entry;
    const rest = { ...payload, seq: (last?.seq ?? 0) + 1, at: entry.at ?? now(), prev: last?.hash ?? GENESIS };
    const record = { ...rest, hash: sha256(canonicalJson(rest)) };
    await mkdir(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
    await appendFile(path, `${JSON.stringify(record)}\n`, { flag: 'a' });
    return record;
  }

  async function verify() {
    try { await readAll(); return { ok: true }; }
    catch (error) {
      if (error.code === 'LEDGER_TAMPERED') return { ok: false, line: error.details.line, error: error.message };
      throw error;
    }
  }

  return Object.freeze({ name, path, append, readAll, verify });
}
```

`core/ledger/events.js`:
```js
/** supersedes 로 가려진 사건을 뺀 현재 유효한 사건. 기록 자체는 지우지 않는다. */
export function activeEvents(records) {
  const superseded = new Set(records.flatMap((record) => record.supersedes ?? []));
  return records.filter((record) => record.type === 'event' && !superseded.has(record.eventId));
}

/** 대상 노드보다 읽는 순서상 앞선 사건. 가지·층과 무관하다. */
export function eventsBefore({ events, positions, nodeId }) {
  const target = positions.get(nodeId);
  if (!target?.ordered) return { applicable: false, events: [] };
  const earlier = events.filter((event) => {
    const position = positions.get(event.nodeId);
    return position?.ordered && position.ordinal < target.ordinal;
  });
  earlier.sort((a, b) => positions.get(a.nodeId).ordinal - positions.get(b.nodeId).ordinal || a.seq - b.seq);
  return { applicable: true, events: earlier };
}
```

`core/promise.js`:
```js
/** 약속(떡밥, "뒤에서 설명", 학습 목표)은 엔티티이고 단계 변화는 사건으로 쌓인다. */
export const PROMISE_KIND = 'promise';
export const PROMISE_STAGES = ['planted', 'advanced', 'misdirected', 'resolved', 'closed', 'parked'];
const PREFIX = 'promise.';

export function promiseStageOf(event) {
  if (typeof event?.kind !== 'string' || !event.kind.startsWith(PREFIX)) return null;
  const stage = event.kind.slice(PREFIX.length);
  return PROMISE_STAGES.includes(stage) ? stage : null;
}

/** 읽는 순서로 정렬된 한 약속의 사건을 접는다. */
export function foldPromise(eventsInOrder) {
  let stage = null;
  const history = [];
  const problems = [];
  for (const event of eventsInOrder) {
    const next = promiseStageOf(event);
    if (!next) continue;
    const at = { nodeId: event.nodeId, eventId: event.eventId };
    if (stage === null && next !== 'planted') {
      problems.push({ code: next === 'resolved' ? 'PROMISE_RESOLVED_BEFORE_PLANT' : 'PROMISE_MOVED_BEFORE_PLANT', severity: 'hard', ...at });
    } else if (stage === 'closed' && next === 'resolved') {
      problems.push({ code: 'PROMISE_RESOLVED_AFTER_CLOSE', severity: 'warning', ...at });
    }
    if (stage !== null || next === 'planted') stage = next;
    history.push({ stage: next, ...at });
  }
  return { stage, history, problems };
}
```

`core/ledger/receipts.js`:
```js
export const RECEIPT_VERSION = 1;
export const STEP_STATUSES = ['passed', 'failed', 'skipped', 'auto', 'approved', 'not_needed', 'not_applicable', 'overridden', 'error'];
const HASH = /^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
const CHECKED = new Set(['passed', 'failed', 'not_applicable']);

/** @typedef {{ checkId: string, severity: 'hard'|'block'|'warning'|'advisory', code: string, message: string, quote?: string, nodeId?: string }} Finding */
/** @typedef {{ checkId: string, status: string, findings: Finding[], reviewer?: { sameHost: boolean, independent: boolean }, unsupported?: Finding[] }} CheckResult */
/** @typedef {{ id: string, kind: string, status: string, reason?: string, source?: string, draftHash?: string|null, checks?: CheckResult[] }} ReceiptStep */
/**
 * @typedef {{ receiptVersion: number, receiptId: string, runId: string, nodeId: string, variant: string, level: string,
 *   draftHash: string, pipelineDigest: string, steps: ReceiptStep[], refs: {kind: string, id: string, rev: string|null}[],
 *   hard: number, advisories: Finding[], overridden: Finding[], attempts: number, events: object[] }} Receipt
 */

export const isBlocking = (finding) => finding?.severity === 'hard' || finding?.severity === 'block';

/** 잠금 규칙 중 영수증 형식에 걸린 것을 검사한다. 빈 배열이면 유효. */
export function validateReceipt(receipt) {
  if (!receipt || typeof receipt !== 'object') return [{ code: 'RECEIPT_MISSING' }];
  const errors = [];
  const push = (code, extra = {}) => errors.push({ code, ...extra });
  for (const field of ['receiptId', 'runId', 'nodeId', 'level', 'pipelineDigest']) {
    if (typeof receipt[field] !== 'string' || !receipt[field]) push('RECEIPT_FIELD_MISSING', { field });
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(receipt.hard) || receipt.hard < 0) push('RECEIPT_FIELD_MISSING', { field: 'hard' });
  if (!Array.isArray(receipt.refs)) push('RECEIPT_FIELD_MISSING', { field: 'refs' });
  if (receipt.variant !== 'main') push('VARIANT_UNSUPPORTED', { variant: receipt.variant });
  if (!HASH.test(receipt.draftHash ?? '')) push('DRAFT_HASH_INVALID');
  if (!Array.isArray(receipt.steps) || receipt.steps.length === 0) push('RECEIPT_STEPS_MISSING');
  for (const step of receipt.steps ?? []) {
    if (step.status === 'not_run') push('STEP_NOT_RUN', { stepId: step.id });
    else if (!STEP_STATUSES.includes(step.status)) push('STEP_STATUS_INVALID', { stepId: step.id });
    if (step.status === 'skipped' && (typeof step.reason !== 'string' || typeof step.source !== 'string')) {
      push('STEP_SKIP_REASON_MISSING', { stepId: step.id });
    }
    if (step.kind === 'check' && CHECKED.has(step.status) && step.draftHash !== receipt.draftHash) {
      push('CHECK_HASH_MISMATCH', { stepId: step.id });
    }
    for (const check of step.checks ?? []) {
      if (check.reviewer?.independent === true) push('SELF_REVIEW_MISLABELED', { stepId: step.id, checkId: check.checkId });
    }
  }
  return errors;
}

export function lastCommits(records) {
  const commits = new Map();
  for (const record of records) if (record.type === 'commit') commits.set(record.nodeId, record);
  return commits;
}

export function receiptsById(records) {
  return new Map(records.filter((record) => record.type === 'receipt').map((record) => [record.receiptId, record]));
}
```

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { rebuildIndex, indexPath } from '../../core/index/rebuild.js';
import { openIndex, dumpIndex, nodesInReadingOrder, usagesOf, eventsFor, compareInIndex } from '../../core/index/query.js';
import { scanMentions } from '../../core/index/scan.js';
import { FIXTURES, copyFixture, seedEvents } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function snapshot(root) {
  const db = openIndex(root);
  try {
    const entityIds = dumpIndex(db).entities.map((e) => e.id);
    return {
      dump: dumpIndex(db),
      order: nodesInReadingOrder(db),
      usages: Object.fromEntries(entityIds.map((id) => [id, usagesOf(db, id)])),
      events: Object.fromEntries(entityIds.map((id) => [id, eventsFor(db, id)])),
    };
  } finally { db.close(); }
}

test('scanMentions prefers longer aliases and never double counts', () => {
  const entities = [{ id: 'mara', name: '마라', aliases: ['마라 언니'] }, { id: 'junho', name: '준호', aliases: [] }];
  const counts = scanMentions('마라 언니와 준호, 그리고 마라.', entities);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.fromEntries(counts), { mara: 2, junho: 1 });
});

test('the index answers reading order, usages, events and comparisons', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await seedEvents(root, 'novel-3level');
  const { counts } = await rebuildIndex(root);
  assert.equal(counts.nodes, 11);
  assert.equal(counts.events, 3);
  const db = openIndex(root);
  try {
    assert.deepEqual(nodesInReadingOrder(db).slice(0, 3), ['book-one', 'the-fall', 'rooftop-scene']);
    const maraSurfaces = usagesOf(db, 'mara').map((u) => `${u.surfaceKind}:${u.surfaceId}:${u.origin}`);
    for (const expected of ['node:book-one:scan', 'node:rooftop-scene:declared', 'node:stairwell:scan', 'node:funeral:scan', 'asset:img-rooftop:declared']) {
      assert.ok(maraSurfaces.includes(expected), `${expected} missing from ${maraSurfaces}`);
    }
    assert.ok(!maraSurfaces.includes('node:market:scan'));
    assert.deepEqual(eventsFor(db, 'red-letter').map((e) => e.kind), ['promise.resolved', 'promise.planted']);
    assert.equal(compareInIndex(db, 'rooftop-scene', 'funeral'), 'before');
  } finally { db.close(); }
});

test('links and block references are projected', async () => {
  const game = await copyFixture('gamebook');
  await rebuildIndex(game);
  const book = await copyFixture('knowledge-book');
  await rebuildIndex(book);
  const g = openIndex(game);
  const k = openIndex(book);
  try {
    assert.equal(dumpIndex(g).links.length, 4);
    assert.ok(dumpIndex(g).links.every((l) => l.valid === 1));
    assert.deepEqual(dumpIndex(k).block_refs.filter((r) => r.valid === 0).map((r) => r.target), ['fig-9-9']);
    assert.equal(compareInIndex(k, 'preface', 'membranes'), 'not_applicable');
  } finally { g.close(); k.close(); }
});

for (const name of FIXTURES) {
  test(`deleting index.db and rebuilding gives identical results (${name})`, async () => {
    const root = await copyFixture(name);
    await seedEvents(root, name);
    await rebuildIndex(root);
    const first = await snapshot(root);
    await rm(indexPath(root));
    await rebuildIndex(root);
    assert.deepEqual(await snapshot(root), first);
  });
}

test('a corrupted index.db is simply replaced', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('serial-2level');
  await rebuildIndex(root);
  await writeFile(indexPath(root), 'not a database');
  await rebuildIndex(root);
  const db = openIndex(root);
  try { assert.equal(nodesInReadingOrder(db).length, 8); } finally { db.close(); }
});
```

`core/index/schema.js`:
```js
/** index.db 는 파일과 기록장의 투영이다. 정본이 아니며 언제든 지우고 다시 만든다. */
export const TABLES = ['nodes', 'entities', 'aliases', 'links', 'blocks', 'block_refs', 'assets', 'node_assets', 'sources', 'occurrences', 'events', 'receipts', 'receipt_refs', 'commits'];

export const SCHEMA_SQL = `
CREATE TABLE nodes(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, level TEXT NOT NULL, role TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL, parent_id TEXT, sibling_index INTEGER NOT NULL, ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL, pos_key TEXT NOT NULL,
  order_mode TEXT NOT NULL, ordered INTEGER NOT NULL, body_hash TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE entities(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, kind TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, path TEXT NOT NULL, rev TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE aliases(entity_id TEXT NOT NULL, alias TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE links(from_id TEXT NOT NULL, to_id TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, label TEXT NOT NULL, valid INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE blocks(id TEXT NOT NULL, node_id TEXT NOT NULL, variant TEXT NOT NULL, line INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE block_refs(node_id TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT NOT NULL, line INTEGER NOT NULL, valid INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE assets(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, rev TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE node_assets(node_id TEXT NOT NULL, asset_id TEXT NOT NULL, placement TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE sources(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, rev TEXT NOT NULL, reliability TEXT);
CREATE TABLE occurrences(entity_id TEXT NOT NULL, surface_kind TEXT NOT NULL, surface_id TEXT NOT NULL, origin TEXT NOT NULL, count INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE events(event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, seq INTEGER NOT NULL, node_id TEXT NOT NULL, entity_id TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, data TEXT NOT NULL, active INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE receipts(receipt_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, node_id TEXT NOT NULL, variant TEXT NOT NULL, draft_hash TEXT NOT NULL, seq INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE receipt_refs(receipt_id TEXT NOT NULL, ref_kind TEXT NOT NULL, ref_id TEXT NOT NULL, rev TEXT);
CREATE TABLE commits(node_id TEXT NOT NULL, variant TEXT NOT NULL, receipt_id TEXT NOT NULL, committed_hash TEXT NOT NULL, seq INTEGER NOT NULL);
`;
```

`core/index/scan.js`:
```js
/**
 * 이름·별칭 정확 일치로 등장 후보를 센다. 긴 말부터 가리고 세서 "마라 언니"가 "마라"로 두 번 세지지 않는다.
 * M4 에서 호스트 제출·인용문 대조 방식이 이것을 보강한다.
 */
export function scanMentions(text, entities) {
  const terms = [];
  for (const entity of entities) {
    for (const term of [entity.name, ...(entity.aliases ?? [])]) if (term) terms.push({ term, id: entity.id });
  }
  terms.sort((a, b) => b.term.length - a.term.length || (a.term < b.term ? -1 : a.term > b.term ? 1 : 0));
  let masked = String(text);
  const counts = new Map();
  for (const { term, id } of terms) {
    let at = masked.indexOf(term);
    while (at !== -1) {
      counts.set(id, (counts.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
      masked = masked.slice(0, at) + '\u0000'.repeat(term.length) + masked.slice(at + term.length);
      at = masked.indexOf(term, at + term.length);
    }
  }
  return counts;
}
```

`core/index/rebuild.js`:
```js
import { mkdir, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { openWork } from '../store/work.js';
import { preorder } from '../store/tree.js';
import { assetRefsInBody } from '../store/assets.js';
import { openLedger } from '../ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../ledger/events.js';
import { sha256, canonicalJson } from '../util/hash.js';
import { scanMentions } from './scan.js';
import { SCHEMA_SQL, TABLES } from './schema.js';

export const indexPath = (root) => join(root, '.folio', 'index.db');

function insertOccurrences(insert, text, entities, surfaceKind, surfaceId, skipId = null) {
  for (const [entityId, count] of scanMentions(text, entities.values())) {
    if (entityId !== skipId) insert.run(entityId, surfaceKind, surfaceId, 'scan', count);
  }
}

/** 작품 폴더와 기록장에서 index.db 를 처음부터 만든다. */
export async function rebuildIndex(root) {
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const receiptRecords = await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll();
  const eventRecords = await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll();
  const path = indexPath(root);
  await mkdir(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
  for (const suffix of ['', '-wal', '-shm', '-journal']) await rm(`${path}${suffix}`, { force: true });
  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
  try {
    db.exec(SCHEMA_SQL);
    db.exec('BEGIN');
    const st = (sql) => db.prepare(sql);
    const nodeRow = st('INSERT INTO nodes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)');
    const linkRow = st('INSERT INTO links VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    const nodeAsset = st('INSERT INTO node_assets VALUES (?,?,?)');
    const occurrence = st('INSERT INTO occurrences VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    for (const id of preorder(work.tree)) {
      const node = work.tree.nodes.get(id);
      const pos = work.positions.get(id);
      nodeRow.run(id, node.path, node.kind, node.level, node.role, node.status, node.parentId, node.siblingIndex,
        pos.ordinal, pos.key, pos.orderMode, pos.ordered ? 1 : 0, sha256(node.body));
      for (const link of node.links) linkRow.run(id, link.to, link.kind, link.label, work.tree.nodes.has(link.to) ? 1 : 0);
      for (const assetId of node.assets) nodeAsset.run(id, assetId, 'attached');
      for (const assetId of assetRefsInBody(node.body)) nodeAsset.run(id, assetId, 'inline');
      for (const entityId of node.entities) occurrence.run(entityId, 'node', id, 'declared', 1);
      insertOccurrences(occurrence, node.body, work.entities, 'node', id);
    }
    const entityRow = st('INSERT INTO entities VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    const aliasRow = st('INSERT INTO aliases VALUES (?,?)');
    for (const entity of work.entities.values()) {
      entityRow.run(entity.id, entity.kind, entity.name, entity.path, entity.rev);
      for (const alias of entity.aliases) aliasRow.run(entity.id, alias);
      insertOccurrences(occurrence, entity.body, work.entities, 'entity', entity.id, entity.id);
    }
    const blockRow = st('INSERT INTO blocks VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
    for (const [id, anchor] of work.blocks.anchors) blockRow.run(id, anchor.nodeId, 'main', anchor.line);
    const refRow = st('INSERT INTO block_refs VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
    for (const ref of work.blocks.refs) refRow.run(ref.nodeId, ref.target, ref.line, ref.valid ? 1 : 0);
    const assetRow = st('INSERT INTO assets VALUES (?,?,?)');
    for (const asset of work.assets.values()) {
      assetRow.run(asset.id, asset.path, asset.rev);
      for (const entityId of asset.entities) occurrence.run(entityId, 'asset', asset.id, 'declared', 1);
      insertOccurrences(occurrence, canonicalJson(asset.brief), work.entities, 'asset', asset.id);
    }
    const sourceRow = st('INSERT INTO sources VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
    for (const source of work.sources.values()) {
      sourceRow.run(source.id, source.path, source.rev, source.reliability === null ? null : String(source.reliability));
      insertOccurrences(occurrence, [source.title, ...source.spans.map((s) => s.quote ?? '')].join('\n'), work.entities, 'source', source.id);
    }
    const active = new Set(activeEvents(eventRecords).map((e) => e.eventId));
    const eventRow = st('INSERT INTO events VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)');
    for (const e of eventRecords.filter((r) => r.type === 'event')) {
      eventRow.run(e.eventId, e.seq, e.nodeId, e.entityId, e.kind, canonicalJson(e.data ?? null), active.has(e.eventId) ? 1 : 0);
    }
    const receiptRow = st('INSERT INTO receipts VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    const receiptRef = st('INSERT INTO receipt_refs VALUES (?,?,?,?)');
    const commitRow = st('INSERT INTO commits VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    for (const r of receiptRecords) {
      if (r.type === 'receipt') {
        receiptRow.run(r.receiptId, r.nodeId, r.variant, r.draftHash, r.seq);
        for (const ref of r.refs ?? []) receiptRef.run(r.receiptId, ref.kind, ref.id, ref.rev ?? null);
      } else if (r.type === 'commit') {
        commitRow.run(r.nodeId, r.variant ?? 'main', r.receiptId, r.committedHash, r.seq);
      }
    }
    db.exec('COMMIT');
    const counts = Object.fromEntries(TABLES.map((t) => [t, Number(db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM ${t}`).get().n)]));
    return { path, counts };
  } catch (error) {
    try { db.exec('ROLLBACK'); } catch { /* no open transaction */ }
    throw error;
  } finally {
    db.close();
  }
}
```

`core/index/query.js`:
```js
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { indexPath } from './rebuild.js';
import { TABLES } from './schema.js';

const plain = (rows) => rows.map((row) => ({ ...row }));

export const openIndex = (root) => new DatabaseSync(indexPath(root), { readOnly: true });

/** 모든 표를 모든 열 기준으로 정렬해 덤프한다. 재생성 동일성 비교용. */
export function dumpIndex(db) {
  const out = {};
  for (const table of TABLES) {
    const columns = db.prepare(`PRAGMA table_info(${table})`).all().map((c) => c.name);
    out[table] = plain(db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${table} ORDER BY ${columns.join(', ')}`).all());
  }
  return out;
}

export const nodesInReadingOrder = (db) => db.prepare('SELECT id FROM nodes ORDER BY ordinal').all().map((r) => r.id);

export function usagesOf(db, entityId) {
  return plain(db.prepare(`
    SELECT o.surface_kind AS surfaceKind, o.surface_id AS surfaceId, o.origin, o.count
    FROM occurrences o LEFT JOIN nodes n ON o.surface_kind = 'node' AND n.id = o.surface_id
    WHERE o.entity_id = ?
    ORDER BY o.surface_kind, COALESCE(n.ordinal, -1), o.surface_id, o.origin`).all(entityId));
}

export function eventsFor(db, entityId) {
  return plain(db.prepare(`
    SELECT e.event_id AS eventId, e.node_id AS nodeId, e.kind
    FROM events e JOIN nodes n ON n.id = e.node_id
    WHERE e.entity_id = ? AND e.active = 1
    ORDER BY n.ordinal, e.seq`).all(entityId));
}

export function compareInIndex(db, a, b) {
  const get = db.prepare('SELECT ordinal, ordered FROM nodes WHERE id = ?');
  const pa = get.get(a);
  const pb = get.get(b);
  if (!pa || !pb) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', !pa ? a : b);
  if (!pa.ordered || !pb.ordered) return 'not_applicable';
  if (pa.ordinal === pb.ordinal) return 'same';
  return pa.ordinal < pb.ordinal ? 'before' : 'after';
}
```
