### Task 11: 손수정 감지 + M1 완료 기준 테스트

**Files:**
- Create: `core/store/drift.js`
- Test: `test/core/drift.test.js`, `test/acceptance/m1.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `lastCommits`, `sha256`, `Work`
- Produces: `detectDrift(work, receiptRecords): { nodeId, code: 'HAND_EDITED'|'NODE_MISSING', path?, expected?, actual? }[]` — 본문(body) hash만 비교한다. frontmatter(제목·상태) 변경은 손수정이 아니다.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/core/drift.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { detectDrift } from '../../core/store/drift.js';
import { sha256 } from '../../core/util/hash.js';
import { copyFixture } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function commitAsIs(root, nodeId) {
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const ledger = openLedger(root, 'receipts');
  await ledger.append({ type: 'commit', receiptId: `seed-${nodeId}`, nodeId, variant: 'main', committedHash: sha256(work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId).body) });
  return ledger;
}

test('an untouched committed node shows no drift', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const ledger = await commitAsIs(root, 'rooftop-scene');
  assert.deepEqual(detectDrift(await openWork(root), await ledger.readAll()), []);
});

test('editing the body by hand is detected; editing the title is not', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const ledger = await commitAsIs(root, 'rooftop-scene');
  const path = join(root, 'tree/01-book-one/01-the-fall/01-rooftop.md');
  await writeFile(path, (await readFile(path, 'utf8')).replace('title: 옥상', 'title: 옥상 위'));
  assert.deepEqual(detectDrift(await openWork(root), await ledger.readAll()), []);
  await writeFile(path, (await readFile(path, 'utf8')).replace('난간에', '벽에'));
  const drift = detectDrift(await openWork(root), await ledger.readAll());
  assert.deepEqual(drift.map((d) => [d.nodeId, d.code]), [['rooftop-scene', 'HAND_EDITED']]);
});

test('a committed node whose file disappeared is reported', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const ledger = await commitAsIs(root, 'market');
  await rm(join(root, 'tree/02-book-two/01-after/02-market.md'));
  assert.deepEqual(detectDrift(await openWork(root), await ledger.readAll()).map((d) => d.code), ['NODE_MISSING']);
});
```

`test/acceptance/m1.test.js` (완료 기준을 한 파일에 모은다):
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { readTree, writeTree } from '../../core/store/tree.js';
import { readEntities, writeEntity } from '../../core/store/entities.js';
import { compareNodes } from '../../core/store/position.js';
import { rebuildIndex, indexPath } from '../../core/index/rebuild.js';
import { openIndex, dumpIndex } from '../../core/index/query.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { detectDrift } from '../../core/store/drift.js';
import { sha256 } from '../../core/util/hash.js';
import { FIXTURES, fixturePath, copyFixture, seedEvents, tempDir } from '../support/fixtures.js';

test('M1-1: all four fixtures round-trip through read and write', async () => {
  for (const name of FIXTURES) {
    const tree = await readTree(fixturePath(name));
    const { entities } = await readEntities(fixturePath(name));
    const target = await tempDir();
    await writeTree(target, tree);
    for (const entity of entities.values()) await writeEntity(target, entity);
    for (const node of tree.nodes.values()) {
      assert.equal(await readFile(join(target, 'tree', node.path), 'utf8'), await readFile(join(fixturePath(name), 'tree', node.path), 'utf8'), `${name}:${node.path}`);
    }
    assert.deepEqual([...(await readTree(target)).nodes.values()], [...tree.nodes.values()], name);
  }
});

test('M1-2: nodes on different branches and levels compare correctly', async () => {
  const novel = await openWork(fixturePath('novel-3level'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(novel.positions, 'last-night', 'funeral'), 'before');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(novel.positions, 'book-two', 'stairwell'), 'after');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(novel.positions, 'the-letter', 'rooftop-scene'), 'after');
  const book = await openWork(fixturePath('knowledge-book'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(book.positions, 'membranes', 'tissues'), 'before');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(book.positions, 'glossary', 'tissues'), 'not_applicable');
  const game = await openWork(fixturePath('gamebook'));
  assert.equal(compareNodes(game.positions, 'gate', 'ending-key'), 'not_applicable');
});

test('M1-3: index.db rebuilt from scratch answers identically', async () => {
  for (const name of FIXTURES) {
    const root = await copyFixture(name);
    await seedEvents(root, name);
    await rebuildIndex(root);
    const read = () => { const db = openIndex(root); try { return dumpIndex(db); } finally { db.close(); } };
    const before = read();
    await rm(indexPath(root));
    await rebuildIndex(root);
    assert.deepEqual(read(), before, name);
  }
});

test('M1-4: a hand edit to committed text is detected', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('serial-2level');
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const ledger = openLedger(root, 'receipts');
  await ledger.append({ type: 'commit', receiptId: 'seed', nodeId: 'ep-003', variant: 'main', committedHash: sha256(work.tree.nodes.get('ep-003').body) });
  const path = join(root, 'tree/01-arc-awakening/03-ep-003.md');
  await writeFile(path, `${await readFile(path, 'utf8')}손으로 덧붙인 줄.\n`);
  assert.deepEqual(detectDrift(await openWork(root), await ledger.readAll()).map((d) => d.nodeId), ['ep-003']);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/store/drift.js`:
```js
import { sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { lastCommits } from '../ledger/receipts.js';

/** 마지막 커밋 hash와 지금 파일 본문 hash를 비교한다. 본문만 본다. */
export function detectDrift(work, receiptRecords) {
  const drift = [];
  for (const [nodeId, commit] of lastCommits(receiptRecords)) {
    const node = work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!node) { drift.push({ nodeId, code: 'NODE_MISSING' }); continue; }
    const actual = sha256(node.body);
    if (actual !== commit.committedHash) {
      drift.push({ nodeId, code: 'HAND_EDITED', path: node.path, expected: commit.committedHash, actual });
    }
  }
  return drift;
}
```

# M2 실행기

M2 완료 기준(계획서 + 요청):
1. 3단 fixture를 **큰 틀부터 / 바로 쓰기** 두 방식으로 끝까지 도는 테스트 통과. 지식 책 fixture도 같은 테스트 통과
2. 잠금 우회 시도(검사 후 원고 변경, 영수증 없는 커밋, 잠금 단계 끄기 외) 전부 실패
3. 중간에 프로세스를 죽여도 재개됨
4. 1권 장면의 사건을 고치면 2권에서 그 인물을 쓴 장면이 "다시 확인 필요"가 됨
5. 게임북 fixture에서 코어가 깨지지 않고 앞뒤 검사는 "해당 없음"으로 영수증에 남음. 부록 노드는 순서 검사에서 빠짐

**실행기 계약 (모든 M2 Task 공통):**
- `run({ node: 'next' | id })`, `resume({ runId, answers: { [requestId]: string } })`, `decide({ runId, action: 'approve'|'request_revision'|'skip', feedback?, reason? })`
- 반환 `Outcome`:
  - `{ status: 'needs_model', runId, nodeId, requests: [{ requestId, stepId, purpose: 'generate'|'check'|'revise'|'user_revision', produces?, payload }] }`
  - `{ status: 'needs_user', runId, nodeId, question: { kind: 'approve'|'blocked', stepId, draft, advisories, blocking, canApprove? } }`
  - `{ status: 'done', runId, nodeId, receiptId }` · `{ status: 'skipped', runId, nodeId, reason }` · `{ status: 'idle' }`
- 동시에 열린 실행은 하나(vibelore와 같음). `waiting_children` 상태의 가지 실행은 열린 실행으로 치지 않는다.
- 매 단계 처리 뒤 `.folio/runs/<runId>.json`에 원자적으로 저장한다. 메모리 상태 없음 — 매 호출마다 새 실행기 객체여도 같은 결과.

### Task 12: 팩 등록, 검사 세트 합치기, 사용자 검사, 코어 내장 검사

**Files:**
- Create: `core/pack/registry.js`, `core/checks/builtin.js`, `core/checks/user-checks.js`, `core/checks/check-set.js`
- Create: `test/support/fake-pack.js`
- Test: `test/run/check-set.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `WorkConfig`, `foldPromise`, `promiseStageOf`, `PROMISE_KIND`
- Produces:
  - `Pack = { name, pipelines: { leaf: StepDef[], branch: StepDef[] }, checks: CheckDef[], checkSets: Record<string, { checks: string[] }>, prompt?(step, basePayload): object }`
  - `CheckDef = { id, tier: 'deterministic'|'model', stage: string, severity: 'hard'|'block'|'warning'|'advisory', severityOverride?, requiresOrder?: 'linear', requiresParentPlan?: boolean, locked?: boolean, experimental?: boolean, origin: 'core'|'pack'|'user', requireQuote?: boolean, instruction?: string|null, instructionSource?: string|null, instructionText?: string|null, samples?: string|null, run?(ctx): FindingInput[] }`
  - `resolvePacks(names: string[], available: Map<string, Pack>): { primary: Pack, packs: Pack[], checks: CheckDef[], checkSets }` — 오류 `PACK_REQUIRED`, `PACK_UNKNOWN`
  - `BUILTIN_CHECKS: CheckDef[]` — `core.promise-order` (deterministic, stage `facts`, hard, linear, locked)
  - `userCheck(id, spec): { check } | { error }` — `words`/`pattern` → deterministic(stage 기본 `facts`), `rule`/`source`/`samples` → model(stage 기본 `critic`, `requireQuote: true`). 심각도 `advisory`(기본) | `block`. 모두 `experimental: true`
  - `resolveChecks({ registry, config }): { checks: CheckDef[], errors }` — 오류 `CHECK_SET_UNKNOWN`, `CHECK_UNKNOWN`, `LOCKED_CHECK_OVERRIDE`, `USER_CHECK_INVALID`, `USER_CHECK_SEVERITY_INVALID`, `USER_CHECK_PATTERN_INVALID`
  - 검사 컨텍스트(`ctx`)는 Task 14·17에서 채운다: `{ nodeId, ordered, parentPlan, draft, draftEvents, priorEvents, appearances, entityIds, work, node }`

- [ ] **Step 1: 가짜 팩 작성 (테스트 지원 코드)**

`test/support/fake-pack.js`:
```js
/**
 * 모델 없이 실행기를 시험하는 팩. 단계 모양은 계획서의 소설 팩 기본값과 같다.
 * leaf 에는 계획서 대조 결과대로 extract 단계가 facts 앞에 있다.
 */
function deadOnStage(ctx) {
  const dead = new Set();
  for (const event of ctx.priorEvents) {
    if (event.kind !== 'state') continue;
    if (event.data?.vital === 'dead') dead.add(event.entityId);
    if (event.data?.vital === 'alive') dead.delete(event.entityId);
  }
  return ctx.appearances.filter((id) => dead.has(id))
    .map((id) => ({ code: 'DEAD_ON_STAGE', message: `${id} is dead but appears on stage`, entityId: id }));
}

function definedBeforeUse(ctx) {
  const defined = new Set([...ctx.priorEvents, ...ctx.draftEvents].filter((e) => e.kind === 'term.defined').map((e) => e.entityId));
  return ctx.draftEvents.filter((e) => e.kind === 'term.used' && !defined.has(e.entityId))
    .map((e) => ({ code: 'USED_BEFORE_DEFINED', message: `${e.entityId} is used before it is defined`, entityId: e.entityId }));
}

export const fakePack = {
  name: 'fake',
  pipelines: {
    leaf: [
      { id: 'plan', kind: 'generate', produces: 'plan' },
      { id: 'draft', kind: 'generate', produces: 'draft' },
      { id: 'extract', kind: 'generate', produces: 'events', requires: ['draft'] },
      { id: 'facts', kind: 'check', tier: 'deterministic', stage: 'facts', locked: true, requires: ['extract'] },
      { id: 'critic', kind: 'check', tier: 'model', stage: 'critic' },
      { id: 'revise', kind: 'generate', produces: 'draft', loop: { until: ['facts'], max: 3 } },
      { id: 'approve', kind: 'gate' },
      { id: 'commit', kind: 'commit', locked: true },
    ],
    branch: [
      { id: 'plan', kind: 'generate', produces: 'plan' },
      { id: 'fit', kind: 'check', tier: 'model', stage: 'fit' },
      { id: 'revise', kind: 'generate', produces: 'draft', loop: { until: ['fit'], max: 3 } },
      { id: 'approve', kind: 'gate' },
      { id: 'children', kind: 'generate', produces: 'children' },
      { id: 'review', kind: 'check', tier: 'model', stage: 'review' },
      { id: 'commit', kind: 'commit', locked: true },
    ],
  },
  checks: [
    { id: 'fake.dead-on-stage', tier: 'deterministic', stage: 'facts', severity: 'hard', requiresOrder: 'linear', origin: 'pack', run: deadOnStage },
    { id: 'fake.defined-before-use', tier: 'deterministic', stage: 'facts', severity: 'hard', requiresOrder: 'linear', origin: 'pack', run: definedBeforeUse },
    { id: 'fake.critic', tier: 'model', stage: 'critic', severity: 'advisory', origin: 'pack', instruction: 'Review the draft.' },
    { id: 'fake.parent-fit', tier: 'model', stage: 'fit', severity: 'block', requiresParentPlan: true, origin: 'pack', instruction: 'Does this plan keep the parent plan promises?' },
    { id: 'fake.promise-kept', tier: 'model', stage: 'review', severity: 'advisory', origin: 'pack', instruction: 'Did the finished children keep this plan?' },
  ],
  checkSets: {
    'fake.basic': { checks: ['fake.dead-on-stage', 'fake.defined-before-use', 'fake.critic', 'fake.parent-fit', 'fake.promise-kept'] },
    'fake.empty': { checks: [] },
  },
};

export const fakePacks = () => new Map([['fake', fakePack]]);
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/run/check-set.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { validateWorkConfig } from '../../core/work/config.js';
import { resolvePacks } from '../../core/pack/registry.js';
import { resolveChecks } from '../../core/checks/check-set.js';
import { BUILTIN_CHECKS } from '../../core/checks/builtin.js';
import { fakePack, fakePacks } from '../support/fake-pack.js';

const config = (patch = {}) => validateWorkConfig({
  levels: [{ name: 'chapter' }, { name: 'scene', leaf: true }], packs: ['fake'], checkSets: ['fake.basic'], ...patch,
}).config;
const resolve = (patch) => resolveChecks({ registry: resolvePacks(config(patch).packs, fakePacks()), config: config(patch) });
const byId = (checks) => Object.fromEntries(checks.map((c) => [c.id, c]));

test('resolvePacks uses the first pack as primary and rejects unknown packs', () => {
  const registry = resolvePacks(['fake'], fakePacks());
  assert.equal(registry.primary.name, 'fake');
  assert.ok(registry.checks.some((c) => c.id === 'fake.critic'));
  assert.throws(() => resolvePacks([], fakePacks()), { code: 'PACK_REQUIRED' });
  assert.throws(() => resolvePacks(['novel'], fakePacks()), { code: 'PACK_UNKNOWN' });
});

test('selected sets plus the locked core checks', () => {
  const { checks, errors } = resolve();
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  assert.deepEqual(checks.map((c) => c.id).sort(), [
    'core.promise-order', 'fake.critic', 'fake.dead-on-stage', 'fake.defined-before-use', 'fake.parent-fit', 'fake.promise-kept',
  ]);
  assert.equal(byId(checks)['core.promise-order'].locked, true);
});

test('an empty set still keeps the core checks', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(resolve({ checkSets: ['fake.empty'] }).checks.map((c) => c.id), ['core.promise-order']);
});

test('pack checks can be turned off or raised to block', () => {
  const { checks, errors } = resolve({ checks: { 'fake.critic': { mode: 'off' }, 'fake.promise-kept': { severity: 'block' } } });
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  assert.equal(byId(checks)['fake.critic'], undefined);
  assert.equal(byId(checks)['fake.promise-kept'].severityOverride, 'block');
});

test('locked checks cannot be turned off or softened', () => {
  for (const spec of [{ mode: 'off' }, { severity: 'advisory' }]) {
    assert.deepEqual(resolve({ checks: { 'core.promise-order': spec } }).errors.map((e) => e.code), ['LOCKED_CHECK_OVERRIDE']);
  }
});

test('a user word check is deterministic, experimental and advisory by default', () => {
  const check = byId(resolve({ checks: { 'no-suddenly': { words: ['갑자기'] } } }).checks)['no-suddenly'];
  assert.deepEqual([check.tier, check.stage, check.severity, check.experimental, check.origin], ['deterministic', 'facts', 'advisory', true, 'user']);
  assert.deepEqual(check.run({ draft: '그는 갑자기 웃었다.' }).map((f) => [f.code, f.quote]), [['USER_PATTERN', '갑자기']]);
});

test('a user rule is a model check that must quote the draft', () => {
  const check = byId(resolve({ checks: { 'no-honorifics': { source: 'checks/no-honorifics.md', severity: 'block' } } }).checks)['no-honorifics'];
  assert.deepEqual([check.tier, check.stage, check.severity, check.requireQuote, check.instructionSource], ['model', 'critic', 'block', true, 'checks/no-honorifics.md']);
});

const invalid = [
  [{ checks: { x: { words: ['a'], severity: 'loud' } } }, 'USER_CHECK_SEVERITY_INVALID'],
  [{ checks: { y: {} } }, 'USER_CHECK_INVALID'],
  [{ checks: { z: { pattern: '(' } } }, 'USER_CHECK_PATTERN_INVALID'],
  [{ checkSets: ['ghost.set'] }, 'CHECK_SET_UNKNOWN'],
  [{ checks: { 'fake.critic': { severity: 'hard' } } }, 'USER_CHECK_SEVERITY_INVALID'],
];
for (const [patch, code] of invalid) {
  test(`reports ${code}`, () => {
    assert.ok(resolve(patch).errors.some((e) => e.code === code), JSON.stringify(resolve(patch).errors));
  });
}

test('a set naming a missing check is reported', () => {
  const pack = { ...fakePack, checkSets: { broken: { checks: ['nope'] } } };
  const cfg = config({ checkSets: ['broken'] });
  assert.deepEqual(resolveChecks({ registry: resolvePacks(['fake'], new Map([['fake', pack]])), config: cfg }).errors.map((e) => e.code), ['CHECK_UNKNOWN']);
});

test('core.promise-order flags a payoff before the plant inside this draft', () => {
  const [check] = BUILTIN_CHECKS;
  const work = { entities: new Map([['red-letter', { id: 'red-letter', kind: 'promise' }]]) };
  const findings = check.run({
    work, nodeId: 'n2', priorEvents: [],
    draftEvents: [{ eventId: 'draft-1', nodeId: 'n2', entityId: 'red-letter', kind: 'promise.resolved' }],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(findings.map((f) => [f.code, f.severity]), [['PROMISE_RESOLVED_BEFORE_PLANT', 'hard']]);
  const ok = check.run({
    work, nodeId: 'n2',
    priorEvents: [{ eventId: 'e1', nodeId: 'n1', entityId: 'red-letter', kind: 'promise.planted' }],
    draftEvents: [{ eventId: 'draft-1', nodeId: 'n2', entityId: 'red-letter', kind: 'promise.resolved' }],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(ok, []);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 구현**

`core/pack/registry.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';

/**
 * work.yaml 의 packs 순서대로 팩을 모은다. 단계 정의와 프롬프트는 첫 팩(primary)이 준다.
 * 검사와 검사 세트는 모든 팩에서 합친다.
 */
export function resolvePacks(names, available) {
  if (!names.length) throw new FolioError('PACK_REQUIRED', 'work.yaml must list at least one pack');
  const packs = names.map((name) => {
    const pack = available.get(name);
    if (!pack) throw new FolioError('PACK_UNKNOWN', name);
    return pack;
  });
  return {
    primary: packs[0],
    packs,
    checks: packs.flatMap((pack) => (pack.checks ?? []).map((check) => ({ origin: 'pack', ...check }))),
    checkSets: Object.assign({}, ...packs.map((pack) => pack.checkSets ?? {})),
  };
}
```

`core/checks/builtin.js`:
```js
import { foldPromise, promiseStageOf, PROMISE_KIND } from '../promise.js';

/** 심기 전 해소는 잠금 규칙이다. 팩과 사용자 설정으로 끌 수 없다. */
export const PROMISE_ORDER_CHECK = Object.freeze({
  id: 'core.promise-order',
  tier: 'deterministic',
  stage: 'facts',
  severity: 'hard',
  requiresOrder: 'linear',
  locked: true,
  origin: 'core',
  run(ctx) {
    const isPromise = (id) => ctx.work.entities.get(id)?.kind === PROMISE_KIND;
    const own = ctx.draftEvents.filter((e) => isPromise(e.entityId) && promiseStageOf(e));
    const findings = [];
    for (const entityId of new Set(own.map((e) => e.entityId))) {
      const sequence = [...ctx.priorEvents.filter((e) => e.entityId === entityId), ...own.filter((e) => e.entityId === entityId)];
      for (const problem of foldPromise(sequence).problems) {
        if (problem.nodeId !== ctx.nodeId) continue;
        findings.push({ severity: problem.severity, code: problem.code, message: `${entityId}: ${problem.code}`, entityId });
      }
    }
    return findings;
  },
});

export const BUILTIN_CHECKS = [PROMISE_ORDER_CHECK];
```

`core/checks/user-checks.js`:
```js
export const USER_SEVERITIES = ['advisory', 'block'];

function patternFindings(draft, words, regex) {
  const findings = [];
  for (const word of words) if (draft.includes(word)) findings.push({ code: 'USER_PATTERN', message: `금지 표현 "${word}"`, quote: word });
  if (regex) for (const match of draft.matchAll(regex)) findings.push({ code: 'USER_PATTERN', message: `패턴 /${regex.source}/`, quote: match[0] });
  return findings;
}

/**
 * work.yaml checks 의 사용자 검사 하나를 검사 정의로 바꾼다.
 * 새 검사는 experimental 이고 기본 심각도는 참고 의견이다(계획서 §12).
 */
export function userCheck(id, spec) {
  if (!spec || typeof spec !== 'object' || Array.isArray(spec)) return { error: { code: 'USER_CHECK_INVALID', checkId: id } };
  const severity = spec.severity ?? 'advisory';
  if (!USER_SEVERITIES.includes(severity)) return { error: { code: 'USER_CHECK_SEVERITY_INVALID', checkId: id, severity } };
  const base = { id, severity, experimental: true, origin: 'user', config: spec };
  if (Array.isArray(spec.words) || typeof spec.pattern === 'string') {
    let regex = null;
    try { regex = spec.pattern ? new RegExp(spec.pattern, 'gu') : null; }
    catch { return { error: { code: 'USER_CHECK_PATTERN_INVALID', checkId: id } }; }
    const words = (spec.words ?? []).filter((word) => typeof word === 'string' && word);
    return { check: { ...base, tier: 'deterministic', stage: spec.stage ?? 'facts', run: (ctx) => patternFindings(ctx.draft ?? '', words, regex) } };
  }
  if (typeof spec.rule === 'string' || typeof spec.source === 'string' || typeof spec.samples === 'string') {
    return {
      check: {
        ...base, tier: 'model', stage: spec.stage ?? 'critic', requireQuote: true,
        instruction: spec.rule ?? null, instructionSource: spec.source ?? null, samples: spec.samples ?? null,
      },
    };
  }
  return { error: { code: 'USER_CHECK_INVALID', checkId: id } };
}
```

`core/checks/check-set.js`:
```js
import { BUILTIN_CHECKS } from './builtin.js';
import { userCheck, USER_SEVERITIES } from './user-checks.js';

/** 코어 내장 검사 + 고른 검사 세트 + work.yaml checks 를 합친다. */
export function resolveChecks({ registry, config }) {
  const errors = [];
  const known = new Map([...BUILTIN_CHECKS, ...registry.checks].map((check) => [check.id, check]));
  const selected = new Map(BUILTIN_CHECKS.map((check) => [check.id, check]));
  for (const setName of config.checkSets) {
    const set = registry.checkSets[setName];
    if (!set) { errors.push({ code: 'CHECK_SET_UNKNOWN', set: setName }); continue; }
    for (const id of set.checks) {
      const check = known.get(id);
      if (check) selected.set(id, check);
      else errors.push({ code: 'CHECK_UNKNOWN', checkId: id, set: setName });
    }
  }
  for (const [id, spec] of Object.entries(config.checks)) {
    const existing = known.get(id);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.locked) { errors.push({ code: 'LOCKED_CHECK_OVERRIDE', checkId: id }); continue; }
      if (spec?.mode === 'off') { selected.delete(id); continue; }
      if (spec?.severity !== undefined && !USER_SEVERITIES.includes(spec.severity)) {
        errors.push({ code: 'USER_CHECK_SEVERITY_INVALID', checkId: id, severity: spec.severity });
        continue;
      }
      selected.set(id, spec?.severity ? { ...existing, severityOverride: spec.severity } : existing);
      continue;
    }
    if (spec?.mode === 'off') continue;
    const made = userCheck(id, spec);
    if (made.error) errors.push(made.error);
    else selected.set(id, made.check);
  }
  return { checks: [...selected.values()], errors };
}
```

### Task 13: 단계 정의를 데이터로 — 덮어쓰기 합치기와 잠금 거부

**Files:**
- Create: `core/pipeline/define.js`, `core/pipeline/resolve.js`
- Test: `test/run/pipeline.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `canonicalJson`, `sha256`, `CheckDef`
- Produces:
  - `StepDef = { id, kind: 'generate'|'check'|'gate'|'commit', tier?, stage?, produces?: 'plan'|'draft'|'events'|'children', requires?: string[], loop?: { until: string[], max: number }, locked?: boolean, prompt? }`
  - `ResolvedStep = StepDef & { mode: 'on'|'off'|'auto', locked: boolean, requires: string[], reason?: string, source?: string, custom?: boolean }`
  - `resolvePipeline(base: StepDef[], overrides): { steps: ResolvedStep[], errors, matched: Set<string>, digest: string }`
  - `resolvePipelines({ primary, overrides, checks }): { leaf, branch, errors }` + `customStepChecks({ leaf, branch }): CheckDef[]`
  - 잠금: `locked: true`인 단계와 그것이 `requires`로 (전이적으로) 요구하는 단계. 잠긴 단계를 건드리는 모든 덮어쓰기는 `LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE`
  - 기타 오류: `STEP_UNKNOWN`, `STEP_AFTER_UNKNOWN`, `STEP_MODE_INVALID`, `STEP_FIELD_IMMUTABLE`, `LOOP_MAX_EXCEEDED`(최대 3), `STEP_KIND_INVALID`, `STEP_TIER_INVALID`, `COMMIT_MUST_BE_LAST`, `REQUIRES_ORDER`, `LOCKED_ORDER_CHANGED`, `CHECK_STAGE_MISSING`, `CHECK_TIER_MISMATCH`, `LOCKED_CHECK_UNGUARDED`
  - `MAX_LOOP = 3`, 건너뛴 단계의 `source` = `'work.yaml#overrides.<id>'`

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { resolvePipeline } from '../../core/pipeline/define.js';
import { resolvePipelines, customStepChecks } from '../../core/pipeline/resolve.js';
import { BUILTIN_CHECKS } from '../../core/checks/builtin.js';
import { fakePack } from '../support/fake-pack.js';

const leaf = (overrides) => resolvePipeline(fakePack.pipelines.leaf, overrides);
const codes = (result) => result.errors.map((e) => e.code);

test('the default leaf pipeline locks commit, facts and everything facts needs', () => {
  const { steps, errors, digest } = leaf({});
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  assert.deepEqual(steps.map((s) => s.id), ['plan', 'draft', 'extract', 'facts', 'critic', 'revise', 'approve', 'commit']);
  assert.deepEqual(steps.filter((s) => s.locked).map((s) => s.id), ['draft', 'extract', 'facts', 'commit']);
  assert.match(digest, /^sha256:/);
  assert.notEqual(leaf({ critic: { mode: 'off' } }).digest, digest);
});

test('adjustable steps can be switched off with a reason, auto-approved or tuned', () => {
  const { steps, errors } = leaf({ critic: { mode: 'off', reason: '초고 속도 우선' }, approve: { mode: 'auto' }, revise: { max: 2 } });
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  const byId = Object.fromEntries(steps.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
  assert.deepEqual([byId.critic.mode, byId.critic.reason, byId.critic.source], ['off', '초고 속도 우선', 'work.yaml#overrides.critic']);
  assert.equal(byId.approve.mode, 'auto');
  assert.equal(byId.revise.loop.max, 2);
});

test('a new check step can be added after an existing one', () => {
  const { steps, errors } = leaf({ tone: { after: 'critic', kind: 'check', tier: 'model', prompt: 'checks/tone.md' } });
  assert.deepEqual(errors, []);
  assert.deepEqual(steps.map((s) => s.id).slice(4, 6), ['critic', 'tone']);
  assert.deepEqual([steps[5].custom, steps[5].stage, steps[5].locked], [true, 'tone', false]);
});

const lockedAttempts = {
  'facts off': { facts: { mode: 'off', reason: 'x' } },
  'extract off': { extract: { mode: 'off' } },
  'draft off': { draft: { mode: 'off' } },
  'commit off': { commit: { mode: 'off' } },
  'facts moved': { facts: { after: 'critic' } },
  'facts unlocked': { facts: { locked: false } },
};
for (const [label, overrides] of Object.entries(lockedAttempts)) {
  test(`locked step override is refused: ${label}`, () => {
    assert.deepEqual(codes(leaf(overrides)), ['LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE']);
  });
}

const otherErrors = [
  [{ critic: { mode: 'auto' } }, 'STEP_MODE_INVALID'],
  [{ revise: { max: 5 } }, 'LOOP_MAX_EXCEEDED'],
  [{ critic: { max: 2 } }, 'STEP_FIELD_IMMUTABLE'],
  [{ critic: { kind: 'gate' } }, 'STEP_FIELD_IMMUTABLE'],
  [{ critic: { after: 'commit' } }, 'COMMIT_MUST_BE_LAST'],
  [{ critic: { after: 'ghost' } }, 'STEP_AFTER_UNKNOWN'],
  [{ extra: { after: 'critic', kind: 'commit' } }, 'STEP_KIND_INVALID'],
  [{ extra: { after: 'critic', kind: 'check', tier: 'magic' } }, 'STEP_TIER_INVALID'],
];
for (const [overrides, code] of otherErrors) {
  test(`reports ${code}`, () => {
    assert.ok(codes(leaf(overrides)).includes(code), JSON.stringify(leaf(overrides).errors));
  });
}

test('resolvePipelines applies overrides where the step exists and reports strays once', () => {
  const checks = [...BUILTIN_CHECKS, ...fakePack.checks];
  const ok = resolvePipelines({ primary: fakePack, overrides: { critic: { mode: 'off' }, fit: { mode: 'off' } }, checks });
  assert.deepEqual(ok.errors, []);
  const stray = resolvePipelines({ primary: fakePack, overrides: { ghost: { mode: 'off' }, commit: { mode: 'off' } }, checks });
  assert.deepEqual(stray.errors.map((e) => e.code).sort(), ['LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE', 'STEP_UNKNOWN']);
});

test('checks must live in a stage of matching tier, and locked checks in a locked step', () => {
  const run = (check) => resolvePipelines({ primary: fakePack, overrides: {}, checks: [check] }).errors.map((e) => e.code);
  assert.deepEqual(run({ id: 'a', tier: 'model', stage: 'facts' }), ['CHECK_TIER_MISMATCH']);
  assert.deepEqual(run({ id: 'b', tier: 'model', stage: 'nowhere' }), ['CHECK_STAGE_MISSING']);
  assert.deepEqual(run({ id: 'c', tier: 'model', stage: 'critic', locked: true }), ['LOCKED_CHECK_UNGUARDED']);
});

test('custom model check steps bring their own advisory check', () => {
  const pipelines = resolvePipelines({ primary: fakePack, overrides: { tone: { after: 'critic', kind: 'check', tier: 'model', prompt: 'checks/tone.md' } }, checks: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(customStepChecks(pipelines).map((c) => [c.id, c.stage, c.severity, c.instructionSource, c.requireQuote]), [
    ['tone', 'tone', 'advisory', 'checks/tone.md', true],
  ]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/pipeline/define.js`:
```js
import { canonicalJson, sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';

export const STEP_KINDS = ['generate', 'check', 'gate', 'commit'];
export const STEP_MODES = ['on', 'off', 'auto'];
export const CHECK_TIERS = ['deterministic', 'model'];
export const MAX_LOOP = 3;
const SOURCE = 'work.yaml#overrides';
const PATCHABLE = ['mode', 'reason', 'max', 'after', 'prompt'];

function markLocked(steps) {
  const byId = new Map(steps.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
  const locked = new Set(steps.filter((s) => s.locked).map((s) => s.id));
  let grew = true;
  while (grew) {
    grew = false;
    for (const id of [...locked]) {
      for (const dep of byId.get(id)?.requires ?? []) if (!locked.has(dep)) { locked.add(dep); grew = true; }
    }
  }
  for (const step of steps) step.locked = locked.has(step.id);
}

function moveAfter(steps, step, targetId, errors) {
  const target = steps.find((s) => s.id === targetId);
  if (!target) { errors.push({ code: 'STEP_AFTER_UNKNOWN', stepId: step.id, after: targetId }); return; }
  steps.splice(steps.indexOf(step), 1);
  steps.splice(steps.indexOf(target) + 1, 0, step);
}

function addCustom(steps, id, patch, errors) {
  if (!['generate', 'check', 'gate'].includes(patch.kind)) { errors.push({ code: 'STEP_KIND_INVALID', stepId: id }); return; }
  if (patch.kind === 'check' && !CHECK_TIERS.includes(patch.tier)) { errors.push({ code: 'STEP_TIER_INVALID', stepId: id }); return; }
  const at = steps.findIndex((s) => s.id === patch.after);
  steps.splice(at + 1, 0, {
    id, kind: patch.kind, tier: patch.tier, stage: id, prompt: patch.prompt ?? null, mode: patch.mode ?? 'on',
    requires: [], locked: false, custom: true, source: `${SOURCE}.${id}`,
  });
}

function applyPatch(steps, step, patch, errors) {
  const id = step.id;
  if (!patch || typeof patch !== 'object') { errors.push({ code: 'OVERRIDE_INVALID', stepId: id }); return; }
  if (step.locked) { errors.push({ code: 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE', stepId: id, fields: Object.keys(patch) }); return; }
  for (const field of Object.keys(patch)) {
    if (!PATCHABLE.includes(field)) errors.push({ code: 'STEP_FIELD_IMMUTABLE', stepId: id, field });
  }
  step.source = `${SOURCE}.${id}`;
  if (patch.mode !== undefined) {
    if (!STEP_MODES.includes(patch.mode) || (patch.mode === 'auto' && step.kind !== 'gate')) {
      errors.push({ code: 'STEP_MODE_INVALID', stepId: id, mode: patch.mode });
    } else {
      step.mode = patch.mode;
      step.reason = typeof patch.reason === 'string' ? patch.reason : '';
    }
  }
  if (patch.max !== undefined) {
    if (!step.loop) errors.push({ code: 'STEP_FIELD_IMMUTABLE', stepId: id, field: 'max' });
    else if (!Number.isInteger(patch.max) || patch.max < 0 || patch.max > MAX_LOOP) errors.push({ code: 'LOOP_MAX_EXCEEDED', stepId: id, max: patch.max });
    else step.loop = { ...step.loop, max: patch.max };
  }
  if (patch.prompt !== undefined) step.prompt = patch.prompt;
  if (patch.after !== undefined) moveAfter(steps, step, patch.after, errors);
}

function checkInvariants(steps, lockedOrder, errors) {
  const ids = new Set();
  for (const step of steps) {
    if (ids.has(step.id)) errors.push({ code: 'STEP_ID_DUPLICATE', stepId: step.id });
    ids.add(step.id);
    if (!STEP_KINDS.includes(step.kind)) errors.push({ code: 'STEP_KIND_INVALID', stepId: step.id });
  }
  if (steps.filter((s) => s.kind === 'commit').length !== 1 || steps.at(-1)?.kind !== 'commit') errors.push({ code: 'COMMIT_MUST_BE_LAST' });
  const index = new Map(steps.map((s, i) => [s.id, i]));
  for (const step of steps) {
    for (const dep of step.requires) if (!(index.get(dep) < index.get(step.id))) errors.push({ code: 'REQUIRES_ORDER', stepId: step.id, requires: dep });
  }
  if (steps.filter((s) => s.locked).map((s) => s.id).join() !== lockedOrder.join()) errors.push({ code: 'LOCKED_ORDER_CHANGED' });
}

/** 팩 기본 단계 + work.yaml overrides. 잠금 단계는 어떤 필드도 바꿀 수 없다. */
export function resolvePipeline(base, overrides = {}) {
  const errors = [];
  const steps = base.map((step) => ({ mode: 'on', ...structuredClone(step), requires: [...(step.requires ?? [])] }));
  markLocked(steps);
  const lockedOrder = steps.filter((s) => s.locked).map((s) => s.id);
  const matched = new Set();
  for (const [id, patch] of Object.entries(overrides)) {
    const step = steps.find((s) => s.id === id);
    if (step) { matched.add(id); applyPatch(steps, step, patch, errors); continue; }
    if (patch?.kind && patch?.after && steps.some((s) => s.id === patch.after)) { matched.add(id); addCustom(steps, id, patch, errors); }
  }
  checkInvariants(steps, lockedOrder, errors);
  const digest = sha256(canonicalJson(steps.map(({ id, kind, tier, stage, produces, mode, locked, loop, requires, prompt }) => ({ id, kind, tier, stage, produces, mode, locked, loop, requires, prompt }))));
  return { steps, errors, matched, digest };
}
```

`core/pipeline/resolve.js`:
```js
import { canonicalJson } from '../util/hash.js';
import { resolvePipeline } from './define.js';

const stageOf = (step) => step.stage ?? step.id;

function dedupe(errors) {
  const seen = new Set();
  return errors.filter((error) => { const key = canonicalJson(error); if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true; });
}

/** leaf/branch 파이프라인을 만들고, 모든 검사가 들어갈 단계가 있는지 본다. */
export function resolvePipelines({ primary, overrides, checks }) {
  const leaf = resolvePipeline(primary.pipelines.leaf, overrides);
  const branch = resolvePipeline(primary.pipelines.branch, overrides);
  const errors = [...leaf.errors, ...branch.errors];
  for (const [id, patch] of Object.entries(overrides)) {
    if (!leaf.matched.has(id) && !branch.matched.has(id)) errors.push({ code: patch?.kind ? 'STEP_AFTER_UNKNOWN' : 'STEP_UNKNOWN', stepId: id });
  }
  const checkSteps = [...leaf.steps, ...branch.steps].filter((s) => s.kind === 'check');
  for (const check of checks) {
    const homes = checkSteps.filter((s) => stageOf(s) === check.stage);
    if (!homes.length) errors.push({ code: 'CHECK_STAGE_MISSING', checkId: check.id, stage: check.stage });
    else if (homes.some((s) => s.tier !== check.tier)) errors.push({ code: 'CHECK_TIER_MISMATCH', checkId: check.id, stage: check.stage });
    if (check.locked && !leaf.steps.some((s) => s.kind === 'check' && s.locked && stageOf(s) === check.stage)) {
      errors.push({ code: 'LOCKED_CHECK_UNGUARDED', checkId: check.id });
    }
  }
  return { leaf, branch, errors: dedupe(errors) };
}

/** work.yaml 로 추가한 검사 단계(prompt 파일 지정)는 같은 이름의 참고 의견 검사를 하나 갖는다. */
export function customStepChecks({ leaf, branch }) {
  const seen = new Set();
  const checks = [];
  for (const step of [...leaf.steps, ...branch.steps]) {
    if (!step.custom || step.kind !== 'check' || !step.prompt || seen.has(step.id)) continue;
    seen.add(step.id);
    checks.push({
      id: step.id, tier: step.tier, stage: step.id, severity: 'advisory', experimental: true, origin: 'user',
      requireQuote: step.tier === 'model', instruction: null, instructionSource: step.prompt,
      ...(step.tier === 'deterministic' ? { run: () => [] } : {}),
    });
  }
  return checks;
}
```

### Task 14: 검사 실행 — 결정론 검사, 모델 검사 요청·응답, 해당 없음

**Files:**
- Create: `core/checks/run-checks.js`, `core/run/answers.js`
- Test: `test/run/run-checks.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CheckDef`, `isBlocking`
- Produces:
  - `parseJsonAnswer(answer: string|object): any` — 코드펜스 제거 후 JSON.parse, 실패 시 `FolioError('ANSWER_JSON_INVALID')`
  - `applicability(check, ctx): 'applicable'|'not_applicable'` — `requiresOrder: 'linear'`이고 `ctx.ordered`가 거짓이면, 또는 `requiresParentPlan`이고 `ctx.parentPlan`이 없으면 해당 없음
  - `runDeterministicChecks(checks, ctx): CheckResult[]` — 던진 검사는 `status: 'error'`; 잠금·hard 검사가 던지면 hard 결과(닫힌 쪽으로 실패)
  - `buildModelCheckRequest(checks, ctx): { checks: [{checkId, instruction, instructionText, severity, requireQuote, samples}], draft, answerFormat }`
  - `parseModelCheckAnswer(checks, answer, draft): CheckResult[]` — 심각도는 검사 정의가 정한다(모델이 못 바꿈). `requireQuote`인데 인용이 원고에 없으면 `unsupported`로 빼고 판정에 안 쓴다. `reviewer`는 항상 `{ sameHost: true, independent: false }`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/run/run-checks.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { applicability, runDeterministicChecks, buildModelCheckRequest, parseModelCheckAnswer } from '../../core/checks/run-checks.js';
import { parseJsonAnswer } from '../../core/run/answers.js';

const linear = { id: 'order', tier: 'deterministic', severity: 'hard', requiresOrder: 'linear', run: () => [{ code: 'X', message: 'x' }] };
const parentFit = { id: 'fit', tier: 'model', severity: 'block', requiresParentPlan: true };

test('order-dependent checks are not applicable off the linear order', () => {
  assert.equal(applicability(linear, { ordered: true }), 'applicable');
  assert.equal(applicability(linear, { ordered: false }), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(applicability(parentFit, { ordered: true, parentPlan: null }), 'not_applicable');
  assert.equal(applicability(parentFit, { ordered: true, parentPlan: '계획' }), 'applicable');
});

test('deterministic results stamp check id and severity', () => {
  const [result] = runDeterministicChecks([linear], { ordered: true });
  assert.equal(result.status, 'failed');
  assert.deepEqual(result.findings, [{ code: 'X', message: 'x', checkId: 'order', severity: 'hard' }]);
  assert.deepEqual(runDeterministicChecks([linear], { ordered: false }), [{ checkId: 'order', status: 'not_applicable', findings: [] }]);
});

test('a finding may carry its own softer severity unless the user overrode it', () => {
  const mixed = { id: 'm', tier: 'deterministic', severity: 'hard', run: () => [{ code: 'W', message: 'w', severity: 'warning' }] };
  assert.equal(runDeterministicChecks([mixed], { ordered: true })[0].findings[0].severity, 'warning');
  assert.equal(runDeterministicChecks([{ ...mixed, severityOverride: 'block' }], { ordered: true })[0].findings[0].severity, 'block');
});

test('a crashing locked check fails closed', () => {
  const crash = { id: 'c', tier: 'deterministic', severity: 'hard', locked: true, run: () => { throw new Error('boom'); } };
  const [result] = runDeterministicChecks([crash], { ordered: true });
  assert.equal(result.status, 'error');
  assert.equal(result.findings[0].severity, 'hard');
  const soft = runDeterministicChecks([{ ...crash, locked: false, severity: 'advisory' }], { ordered: true })[0];
  assert.equal(soft.findings[0].severity, 'advisory');
});

test('model check requests carry only the check instructions and the draft', () => {
  const request = buildModelCheckRequest([{ id: 'r', tier: 'model', severity: 'block', requireQuote: true, instructionText: '존댓말 금지' }], { draft: '본문' });
  assert.deepEqual(request.checks, [{ checkId: 'r', instruction: null, instructionText: '존댓말 금지', severity: 'block', requireQuote: true, samples: null }]);
  assert.equal(request.draft, '본문');
  assert.match(request.answerFormat, /findings/);
});

test('model answers cannot choose severity, need quotes when required and are never independent', () => {
  const checks = [
    { id: 'rule', tier: 'model', severity: 'block', requireQuote: true },
    { id: 'critic', tier: 'model', severity: 'advisory' },
  ];
  const answer = JSON.stringify({
    reviewer: { independent: true },
    findings: [
      { checkId: 'rule', code: 'HONORIFIC', message: '존댓말', quote: '하셨어요', severity: 'advisory' },
      { checkId: 'rule', code: 'HONORIFIC', message: '인용 없음' },
      { checkId: 'critic', code: 'PACE', message: '느림', severity: 'hard' },
      { checkId: 'ghost', code: 'X', message: 'x' },
    ],
  });
  const results = parseModelCheckAnswer(checks, `\`\`\`json\n${answer}\n\`\`\``, '그가 말하셨어요.');
  const rule = results.find((r) => r.checkId === 'rule');
  const critic = results.find((r) => r.checkId === 'critic');
  assert.deepEqual(rule.findings.map((f) => [f.severity, f.quote]), [['block', '하셨어요']]);
  assert.equal(rule.unsupported.length, 1);
  assert.equal(rule.status, 'failed');
  assert.deepEqual(critic.findings.map((f) => f.severity), ['advisory']);
  assert.equal(critic.status, 'passed');
  for (const r of results) assert.deepEqual(r.reviewer, { sameHost: true, independent: false });
});

test('an unreadable model answer is an error result, blocking only for locked checks', () => {
  const [open] = parseModelCheckAnswer([{ id: 'critic', tier: 'model', severity: 'advisory' }], 'not json', '');
  assert.deepEqual([open.status, open.findings.length], ['error', 0]);
  const [locked] = parseModelCheckAnswer([{ id: 'l', tier: 'model', severity: 'hard', locked: true }], 'not json', '');
  assert.equal(locked.findings[0].severity, 'hard');
});

test('parseJsonAnswer strips code fences and rejects garbage', () => {
  const fence = '`'.repeat(3);
  assert.deepEqual(parseJsonAnswer(`${fence}json\n{"a":1}\n${fence}`), { a: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(parseJsonAnswer({ a: 1 }), { a: 1 });
  assert.throws(() => parseJsonAnswer('nope'), { code: 'ANSWER_JSON_INVALID' });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/run/answers.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';

/** 호스트 답은 문자열 JSON 또는 객체다. 코드펜스는 벗긴다. */
export function parseJsonAnswer(answer) {
  if (answer !== null && typeof answer === 'object') return answer;
  const text = String(answer ?? '').replace(/^\s*`{3}(?:json)?\s*/i, '').replace(/\s*`{3}\s*$/, '').trim();
  try { return JSON.parse(text); }
  catch { throw new FolioError('ANSWER_JSON_INVALID', 'the answer is not valid JSON'); }
}
```

`core/checks/run-checks.js`:
```js
import { isBlocking } from '../ledger/receipts.js';
import { parseJsonAnswer } from '../run/answers.js';

const SAME_HOST = Object.freeze({ sameHost: true, independent: false });
const ANSWER_FORMAT = '{"findings":[{"checkId":"<id>","code":"<CODE>","message":"<why>","quote":"<verbatim text from the draft>"}]}';

export function applicability(check, ctx) {
  if (check.requiresOrder === 'linear' && !ctx.ordered) return 'not_applicable';
  if (check.requiresParentPlan && !ctx.parentPlan) return 'not_applicable';
  return 'applicable';
}

function stamp(check, finding) {
  return {
    ...finding,
    code: String(finding.code ?? 'FINDING'),
    message: String(finding.message ?? finding.code ?? ''),
    checkId: check.id,
    severity: check.severityOverride ?? finding.severity ?? check.severity,
  };
}

const statusOf = (findings) => (findings.some(isBlocking) ? 'failed' : 'passed');

export function runDeterministicChecks(checks, ctx) {
  return checks.map((check) => {
    if (applicability(check, ctx) === 'not_applicable') return { checkId: check.id, status: 'not_applicable', findings: [] };
    try {
      const findings = (check.run(ctx) ?? []).map((finding) => stamp(check, finding));
      return { checkId: check.id, status: statusOf(findings), findings };
    } catch (error) {
      const severity = check.locked || check.severity === 'hard' ? 'hard' : 'advisory';
      return { checkId: check.id, status: 'error', findings: [{ checkId: check.id, severity, code: 'CHECK_CRASHED', message: error.message }] };
    }
  });
}

export function buildModelCheckRequest(checks, ctx) {
  return {
    checks: checks.map((check) => ({
      checkId: check.id, instruction: check.instruction ?? null, instructionText: check.instructionText ?? null,
      severity: check.severityOverride ?? check.severity, requireQuote: Boolean(check.requireQuote), samples: check.samples ?? null,
    })),
    draft: ctx.draft,
    answerFormat: ANSWER_FORMAT,
  };
}

/** 같은 호스트의 검토다. 모델이 뭐라고 하든 독립 평가로 표기하지 않는다. */
export function parseModelCheckAnswer(checks, answer, draft) {
  let parsed = null;
  try { parsed = parseJsonAnswer(answer); } catch { parsed = null; }
  if (!parsed || !Array.isArray(parsed.findings)) {
    return checks.map((check) => ({
      checkId: check.id, status: 'error', reviewer: { ...SAME_HOST }, unsupported: [],
      findings: check.locked ? [{ checkId: check.id, severity: 'hard', code: 'CHECK_ANSWER_INVALID', message: 'model answer was not a findings list' }] : [],
    }));
  }
  const results = new Map(checks.map((check) => [check.id, { checkId: check.id, status: 'passed', findings: [], reviewer: { ...SAME_HOST }, unsupported: [] }]));
  for (const raw of parsed.findings) {
    const check = checks.find((c) => c.id === raw?.checkId);
    if (!check) continue;
    const { severity: _ignored, ...rest } = raw;
    const finding = stamp(check, { code: rest.code, message: rest.message, ...(typeof rest.quote === 'string' ? { quote: rest.quote } : {}) });
    finding.severity = check.severityOverride ?? check.severity;
    const result = results.get(check.id);
    if (check.requireQuote && !(finding.quote && String(draft).includes(finding.quote))) { result.unsupported.push(finding); continue; }
    result.findings.push(finding);
  }
  for (const result of results.values()) result.status = statusOf(result.findings);
  return [...results.values()];
}
```

### Task 15: 검사 범위(앞쪽 방향)와 다시 확인 필요(뒤쪽 방향)

**Files:**
- Create: `core/scope/scope.js`, `core/scope/stale.js`
- Test: `test/run/scope.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Work`, `eventsBefore`, `ancestorsOf`, `scanMentions`, `assetRefsInBody`, `lastCommits`, `receiptsById`, `sha256`, `canonicalJson`
- Produces:
  - `Ref = { kind: 'node'|'entity'|'asset'|'source', id, rev: string|null }`
  - `gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId, draftText?, extracted? }): Scope`
  - `Scope = { nodeId, ordered, appearances: string[], entityIds: string[], priorEvents, upperPlans: {nodeId, level, rev, text}[], parentPlan: string|null, upperMentions: {nodeId, entityIds}[], assets, sources, refs: Ref[] }`
    - `appearances` = frontmatter `entities` ∪ `extracted.appearances` (M4에서 등장/언급 구분이 정교해진다)
    - `entityIds` = appearances ∪ 원고 이름 스캔 ∪ `extracted.mentions` ∪ 추출 사건의 엔티티
    - `upperPlans` = 본문(계획)이 비어 있지 않은 조상만. 비어 있는 조상은 "참고하지 않은 것"이라 ref에 안 들어간다(바로 쓰기 뒤 묶기에서 자식이 stale 되지 않게)
  - `entityRevAt({ work, events, entityId, nodeId }): string` — `sha256(canonicalJson({ file: entity.rev, events: [[eventId, kind, data] 중 nodeId보다 앞선 것] }))`
  - `findStale({ work, receiptRecords, events }): { nodeId, reasons: (Ref & { was, now })[] }[]` — 커밋된 노드마다 마지막 영수증의 refs를 지금 값으로 다시 계산해 다르면 표시. 읽는 순서로 정렬. 자동으로 고치지 않는다.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../../core/ledger/events.js';
import { gatherScope, entityRevAt } from '../../core/scope/scope.js';
import { findStale } from '../../core/scope/stale.js';
import { sha256 } from '../../core/util/hash.js';
import { copyFixture, seedEvents } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function seeded(name) {
  const root = await copyFixture(name);
  const seeds = await seedEvents(root, name);
  return { root, seeds, work: await openWork(root), events: activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll()) };
}

test('scope gathers entities, earlier events on any branch, upper plans and assets', async () => {
  const { work, events } = await seeded('novel-3level');
  const scope = gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId: 'funeral', draftText: work.tree.nodes.get('funeral').body });
  assert.equal(scope.ordered, true);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.entityIds, ['junho', 'mara']);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.priorEvents.map((e) => `${e.nodeId}:${e.entityId}`), ['last-night:mara']);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.upperPlans.map((p) => p.nodeId), ['after', 'book-two']);
  assert.equal(scope.parentPlan, work.tree.nodes.get('after').body);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.upperMentions.map((m) => [m.nodeId, m.entityIds]), [
    ['book-one', ['junho', 'mara']], ['the-fall', ['junho', 'mara']], ['the-letter', ['mara']],
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.assets.map((a) => a.id), ['img-rooftop']);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.refs.map((r) => `${r.kind}:${r.id}`), ['node:after', 'node:book-two', 'entity:junho', 'entity:mara', 'asset:img-rooftop']);
});

test('extracted events and appearances join the scope', async () => {
  const { work, events } = await seeded('novel-3level');
  const scope = gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId: 'market', draftText: '시장.', extracted: { events: [{ entityId: 'red-letter', kind: 'promise.advanced' }], appearances: ['junho'], mentions: [] } });
  assert.deepEqual(scope.appearances, ['junho']);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.entityIds, ['junho', 'red-letter']);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.priorEvents.map((e) => e.kind), ['promise.resolved', 'promise.planted']);
});

test('off-order nodes gather no earlier events', async () => {
  const { work, events } = await seeded('gamebook');
  const scope = gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId: 'ending-trap', draftText: '놋쇠 열쇠' });
  assert.equal(scope.ordered, false);
  assert.deepEqual(scope.priorEvents, []);
});

test('an entity rev at a node changes only when earlier events or the entity file change', async () => {
  const { root, seeds, work, events } = await seeded('novel-3level');
  const at = (evts, w = work) => entityRevAt({ work: w, events: evts, entityId: 'mara', nodeId: 'funeral' });
  const base = at(events);
  const later = [...events, { type: 'event', eventId: 'x', seq: 99, nodeId: 'market', entityId: 'mara', kind: 'state', data: {} }];
  assert.equal(at(later), base);
  const ledger = openLedger(root, 'events');
  await ledger.append({ type: 'retraction', receiptId: 'r', nodeId: 'last-night', supersedes: [seeds[2].eventId] });
  assert.notEqual(at(activeEvents(await ledger.readAll())), base);
  const path = join(root, 'entities/character/mara.md');
  await writeFile(path, `${await readFile(path, 'utf8')}설정 추가.\n`);
  assert.notEqual(at(events, await openWork(root)), base);
});

async function commitWithScope(root, nodeId) {
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const events = activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll());
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId);
  const scope = gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId, draftText: node.body });
  const receipts = openLedger(root, 'receipts');
  const receiptId = `rcpt-${nodeId}`;
  await receipts.append({ type: 'receipt', receiptId, nodeId, variant: 'main', draftHash: sha256(node.body), refs: scope.refs });
  await receipts.append({ type: 'commit', receiptId, nodeId, variant: 'main', committedHash: sha256(node.body) });
  return receipts;
}

test('changing an earlier event marks later users of that entity stale, and nobody else', async () => {
  const { root, seeds } = await seeded('novel-3level');
  for (const id of ['stairwell', 'funeral', 'market']) await commitWithScope(root, id);
  const read = async () => findStale({
    work: await openWork(root),
    receiptRecords: await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll(),
    events: activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll()),
  });
  assert.deepEqual(await read(), []);
  await openLedger(root, 'events').append({ type: 'retraction', receiptId: 'r', nodeId: 'last-night', supersedes: [seeds[2].eventId] });
  const stale = await read();
  assert.deepEqual(stale.map((s) => s.nodeId), ['funeral']);
  assert.deepEqual(stale[0].reasons.map((r) => `${r.kind}:${r.id}`), ['entity:mara']);
});

test('changing an upper plan marks its children stale', async () => {
  const { root } = await seeded('novel-3level');
  await commitWithScope(root, 'market');
  const path = join(root, 'tree/02-book-two/01-after/_node.md');
  await writeFile(path, (await readFile(path, 'utf8')).replace('장례 뒤의 나날.', '장례 뒤, 이사.'));
  const stale = findStale({ work: await openWork(root), receiptRecords: await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll(), events: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(stale.map((s) => [s.nodeId, s.reasons.map((r) => `${r.kind}:${r.id}`)]), [['market', ['node:after']]]);
});
```

`core/scope/scope.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { ancestorsOf } from '../store/tree.js';
import { assetRefsInBody } from '../store/assets.js';
import { eventsBefore } from '../ledger/events.js';
import { scanMentions } from '../index/scan.js';
import { canonicalJson, sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';

const uniqueSorted = (ids) => [...new Set(ids)].sort();

/** 엔티티 파일 + 이 노드보다 앞선 그 엔티티의 사건. 뒤쪽 방향 전파의 기준값이다. */
export function entityRevAt({ work, events, entityId, nodeId }) {
  const before = eventsBefore({ events, positions: work.positions, nodeId });
  const relevant = before.events.filter((e) => e.entityId === entityId).map((e) => [e.eventId, e.kind, e.data ?? null]);
  return sha256(canonicalJson({ file: work.entities.get(entityId)?.rev ?? null, events: relevant }));
}

/**
 * 앞쪽 방향: 이 노드에 나온 엔티티가 나온 모든 곳을 모은다(가지·층 무관).
 * 트리 이웃이 아니라 엔티티가 기준이다(계획서 §10).
 */
export function gatherScope({ work, events, nodeId, draftText = '', extracted = null }) {
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId);
  if (!node) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', nodeId);
  const known = (id) => work.entities.has(id);
  const appearances = uniqueSorted([...node.entities, ...(extracted?.appearances ?? [])].filter(known));
  const scanned = [...scanMentions(draftText, work.entities.values()).keys()];
  const fromEvents = (extracted?.events ?? []).map((e) => e.entityId);
  const entityIds = uniqueSorted([...appearances, ...scanned, ...(extracted?.mentions ?? []), ...fromEvents].filter(known));
  const before = eventsBefore({ events, positions: work.positions, nodeId });
  const priorEvents = before.events.filter((e) => entityIds.includes(e.entityId));
  const ancestors = ancestorsOf(work.tree, nodeId);
  const ancestorIds = new Set(ancestors.map((a) => a.id));
  const upperPlans = ancestors.filter((a) => a.body.trim())
    .map((a) => ({ nodeId: a.id, level: a.level, rev: sha256(a.body), text: a.body }));
  const upperMentions = [...work.tree.nodes.values()]
    .filter((n) => n.kind === 'branch' && n.id !== nodeId && !ancestorIds.has(n.id) && n.body.trim())
    .map((n) => ({ nodeId: n.id, entityIds: [...scanMentions(n.body, work.entities.values()).keys()].filter((id) => entityIds.includes(id)).sort() }))
    .filter((m) => m.entityIds.length > 0);
  const assetIds = new Set([...node.assets, ...assetRefsInBody(draftText)]);
  const assets = [...work.assets.values()].filter((a) => assetIds.has(a.id) || a.entities.some((id) => entityIds.includes(id)));
  const sourceIds = Array.isArray(node.data.sources) ? node.data.sources : [];
  const sources = sourceIds.map((id) => work.sources.get(id)).filter(Boolean);
  const refs = [
    ...upperPlans.map((p) => ({ kind: 'node', id: p.nodeId, rev: p.rev })),
    ...entityIds.map((id) => ({ kind: 'entity', id, rev: entityRevAt({ work, events, entityId: id, nodeId }) })),
    ...assets.map((a) => ({ kind: 'asset', id: a.id, rev: a.rev })),
    ...sources.map((s) => ({ kind: 'source', id: s.id, rev: s.rev })),
  ];
  const parent = upperPlans.find((p) => p.nodeId === node.parentId);
  return {
    nodeId, ordered: before.applicable, appearances, entityIds, priorEvents, upperPlans,
    parentPlan: parent?.text ?? null, upperMentions, assets, sources, refs,
  };
}
```

`core/scope/stale.js`:
```js
import { sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { lastCommits, receiptsById } from '../ledger/receipts.js';
import { entityRevAt } from './scope.js';

function currentRev({ work, events, ref, nodeId }) {
  switch (ref.kind) {
    case 'node': { const node = work.tree.nodes.get(ref.id); return node && node.body.trim() ? sha256(node.body) : null; }
    case 'entity': return work.entities.has(ref.id) ? entityRevAt({ work, events, entityId: ref.id, nodeId }) : null;
    case 'asset': return work.assets.get(ref.id)?.rev ?? null;
    case 'source': return work.sources.get(ref.id)?.rev ?? null;
    default: return null;
  }
}

/** 뒤쪽 방향: 커밋 때 참고한 버전이 지금과 다르면 "다시 확인 필요". 표시만 하고 고치지 않는다. */
export function findStale({ work, receiptRecords, events }) {
  const receipts = receiptsById(receiptRecords);
  const stale = [];
  for (const [nodeId, commit] of lastCommits(receiptRecords)) {
    const receipt = receipts.get(commit.receiptId);
    if (!receipt || !work.tree.nodes.has(nodeId)) continue;
    const reasons = [];
    for (const ref of receipt.refs ?? []) {
      const now = currentRev({ work, events, ref, nodeId });
      if (now !== ref.rev) reasons.push({ ...ref, was: ref.rev, now });
    }
    if (reasons.length) stale.push({ nodeId, reasons });
  }
  return stale.sort((a, b) => work.positions.get(a.nodeId).ordinal - work.positions.get(b.nodeId).ordinal);
}
```

- Test: `test/run/commit.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `validateReceipt`, `activeEvents`, `updateNode`, `sha256`, `canonicalJson`, `ResolvedStep`
- Produces:
  - `commitNode({ root, work, ledgers: { receipts, events }, pipeline: { steps, digest }, receipt, text, hooks?, resume? }): Promise<{ receiptId, nodeId, committedHash, alreadyCommitted? }>` — 순서: 잠금 검사 → 영수증 기록 → 본문 파일 쓰기(status committed) → `hooks.afterBodyWrite?.()` → 이전 사건 철회 + 새 사건 → commit 기록. 각 단계는 멱등이라 중간에 죽어도 `resume: true`로 이어서 끝낸다.
  - 잠금 오류 코드: `LOCK_NO_RECEIPT`, `LOCK_RECEIPT_INVALID`(details.problems), `LOCK_PIPELINE_MISMATCH`, `LOCK_STEP_MISSING`, `LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH`, `LOCK_HARD_VIOLATION`, `LOCK_RECEIPT_CONSUMED`, `LOCK_RECEIPT_MISMATCH`
  - `buildReceipt({ run, steps, work, refs, newId }): Receipt`
  - 테스트 지원: `makeReceipt({ nodeId, text, pipeline, level?, receiptId?, events? }): Receipt`

`test/support/receipts.js`:
```js
import { sha256 } from '../../core/util/hash.js';

/** 모든 단계가 통과한 영수증. 우회 테스트는 이것을 망가뜨려 쓴다. */
export function makeReceipt({ nodeId, text, pipeline, level = 'scene', receiptId = 'rcpt-test', events = [] }) {
  const draftHash = sha256(text);
  const steps = pipeline.steps.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'commit').map((s) => {
    if (s.kind === 'check') return { id: s.id, kind: s.kind, status: 'passed', draftHash, checks: [] };
    if (s.kind === 'gate') return { id: s.id, kind: s.kind, status: 'approved', source: 'user' };
    if (s.loop) return { id: s.id, kind: s.kind, status: 'not_needed' };
    return { id: s.id, kind: s.kind, status: 'passed' };
  });
  return {
    receiptVersion: 1, receiptId, runId: 'run-test', nodeId, variant: 'main', level, draftHash,
    pipelineDigest: pipeline.digest, steps, refs: [], hard: 0, advisories: [], overridden: [], attempts: 0, events,
  };
}
```

`test/run/commit.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../../core/ledger/events.js';
import { detectDrift } from '../../core/store/drift.js';
import { resolvePipeline } from '../../core/pipeline/define.js';
import { commitNode } from '../../core/run/commit.js';
import { copyFixture } from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { makeReceipt } from '../support/receipts.js';
import { fakePack } from '../support/fake-pack.js';

const pipeline = resolvePipeline(fakePack.pipelines.leaf, {});
const SCENE = 'tree/01-book-one/01-the-fall/02-stairwell.md';

async function setup() {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const ledgers = { receipts: openLedger(root, 'receipts'), events: openLedger(root, 'events') };
  const commit = async (receipt, text, extra = {}) => commitNode({ root, work: await openWork(root), ledgers, pipeline, receipt, text, ...extra });
  return { root, ledgers, commit };
}

test('a valid receipt commits the text, records events and leaves no drift', async () => {
  const { root, ledgers, commit } = await setup();
  const text = '준호는 계단참에서 오래 기다렸다.\n';
  const receipt = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text, pipeline, events: [{ eventId: 'ev-1', entityId: 'junho', kind: 'state', data: { waiting: true } }] });
  const result = await commit(receipt, text);
  assert.equal(result.committedHash, receipt.draftHash);
  const work = await openWork(root);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('stairwell').body, text);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('stairwell').status, 'committed');
  const records = await ledgers.receipts.readAll();
  assert.deepEqual(records.map((r) => r.type), ['receipt', 'commit']);
  assert.deepEqual(activeEvents(await ledgers.events.readAll()).map((e) => e.eventId), ['ev-1']);
  assert.deepEqual(detectDrift(work, records), []);
});

const attempts = {
  LOCK_NO_RECEIPT: () => [null, 'x'],
  LOCK_RECEIPT_INVALID: () => { const r = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline }); r.steps[4] = { id: 'critic', kind: 'check', status: 'skipped' }; return [r, 'x']; },
  LOCK_PIPELINE_MISMATCH: () => { const r = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline }); r.pipelineDigest = 'sha256:' + '0'.repeat(64); return [r, 'x']; },
  LOCK_STEP_MISSING: () => { const r = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline }); r.steps = r.steps.filter((s) => s.id !== 'facts'); return [r, 'x']; },
  LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH: () => [makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: '검사한 본문', pipeline }), '바꿔치기한 본문'],
  LOCK_HARD_VIOLATION: () => { const r = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline }); r.hard = 1; return [r, 'x']; },
};
for (const [code, make] of Object.entries(attempts)) {
  test(`commit is refused with ${code} and nothing changes`, async () => {
    const { root, ledgers, commit } = await setup();
    const before = await readFile(join(root, SCENE), 'utf8');
    const [receipt, text] = make();
    await assert.rejects(commit(receipt, text), { code });
    assert.equal(await readFile(join(root, SCENE), 'utf8'), before);
    assert.deepEqual(await ledgers.receipts.readAll(), []);
  });
}

test('a self-review labelled independent is refused', async () => {
  const { commit } = await setup();
  const receipt = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline });
  receipt.steps[4].checks = [{ checkId: 'fake.critic', status: 'passed', findings: [], reviewer: { sameHost: true, independent: true } }];
  await assert.rejects(commit(receipt, 'x'), (error) => error.code === 'LOCK_RECEIPT_INVALID' && error.details.problems.some((p) => p.code === 'SELF_REVIEW_MISLABELED'));
});

test('a receipt is consumed once', async () => {
  const { commit } = await setup();
  const receipt = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline });
  await commit(receipt, 'x');
  await assert.rejects(commit(receipt, 'x'), { code: 'LOCK_RECEIPT_CONSUMED' });
});

test('a receipt id reused with different content is refused', async () => {
  const { ledgers, commit } = await setup();
  const receipt = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'x', pipeline });
  await ledgers.receipts.append({ type: 'receipt', ...receipt, attempts: 2 });
  await assert.rejects(commit(receipt, 'x'), { code: 'LOCK_RECEIPT_MISMATCH' });
});

test('recommitting a node retracts its earlier events', async () => {
  const { ledgers, commit } = await setup();
  await commit(makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'a', pipeline, receiptId: 'r1', events: [{ eventId: 'ev-a', entityId: 'mara', kind: 'state', data: { vital: 'alive' } }] }), 'a');
  await commit(makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'b', pipeline, receiptId: 'r2', events: [{ eventId: 'ev-b', entityId: 'mara', kind: 'state', data: { vital: 'dead' } }] }), 'b');
  assert.deepEqual(activeEvents(await ledgers.events.readAll()).map((e) => e.eventId), ['ev-b']);
});

test('a commit interrupted after the body write finishes on resume, exactly once', async () => {
  const { root, ledgers, commit } = await setup();
  const receipt = makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'stairwell', text: 'z', pipeline, events: [{ eventId: 'ev-z', entityId: 'junho', kind: 'state' }] });
  await assert.rejects(commit(receipt, 'z', { hooks: { afterBodyWrite: () => { throw new Error('crash'); } } }), /crash/);
  assert.deepEqual((await ledgers.receipts.readAll()).map((r) => r.type), ['receipt']);
  await commit(receipt, 'z', { resume: true });
  await commit(receipt, 'z', { resume: true });
  assert.deepEqual((await ledgers.receipts.readAll()).map((r) => r.type), ['receipt', 'commit']);
  assert.deepEqual((await ledgers.events.readAll()).map((r) => r.eventId), ['ev-z']);
  assert.equal((await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.get('stairwell').body, 'z');
});
```

`core/run/commit.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { canonicalJson, sha256 } from '../util/hash.js';
import { updateNode } from '../store/tree.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../ledger/events.js';
import { validateReceipt } from '../ledger/receipts.js';

const LOCKED_OK = { generate: ['passed'], check: ['passed', 'failed', 'not_applicable'], gate: ['approved', 'auto'] };

function assertLocks({ pipeline, receipt, text }) {
  if (!receipt) throw new FolioError('LOCK_NO_RECEIPT', 'a commit needs a check receipt');
  const problems = validateReceipt(receipt);
  if (problems.length) throw new FolioError('LOCK_RECEIPT_INVALID', problems.map((p) => p.code).join(', '), { problems });
  if (receipt.pipelineDigest !== pipeline.digest) throw new FolioError('LOCK_PIPELINE_MISMATCH', 'the receipt was issued under a different pipeline');
  for (const step of pipeline.steps.filter((s) => s.locked && s.kind !== 'commit')) {
    const entry = receipt.steps.find((s) => s.id === step.id);
    if (!entry || !(LOCKED_OK[step.kind] ?? []).includes(entry.status)) {
      throw new FolioError('LOCK_STEP_MISSING', `locked step ${step.id} did not run`, { stepId: step.id });
    }
  }
  if (receipt.draftHash !== sha256(text)) throw new FolioError('LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH', 'the text to commit is not the text that was checked');
  const hard = receipt.steps.flatMap((s) => s.checks ?? []).flatMap((c) => c.findings ?? []).filter((f) => f.severity === 'hard');
  if (receipt.hard !== 0 || hard.length) throw new FolioError('LOCK_HARD_VIOLATION', 'hard conflicts block the commit', { findings: hard });
}

async function appendNodeEvents(ledger, receipt) {
  const records = await ledger.readAll();
  if (!records.some((r) => r.type === 'retraction' && r.receiptId === receipt.receiptId)) {
    const prior = activeEvents(records).filter((e) => e.nodeId === receipt.nodeId && e.receiptId !== receipt.receiptId).map((e) => e.eventId);
    if (prior.length) await ledger.append({ type: 'retraction', receiptId: receipt.receiptId, nodeId: receipt.nodeId, supersedes: prior });
  }
  for (const event of receipt.events) {
    if (!records.some((r) => r.eventId === event.eventId)) {
      await ledger.append({ type: 'event', receiptId: receipt.receiptId, nodeId: receipt.nodeId, ...event });
    }
  }
}

/**
 * 원고를 확정하는 유일한 함수. 잠금 규칙은 여기서 전부 검사하며 설정으로 끌 수 없다.
 * 모든 쓰기는 멱등이라 중간에 죽으면 resume: true 로 다시 불러 끝낸다.
 */
export async function commitNode({ root, work, ledgers, pipeline, receipt, text, hooks = {}, resume = false }) {
  assertLocks({ pipeline, receipt, text });
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(receipt.nodeId);
  if (!node) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', receipt.nodeId);
  const records = await ledgers.receipts.readAll();
  if (records.some((r) => r.type === 'commit' && r.receiptId === receipt.receiptId)) {
    if (resume) return { receiptId: receipt.receiptId, nodeId: node.id, committedHash: receipt.draftHash, alreadyCommitted: true };
    throw new FolioError('LOCK_RECEIPT_CONSUMED', `${receipt.receiptId} was already used`);
  }
  const stored = records.find((r) => r.type === 'receipt' && r.receiptId === receipt.receiptId);
  if (stored) {
    const { type: _t, seq: _s, at: _a, prev: _p, hash: _h, ...body } = stored;
    if (canonicalJson(body) !== canonicalJson(receipt)) throw new FolioError('LOCK_RECEIPT_MISMATCH', `${receipt.receiptId} differs from the recorded receipt`);
  } else {
    await ledgers.receipts.append({ type: 'receipt', ...receipt });
  }
  await updateNode(root, node, { data: { status: 'committed' }, body: text });
  await hooks.afterBodyWrite?.();
  await appendNodeEvents(ledgers.events, receipt);
  await ledgers.receipts.append({ type: 'commit', receiptId: receipt.receiptId, nodeId: node.id, variant: receipt.variant, committedHash: receipt.draftHash });
  return { receiptId: receipt.receiptId, nodeId: node.id, committedHash: receipt.draftHash };
}
```

`core/run/receipt-builder.js`:
```js
import { RECEIPT_VERSION, isBlocking } from '../ledger/receipts.js';

/** 실행 상태에서 영수증을 만든다. 건너뛴 단계도 이유·출처와 함께 남는다. */
export function buildReceipt({ run, steps, work, refs, newId }) {
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(run.nodeId);
  const entries = steps.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'commit').map((step) => {
    const result = run.results[step.id];
    const entry = { id: step.id, kind: step.kind, status: result?.status ?? 'not_run' };
    if (result?.reason !== undefined) entry.reason = result.reason;
    if (result?.source !== undefined) entry.source = result.source;
    if (step.kind === 'check') { entry.draftHash = result?.draftHash ?? null; entry.checks = result?.checks ?? []; }
    return entry;
  });
  const findings = entries.flatMap((e) => (e.checks ?? []).flatMap((c) => c.findings ?? []));
  return {
    receiptVersion: RECEIPT_VERSION,
    receiptId: newId('rcpt'),
    runId: run.runId,
    nodeId: run.nodeId,
    variant: run.variant,
    level: node.level,
    draftHash: run.draftHash,
    pipelineDigest: run.pipelineDigest,
    steps: entries,
    refs,
    hard: findings.filter((f) => f.severity === 'hard').length,
    advisories: findings.filter((f) => !isBlocking(f)),
    overridden: run.overridden ?? [],
    attempts: run.attempts,
    events: (run.outputs.events?.events ?? []).map((event) => ({ eventId: newId('ev'), ...event })),
  };
}
```

`test/support/fake-host.js`:
```js
/**
 * 모델 대신 답하는 가짜 호스트. script 값은 노드 id(또는 '*')별 값이나 (payload) => 값 함수다.
 * 검사 요청은 단계 id(critic/fit/review/...)별 함수로 답한다.
 */
export function createFakeHost(script = {}) {
  const s = { plans: {}, drafts: {}, events: {}, children: {}, revisions: {}, ...script };
  const calls = [];
  const pick = (table, payload) => {
    const value = table?.[payload.node.id] ?? table?.['*'];
    return typeof value === 'function' ? value(payload) : value;
  };
  function respond(request) {
    const p = request.payload;
    calls.push({ purpose: request.purpose, stepId: request.stepId, nodeId: p.node.id, payload: p });
    if (request.purpose === 'generate') {
      if (p.produces === 'plan') return pick(s.plans, p) ?? `${p.node.title || p.node.id} 계획`;
      if (p.produces === 'draft') return pick(s.drafts, p) ?? `${p.node.title || p.node.id} 본문`;
      if (p.produces === 'events') return JSON.stringify(pick(s.events, p) ?? { events: [] });
      if (p.produces === 'children') return JSON.stringify({ children: pick(s.children, p) ?? [] });
    }
    if (request.purpose === 'revise' || request.purpose === 'user_revision') return pick(s.revisions, p) ?? p.draft;
    if (request.purpose === 'check') return JSON.stringify({ findings: (s[p.step] ?? (() => []))(p), ...(s.reviewer ? { reviewer: s.reviewer } : {}) });
    throw new Error(`fake host cannot answer ${request.purpose}/${p.produces}`);
  }
  return { calls, answer: (outcome) => Object.fromEntries(outcome.requests.map((r) => [r.requestId, respond(r)])) };
}

/** 매 호출마다 새 실행기를 만든다 — 메모리 상태 없이 파일만으로 이어지는지 함께 검증한다. */
export async function driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, {
  start = { node: 'next' }, from = null, user = () => ({ action: 'approve' }),
  until = (o) => o.status === 'idle', maxSteps = 2000,
} = {}) {
  const trail = [];
  let outcome = from ?? await makeRunner().run(start);
  for (let i = 0; i < maxSteps; i += 1) {
    trail.push(outcome);
    if (until(outcome) || outcome.status === 'idle' || outcome.status === 'skipped') return trail;
    if (outcome.status === 'needs_model') outcome = await makeRunner().resume({ runId: outcome.runId, answers: host.answer(outcome) });
    else if (outcome.status === 'needs_user') outcome = await makeRunner().decide({ runId: outcome.runId, ...user(outcome) });
    else if (outcome.status === 'done') outcome = await makeRunner().run({ node: 'next' });
    else throw new Error(`unexpected outcome ${outcome.status}`);
  }
  throw new Error('driveToEnd exceeded maxSteps');
}

export const label = (o) => (o.status === 'needs_model' ? `model:${o.requests[0].stepId}`
  : o.status === 'needs_user' ? `user:${o.question.kind}` : o.status);
```

`test/support/fixtures.js`에 추가 (import 목록에 `rm`, `mkdir`, `writeFile`, `stringifyYaml` 합치기):
```js
/** work.yaml 을 가짜 팩으로 바꾼다. 원래 사용자 검사는 지운다(테스트가 필요한 것만 넣는다). */
export async function useFakePack(root, { checks = {}, overrides = {}, checkSets = ['fake.basic'] } = {}) {
  const path = join(root, 'work.yaml');
  const config = parseYaml(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
  await writeFile(path, stringifyYaml({ ...config, packs: ['fake'], checkSets, checks, overrides }));
}

/** fixture 의 설정·엔티티는 두고 트리만 비운다(큰 틀부터/바로 쓰기 테스트용). */
export async function emptyTreeCopy(name) {
  const root = await copyFixture(name);
  await rm(join(root, 'tree'), { recursive: true });
  await mkdir(join(root, 'tree'));
  return root;
}

export { fakePacks } from './fake-pack.js';
```
