`test/run/runner-leaf.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { createRunner } from '../../core/run/runner.js';
import { createRunStore } from '../../core/run/run-store.js';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../../core/ledger/events.js';
import { findStale } from '../../core/scope/stale.js';
import { copyFixture, seedEvents, useFakePack, fakePacks } from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { createFakeHost, driveToEnd, label } from '../support/fake-host.js';

async function setup({ fixture = 'novel-3level', seed = false, checks, overrides } = {}) {
  const root = await copyFixture(fixture);
  if (seed) await seedEvents(root, fixture);
  await useFakePack(root, { checks, overrides });
  return { root, makeRunner: () => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() }) };
}
const untilDone = (o) => o.status === 'done';
const receiptsOf = async (root) => (await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'receipt');
const stepStatus = (receipt) => Object.fromEntries(receipt.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.status]));

test('a leaf runs plan, draft, extract, facts, critic, approval and commit', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup();
  const text = '준호는 계단참에서 마라를 기다렸다.\n';
  const host = createFakeHost({ drafts: { stairwell: text } });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: untilDone });
  assert.deepEqual(trail.map(label), ['model:plan', 'model:draft', 'model:extract', 'model:critic', 'user:approve', 'done']);
  const node = (await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.get('stairwell');
  assert.deepEqual([node.body, node.status], [text, 'committed']);
  const [receipt] = await receiptsOf(root);
  assert.deepEqual(stepStatus(receipt), { plan: 'passed', draft: 'passed', extract: 'passed', facts: 'passed', critic: 'passed', revise: 'not_needed', approve: 'approved' });
  assert.deepEqual(receipt.refs.map((r) => `${r.kind}:${r.id}`), ['node:the-fall', 'node:book-one', 'entity:junho', 'entity:mara', 'asset:img-rooftop']);
  assert.deepEqual(receipt.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'critic').checks[0].reviewer, { sameHost: true, independent: false });
  assert.equal((await createRunStore(root).list())[0].state, 'done');
});

test('critic can be skipped with a reason and approval automated', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup({ overrides: { critic: { mode: 'off', reason: '초고 속도 우선' }, approve: { mode: 'auto' } } });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, createFakeHost(), { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: untilDone });
  assert.deepEqual(trail.map(label), ['model:plan', 'model:draft', 'model:extract', 'done']);
  const [receipt] = await receiptsOf(root);
  const critic = receipt.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'critic');
  assert.deepEqual([critic.status, critic.reason, critic.source], ['skipped', '초고 속도 우선', 'work.yaml#overrides.critic']);
  assert.deepEqual(receipt.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'approve').status, 'auto');
});

test('soft findings are shown to the user and never auto-revised', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup();
  const host = createFakeHost({ critic: () => [{ checkId: 'fake.critic', code: 'PACE', message: '전개가 느리다' }] });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: untilDone });
  const question = trail.find((o) => o.status === 'needs_user').question;
  assert.deepEqual(question.advisories.map((f) => f.code), ['PACE']);
  assert.ok(!host.calls.some((c) => c.purpose === 'revise'));
  assert.deepEqual((await receiptsOf(root))[0].advisories.map((f) => f.code), ['PACE']);
});

test('a user block check triggers a minimal revision that sees only blocking findings', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup({ checks: { 'no-suddenly': { words: ['갑자기'], severity: 'block' } } });
  const host = createFakeHost({
    drafts: { stairwell: '그는 갑자기 나타났다.' },
    revisions: { stairwell: '그는 천천히 나타났다.' },
    critic: () => [{ checkId: 'fake.critic', code: 'PACE', message: '느리다' }],
  });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: untilDone });
  assert.deepEqual(trail.map(label), ['model:plan', 'model:draft', 'model:extract', 'model:critic', 'model:revise', 'model:extract', 'model:critic', 'user:approve', 'done']);
  const revise = host.calls.find((c) => c.purpose === 'revise');
  assert.deepEqual(revise.payload.blocking.map((f) => f.code), ['USER_PATTERN']);
  assert.equal(revise.payload.advisories, undefined);
  const [receipt] = await receiptsOf(root);
  assert.deepEqual([receipt.attempts, stepStatus(receipt).facts, stepStatus(receipt).revise], [1, 'passed', 'passed']);
});

test('a user block that survives three revisions can be overridden with a reason', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup({ checks: { 'no-suddenly': { words: ['갑자기'], severity: 'block' } } });
  const host = createFakeHost({ drafts: { stairwell: '그는 갑자기 나타났다.' } });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' });
  const blocked = trail.at(-1);
  assert.deepEqual([blocked.question.kind, blocked.question.canApprove], ['blocked', true]);
  assert.equal(host.calls.filter((c) => c.purpose === 'revise').length, 3);
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'approve' }), { code: 'OVERRIDE_REASON_REQUIRED' });
  const done = await makeRunner().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'approve', reason: '의도한 표현' });
  assert.equal(done.status, 'done');
  const [receipt] = await receiptsOf(root);
  assert.deepEqual([receipt.hard, stepStatus(receipt).revise, receipt.overridden[0].code, receipt.overridden[0].overrideReason], [0, 'overridden', 'USER_PATTERN', '의도한 표현']);
});

test('hard conflicts cannot be approved and are never committed', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup({ fixture: 'serial-2level', seed: true });
  const host = createFakeHost();
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'ep-004' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' });
  const blocked = trail.at(-1);
  assert.deepEqual([blocked.question.kind, blocked.question.canApprove], ['blocked', false]);
  assert.deepEqual(host.calls.find((c) => c.purpose === 'revise').payload.blocking.map((f) => f.code), ['DEAD_ON_STAGE']);
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'approve', reason: '괜찮다' }), { code: 'APPROVE_BLOCKED_BY_HARD' });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'skip' }), { code: 'SKIP_REASON_REQUIRED' });
  const skipped = await makeRunner().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'skip', reason: '설정 재검토' });
  assert.equal(skipped.status, 'skipped');
  assert.deepEqual((await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'commit'), []);
});

  const { makeRunner } = await setup({ checks: { 'no-honorifics': { source: 'checks/no-honorifics.md', severity: 'block' } } });
  const host = createFakeHost({ critic: () => [{ checkId: 'no-honorifics', code: 'HONORIFIC', message: '존댓말', quote: '원고에 없는 문장' }] });

  assert.match(critic.payload.checks.find((c) => c.checkId === 'no-honorifics').instructionText, /존댓말/);
});

test('a model claiming independence is still labelled a same-host review', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup();

  const [receipt] = await receiptsOf(root);

test('answers must match the pending requests exactly', async () => {
  const { makeRunner } = await setup();
  const first = await makeRunner().run({ node: 'stairwell' });
  const [request] = first.requests;
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().resume({ runId: first.runId, answers: {} }), { code: 'ANSWERS_INCOMPLETE' });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().resume({ runId: first.runId, answers: { [request.requestId]: 'p', 'req-ghost': 'x' } }), { code: 'ANSWER_UNKNOWN_REQUEST' });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().decide({ runId: first.runId, action: 'approve' }), { code: 'RUN_NOT_AWAITING_USER' });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().run({ node: 'market' }), { code: 'RUN_IN_PROGRESS' });
  assert.deepEqual(await makeRunner().run({ node: 'next' }), first);
  const second = await makeRunner().resume({ runId: first.runId, answers: { [request.requestId]: '계획' } });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().resume({ runId: first.runId, answers: { [request.requestId]: '계획' } }), { code: 'ANSWERS_INCOMPLETE' });
  assert.equal(second.requests[0].stepId, 'draft');
});

test('tampering with the checked draft in the run file cannot reach a commit', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup();
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, createFakeHost(), { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' });
  const { runId } = trail.at(-1);
  const path = createRunStore(root).pathOf(runId);
  const run = JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
  run.outputs.draft = '검사 뒤에 몰래 바꾼 원고';
  await writeFile(path, JSON.stringify(run));
  const scene = join(root, 'tree/01-book-one/01-the-fall/02-stairwell.md');
  const before = await readFile(scene, 'utf8');
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().decide({ runId, action: 'approve' }), { code: 'LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH' });
  assert.equal(await readFile(scene, 'utf8'), before);
  assert.deepEqual(await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll(), []);
});

test('an entity edit between check and commit forces a re-check and a fresh approval', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup();
  const host = createFakeHost({ drafts: { stairwell: '마라가 계단을 올랐다.' } });
  const first = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { start: { node: 'stairwell' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' });

  assert.equal(label(next), 'model:extract');
  const rest = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { from: next, until: untilDone });
  assert.deepEqual(rest.map(label), ['model:extract', 'model:critic', 'user:approve', 'done']);
  const stale = findStale({
    work: await openWork(root),
    receiptRecords: await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll(),
    events: activeEvents(await openLedger(root, 'events').readAll()),
  });
  assert.deepEqual(stale, [], 'the receipt must reference the edited entity, not the old one');
});

test('a tampered ledger stops the runner', async () => {
  const { root, makeRunner } = await setup({ overrides: { approve: { mode: 'auto' } } });
```

`core/run/run-store.js`:
```js
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { listEntries, readTextOrNull, writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';

export const OPEN_STATES = new Set(['active', 'awaiting_model', 'awaiting_user', 'committing']);
const RUN_ID = /^run-[a-z0-9-]+$/;

/** 진행 중 작업 상태. 매 단계 뒤 원자적으로 저장해 프로세스가 죽어도 이어간다. */
export function createRunStore(root) {
  const dir = join(root, '.folio', 'runs');
  const pathOf = (runId) => {
    if (!RUN_ID.test(runId)) throw new FolioError('RUN_ID_INVALID', String(runId));
    return join(dir, `${runId}.json`);
  };
  return {
    pathOf,
    async save(run) { await writeFileAtomic(pathOf(run.runId), `${JSON.stringify(run, null, 2)}\n`); },
    async load(runId) {
      const text = await readTextOrNull(pathOf(runId));
      if (text === null) throw new FolioError('RUN_NOT_FOUND', runId);
      return JSON.parse(text);
    },
    async list() {
      const runs = [];
      for (const entry of await listEntries(dir)) {
        if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith('.json')) runs.push(JSON.parse(await readTextOrNull(join(dir, entry.name))));
      }
      return runs.sort((a, b) => (a.createdAt < b.createdAt ? -1 : a.createdAt > b.createdAt ? 1 : a.runId < b.runId ? -1 : 1));
    },
  };
}
```

`core/run/workspace.js`:
```js
import { join, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { openWork } from '../store/work.js';
import { readTextOrNull } from '../util/fs.js';
import { resolvePacks } from '../pack/registry.js';
import { resolveChecks } from '../checks/check-set.js';
import { resolvePipelines, customStepChecks } from '../pipeline/resolve.js';

/** 작품 + 팩 + 검사 + 단계. 잠금 덮어쓰기를 포함한 설정 오류는 여기서 전부 거부된다. */
export async function openWorkspace(root, { packs }) {
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const registry = resolvePacks(work.config.packs, packs);
  const resolved = resolveChecks({ registry, config: work.config });
  const pipelines = resolvePipelines({ primary: registry.primary, overrides: work.config.overrides, checks: resolved.checks });
  const errors = [...resolved.errors, ...pipelines.errors];
  const checks = [];
  for (const check of [...resolved.checks, ...customStepChecks(pipelines)]) {
    if (!check.instructionSource) { checks.push(check); continue; }
    const path = resolve(root, check.instructionSource);
    if (!path.startsWith(resolve(root) + sep)) { errors.push({ code: 'USER_CHECK_SOURCE_INVALID', checkId: check.id }); continue; }
    const text = await readTextOrNull(join(path));
    if (text === null) errors.push({ code: 'USER_CHECK_SOURCE_MISSING', checkId: check.id, source: check.instructionSource });
    checks.push({ ...check, instructionText: text });
  }
  if (errors.length) throw new FolioError('CONFIG_INVALID', errors.map((e) => e.code).join(', '), { errors });
  return { work, registry, checks, pipelines: { leaf: pipelines.leaf, branch: pipelines.branch } };
}
```

`core/run/payload.js`:
```js
import { levelIndex } from '../work/config.js';
import { gatherScope } from '../scope/scope.js';

export function scopeFor(ctx, run) {
  return gatherScope({ work: ctx.work, events: ctx.events, nodeId: run.nodeId, draftText: run.outputs.draft ?? '', extracted: run.outputs.events ?? null });
}

/** 호스트에 넘기는 공통 재료. 검토 결과(advisory)는 넣지 않는다 — 참고 의견은 자동 수정 사유가 아니다. */
export function basePayload(ctx, run, step, scope = scopeFor(ctx, run)) {
  const { work } = ctx;
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(run.nodeId);
  const index = levelIndex(work.config, node.level);
  const next = work.config.levels[index + 1] ?? null;
  return {
    step: step.id, produces: step.produces ?? null, prompt: step.prompt ?? null, mode: run.mode,
    node: { id: node.id, level: node.level, kind: node.kind, role: node.role, title: node.title, brief: node.data.brief ?? null },
    plan: run.outputs.plan ?? null,
    draft: run.outputs.draft ?? null,
    upperPlans: scope.upperPlans.map(({ nodeId, level, text }) => ({ nodeId, level, text })),
    entities: scope.entityIds.map((id) => {
      const entity = work.entities.get(id);
      return { id, kind: entity.kind, name: entity.name, aliases: entity.aliases, body: entity.body };
    }),
    priorEvents: scope.priorEvents.map(({ eventId, nodeId, entityId, kind, data }) => ({ eventId, nodeId, entityId, kind, data: data ?? null })),
    children: node.children.map((id) => {
      const child = work.tree.nodes.get(id);
      return { id, title: child.title, status: child.status, body: child.body };
    }),
    childLevel: next ? { name: next.name, leaf: next.leaf, range: work.config.levels[index].children } : null,
  };
}

export function packPayload(ctx, run, step, extra = {}) {
  const base = { ...basePayload(ctx, run, step), ...extra };
  return ctx.registry.primary.prompt ? ctx.registry.primary.prompt(step, base) : base;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 구현 — 단계 처리기**

`core/run/steps/common.js`:
```js
import { isBlocking } from '../../ledger/receipts.js';

export function skip(run, step) {
  run.results[step.id] = { status: 'skipped', reason: step.reason || '(no reason given)', source: step.source ?? 'pack' };
  run.cursor += 1;
  return null;
}

export function outcomeFromPending(run) {
  if (run.pending?.kind === 'model') return { status: 'needs_model', runId: run.runId, nodeId: run.nodeId, requests: run.pending.requests };
  if (run.pending?.kind === 'user') return { status: 'needs_user', runId: run.runId, nodeId: run.nodeId, question: run.pending.question };
  return null;
}

/** 엔진은 모델을 부르지 않는다. 요청을 기록하고 호스트에 돌려줄 뿐이다. */
export function requestModel(run, env, requests) {
  run.pending = { kind: 'model', requests: requests.map((request) => ({ requestId: env.newId('req'), ...request })) };
  run.state = 'awaiting_model';
  return outcomeFromPending(run);
}

export function awaitUser(run, question) {
  run.pending = { kind: 'user', question };
  run.state = 'awaiting_user';
  return outcomeFromPending(run);
}

export function takeAnswer(run, stepId) {
  const entry = run.inbox?.[stepId];
  if (entry === undefined) return undefined;
  delete run.inbox[stepId];
  return entry;
}

export function collectFindings(run) {
  return Object.values(run.results).flatMap((r) => (r.checks ?? []).flatMap((c) => c.findings ?? []));
}

export const blockingFindings = (run) => collectFindings(run).filter(isBlocking);

/** 원고가 바뀌면 그 뒤 검사·승인은 무효다(hash 잠금). 자식 생성 결과만 남긴다. */
export function invalidateFrom(run, steps, index) {
  for (const step of steps.slice(index)) {
    if (step.kind === 'commit' || step.produces === 'children') continue;
    delete run.results[step.id];
  }
  run.approval = null;
}

/** 다시 검사할 때 시작점: 첫 검사 단계, 그 앞에 사건 추출 단계가 붙어 있으면 거기부터. */
export function recheckStart(steps) {
  let index = steps.findIndex((s) => s.kind === 'check');
  while (index > 0 && steps[index - 1].kind === 'generate' && steps[index - 1].produces === 'events') index -= 1;
  return index;
}
```

`core/run/steps/generate.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../../errors.js';
import { sha256 } from '../../util/hash.js';
import { parseJsonAnswer } from '../answers.js';
import { packPayload } from '../payload.js';
import { skip, requestModel, takeAnswer } from './common.js';

export function setDraft(run, text) {
  run.outputs.draft = text;
  run.draftHash = sha256(text);
}

function parseEvents(answer, work, draft) {
  let data;
  try { data = parseJsonAnswer(answer); } catch { return { error: 'EVENTS_JSON_INVALID' }; }
  if (!data || (data.events !== undefined && !Array.isArray(data.events))) return { error: 'EVENTS_SHAPE_INVALID' };
  const events = [];
  for (const event of data.events ?? []) {
    if (!work.entities.has(event?.entityId) || typeof event?.kind !== 'string' || !event.kind) return { error: `EVENT_INVALID:${event?.entityId ?? '?'}` };
    if (event.quote !== undefined && (typeof event.quote !== 'string' || !draft.includes(event.quote))) return { error: `EVENT_QUOTE_NOT_IN_DRAFT:${event.entityId}` };
    events.push({ entityId: event.entityId, kind: event.kind, ...(event.data !== undefined ? { data: event.data } : {}), ...(event.quote ? { quote: event.quote } : {}) });
  }
  const ids = (list) => (Array.isArray(list) ? list.filter((id) => work.entities.has(id)) : []);
  return { value: { events, appearances: ids(data.appearances), mentions: ids(data.mentions) } };
}

async function childrenStep() {
  throw new FolioError('CHILDREN_UNSUPPORTED', 'branch children are implemented in Task 18');
}

export async function generateStep({ ctx, run, step, env }) {
  const done = run.results[step.id]?.status;
  if (done === 'passed' || done === 'skipped') { run.cursor += 1; return null; }
  if (step.mode === 'off') return skip(run, step);
  if (step.produces === 'children') return childrenStep({ ctx, run, step, env });
  const request = (extra) => requestModel(run, env, [{ stepId: step.id, purpose: 'generate', produces: step.produces, payload: packPayload(ctx, run, step, extra) }]);
  const entry = takeAnswer(run, step.id);
  if (entry === undefined) return request();
  const text = typeof entry.answer === 'string' ? entry.answer : JSON.stringify(entry.answer);
  if (step.produces === 'plan') {
    run.outputs.plan = text;
    if (run.nodeKind === 'branch') setDraft(run, text);
  } else if (step.produces === 'draft') {
    setDraft(run, text);
  } else if (step.produces === 'events') {
    const parsed = parseEvents(entry.answer, ctx.work, run.outputs.draft ?? '');
    if (parsed.error) return request({ retryReason: parsed.error });
    run.outputs.events = { ...parsed.value, draftHash: run.draftHash };
  } else {
    throw new FolioError('STEP_PRODUCES_UNKNOWN', `${step.id}: ${step.produces}`);
  }
  run.results[step.id] = { status: 'passed' };
  run.cursor += 1;
  return null;
}
```

`core/run/steps/check.js`:
```js
import { isBlocking } from '../../ledger/receipts.js';
import { applicability, runDeterministicChecks, buildModelCheckRequest, parseModelCheckAnswer } from '../../checks/run-checks.js';
import { basePayload, scopeFor } from '../payload.js';
import { skip, requestModel, takeAnswer } from './common.js';

function checkContext(ctx, run, scope) {
  return {
    ...scope, work: ctx.work, node: ctx.work.tree.nodes.get(run.nodeId), draft: run.outputs.draft ?? '',
    draftEvents: (run.outputs.events?.events ?? []).map((e, i) => ({ ...e, eventId: `draft-${i + 1}`, nodeId: run.nodeId })),
  };
}

function stepStatus(results) {
  if (results.every((r) => r.status === 'not_applicable')) return 'not_applicable';
  return results.some((r) => r.findings.some(isBlocking)) ? 'failed' : 'passed';
}

export async function checkStep({ ctx, run, step, env }) {
  const stage = step.stage ?? step.id;
  const checks = ctx.checks.filter((c) => c.stage === stage);
  if (step.mode === 'off') {
    skip(run, step);
    run.results[step.id].checks = checks.map((c) => ({ checkId: c.id, status: 'skipped', findings: [] }));
    return null;
  }
  const scope = scopeFor(ctx, run);
  const checkCtx = checkContext(ctx, run, scope);
  const record = (results) => {
    run.results[step.id] = { status: stepStatus(results), draftHash: run.draftHash, checks: results, refs: scope.refs };
    run.cursor += 1;
    return null;
  };
  if (step.tier === 'deterministic') return record(runDeterministicChecks(checks, checkCtx));
  const applicable = checks.filter((c) => applicability(c, checkCtx) === 'applicable');
  const notApplicable = checks.filter((c) => !applicable.includes(c)).map((c) => ({ checkId: c.id, status: 'not_applicable', findings: [] }));
  if (applicable.length === 0) return record(notApplicable);
  const entry = takeAnswer(run, step.id);
  if (entry !== undefined) return record([...parseModelCheckAnswer(applicable, entry.answer, checkCtx.draft), ...notApplicable]);
  return requestModel(run, env, [{
    stepId: step.id, purpose: 'check',
    payload: { ...basePayload(ctx, run, step, scope), ...buildModelCheckRequest(applicable, checkCtx) },
  }]);
}
```

`core/run/steps/revise.js`:
```js
import { isBlocking } from '../../ledger/receipts.js';
import { packPayload } from '../payload.js';
import { setDraft } from './generate.js';
import { skip, requestModel, awaitUser, takeAnswer, collectFindings, invalidateFrom, recheckStart } from './common.js';

/**
 * 필요한 최소 수정. 막는 지적(hard, block)만 넘긴다. 참고 의견은 절대 넘기지 않는다.
 * 최대 횟수를 넘기면 사람에게 넘기고, hard 가 남아 있으면 승인도 막는다.
 */
export async function reviseStep({ ctx, run, step, steps, env }) {
  const start = recheckStart(steps);
  const reviseIndex = steps.indexOf(step);
  const blocking = steps.slice(start, reviseIndex).filter((s) => s.kind === 'check')
    .flatMap((s) => (run.results[s.id]?.checks ?? []).flatMap((c) => c.findings.filter(isBlocking)));
  const entry = takeAnswer(run, step.id);
  if (entry !== undefined) {
    setDraft(run, typeof entry.answer === 'string' ? entry.answer : JSON.stringify(entry.answer));
    if (entry.purpose === 'user_revision') { run.attempts = 0; delete run.userRevision; } else run.attempts += 1;
    run.history.push({ at: env.now(), event: entry.purpose, attempts: run.attempts });
    invalidateFrom(run, steps, start);
    run.cursor = start;
    return null;
  }
  if (run.userRevision) {
    return requestModel(run, env, [{ stepId: step.id, purpose: 'user_revision', payload: packPayload(ctx, run, step, { feedback: run.userRevision }) }]);
  }
  if (blocking.length === 0) {
    if (step.mode === 'off') return skip(run, step);
    run.results[step.id] = { status: run.attempts > 0 ? 'passed' : 'not_needed' };
    run.cursor += 1;
    return null;
  }
  const max = step.mode === 'off' ? 0 : step.loop.max;
  if (run.attempts >= max) {
    return awaitUser(run, {
      kind: 'blocked', stepId: step.id, draft: run.outputs.draft ?? '', blocking,
      advisories: collectFindings(run).filter((f) => !isBlocking(f)),
      canApprove: !blocking.some((f) => f.severity === 'hard'), attempts: run.attempts,
    });
  }
  return requestModel(run, env, [{ stepId: step.id, purpose: 'revise', payload: packPayload(ctx, run, step, { blocking }) }]);
}
```

`core/run/steps/gate.js`:
```js
import { isBlocking } from '../../ledger/receipts.js';
import { skip, awaitUser, collectFindings } from './common.js';

export async function gateStep({ run, step }) {
  if (step.mode === 'off') return skip(run, step);
  if (step.mode === 'auto') {
    run.results[step.id] = { status: 'auto', source: step.source ?? 'pack' };
    run.cursor += 1;
    return null;
  }
  if (run.approval?.stepId === step.id && run.approval.draftHash === run.draftHash) {
    run.results[step.id] = { status: 'approved', source: 'user' };
    run.cursor += 1;
    return null;
  }
  return awaitUser(run, {
    kind: 'approve', stepId: step.id, draft: run.outputs.draft ?? '',
    advisories: collectFindings(run).filter((f) => !isBlocking(f)), blocking: [],
  });
}
```

`core/run/steps/commit-step.js`:
```js
import { canonicalJson } from '../../util/hash.js';
import { commitNode } from '../commit.js';
import { buildReceipt } from '../receipt-builder.js';
import { scopeFor } from '../payload.js';
import { invalidateFrom, recheckStart } from './common.js';

/**
 * 커밋 직전에 참고한 버전 목록을 다시 계산한다. 검사 뒤 설정·사건이 바뀌었으면
 * 검사부터 다시 돈다(vibelore STALE_WORK_CONTRACT 와 같은 원칙).
 */
export async function commitStep({ ctx, run, step, steps, pipeline, env }) {
  const text = run.outputs.draft ?? '';
  if (run.state !== 'committing') {
    const scope = scopeFor(ctx, run);
    const checkedRefs = steps.filter((s) => s.kind === 'check' && run.results[s.id]?.refs).map((s) => run.results[s.id].refs);
    if (checkedRefs.some((refs) => canonicalJson(refs) !== canonicalJson(scope.refs))) {
      const start = recheckStart(steps);
      invalidateFrom(run, steps, start);
      run.cursor = start;
      run.history.push({ at: env.now(), event: 'refs_changed' });
      return null;
    }
    run.receipt = buildReceipt({ run, steps, work: ctx.work, refs: scope.refs, newId: env.newId });
    run.state = 'committing';
    await env.runs.save(run);
  }
  const result = await commitNode({ root: env.root, work: ctx.work, ledgers: env.ledgers, pipeline, receipt: run.receipt, text, hooks: env.hooks, resume: true });
  run.results[step.id] = { status: 'passed' };
  run.state = 'done';
  run.pending = null;
  return { status: 'done', runId: run.runId, nodeId: run.nodeId, receiptId: result.receiptId };
}
```

`core/run/next.js`:
```js
import { preorder } from '../store/tree.js';

function latestRunByNode(runs) {
  const latest = new Map();
  for (const run of runs) latest.set(run.nodeId, run);
  return latest;
}

/** 다음 노드: 커밋 안 된 잎, 자식 없는 가지(큰 틀부터), 자식이 다 끝난 가지(돌아보기·묶기). */
export function pickNext(work, runs = []) {
  const latest = latestRunByNode(runs);
  for (const id of preorder(work.tree)) {
    const node = work.tree.nodes.get(id);
    if (node.status === 'committed' || latest.get(id)?.state === 'abandoned') continue;
    if (node.kind === 'leaf') return node;
    const children = node.children.map((childId) => work.tree.nodes.get(childId));
    if (children.length === 0 || children.every((child) => child.status === 'committed')) return node;
  }
  return null;
}
```

`core/run/runner.js`:
```js
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { openLedger } from '../ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from '../ledger/events.js';
import { openWorkspace } from './workspace.js';
import { createRunStore, OPEN_STATES } from './run-store.js';
import { pickNext } from './next.js';
import { generateStep } from './steps/generate.js';
import { reviseStep } from './steps/revise.js';
import { checkStep } from './steps/check.js';
import { gateStep } from './steps/gate.js';
import { commitStep } from './steps/commit-step.js';
import { outcomeFromPending, invalidateFrom, recheckStart } from './steps/common.js';

const HANDLERS = { generate: generateStep, check: checkStep, gate: gateStep, commit: commitStep };
const defaultId = (prefix) => `${prefix}-${randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 12)}`;

/**
 * 순서를 지키는 실행기. 모델은 부르지 않고 needs_model/needs_user 를 돌려준다.
 * 상태는 .folio/runs 에만 있다. 매 호출 새 객체여도 같은 결과가 나온다.
 */
export function createRunner({ root, packs, now = () => new Date().toISOString(), newId = defaultId, hooks = {} }) {
  const runs = createRunStore(root);
  const ledgers = { receipts: openLedger(root, 'receipts', { now }), events: openLedger(root, 'events', { now }) };
  const env = { root, now, newId, hooks, ledgers, runs };

  async function context() {
    const workspace = await openWorkspace(root, { packs });
    const receiptRecords = await ledgers.receipts.readAll();
    const events = activeEvents(await ledgers.events.readAll());
    return { ...workspace, receiptRecords, events };
  }

  function syncPipeline(run, pipeline) {
    if (run.pipelineDigest === pipeline.digest || run.state === 'committing') return;
    const start = recheckStart(pipeline.steps);
    run.pipelineDigest = pipeline.digest;
    if (run.cursor > start) { invalidateFrom(run, pipeline.steps, start); run.cursor = start; }
    run.history.push({ at: now(), event: 'pipeline_changed' });
  }

  async function drive(run) {
    for (;;) {
      const ctx = await context();
      const pipeline = ctx.pipelines[run.nodeKind];
      syncPipeline(run, pipeline);
      const step = pipeline.steps[run.cursor];
      if (!step) throw new FolioError('RUN_CURSOR_INVALID', `${run.runId} cursor ${run.cursor}`);
      const handler = step.loop ? reviseStep : HANDLERS[step.kind];
      const outcome = await handler({ ctx, run, step, steps: pipeline.steps, pipeline, env });
      run.updatedAt = now();
      await runs.save(run);
      if (outcome) return outcome;
    }
  }

  const settle = async (outcome) => (outcome.status === 'waiting_children' ? run({ node: 'next' }) : outcome);

  function newRun(ctx, node) {
    const at = now();
    return {
      runId: newId('run'), nodeId: node.id, variant: 'main', nodeKind: node.kind,
      mode: node.kind === 'branch' && node.children.length > 0 ? 'derive' : 'open',
      pipelineDigest: ctx.pipelines[node.kind].digest, cursor: 0, state: 'active',
      outputs: {}, draftHash: null, results: {}, attempts: 0, inbox: {}, pending: null,
      approval: null, overridden: [], receipt: null, createdAt: at, updatedAt: at, history: [],
    };
  }

  async function run({ node = 'next' } = {}) {
    const all = await runs.list();
    const open = all.find((r) => OPEN_STATES.has(r.state));
    const ctx = await context();
    if (open) {
      if (node !== 'next' && node !== open.nodeId) throw new FolioError('RUN_IN_PROGRESS', `${open.runId} for ${open.nodeId} is not finished`, { runId: open.runId });
      return outcomeFromPending(open) ?? settle(await drive(open));
    }
    const target = node === 'next' ? pickNext(ctx.work, all) : ctx.work.tree.nodes.get(node);
    if (!target) {
      if (node === 'next') return { status: 'idle' };
      throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', node);
    }
    const waiting = all.find((r) => r.state === 'waiting_children' && r.nodeId === target.id);
    if (waiting && !target.children.every((id) => ctx.work.tree.nodes.get(id).status === 'committed')) {
      throw new FolioError('CHILDREN_PENDING', `${target.id} still has unfinished children`);
    }
    const current = waiting ?? newRun(ctx, target);
    current.state = 'active';
    return settle(await drive(current));
  }

  async function resume({ runId, answers = {} }) {
    const current = await runs.load(runId);
    if (current.state !== 'awaiting_model') throw new FolioError('RUN_NOT_AWAITING_MODEL', runId, { state: current.state });
    const requests = current.pending.requests;
    const missing = requests.filter((r) => !(r.requestId in answers)).map((r) => r.requestId);
    if (missing.length) throw new FolioError('ANSWERS_INCOMPLETE', missing.join(', '), { missing });
    const unknown = Object.keys(answers).filter((id) => !requests.some((r) => r.requestId === id));
    if (unknown.length) throw new FolioError('ANSWER_UNKNOWN_REQUEST', unknown.join(', '), { unknown });
    current.inbox = {};
    for (const request of requests) current.inbox[request.stepId] = { purpose: request.purpose, answer: answers[request.requestId] };
    current.pending = null;
    current.state = 'active';
    await runs.save(current);
    return settle(await drive(current));
  }

  async function decide({ runId, action, feedback = '', reason = '' }) {
    const current = await runs.load(runId);
    if (current.state !== 'awaiting_user') throw new FolioError('RUN_NOT_AWAITING_USER', runId, { state: current.state });
    const question = current.pending.question;
    const steps = (await context()).pipelines[current.nodeKind].steps;
    const why = String(reason || feedback).trim();
    if (action === 'approve') {
      if (question.kind === 'blocked') {
        if (!question.canApprove) throw new FolioError('APPROVE_BLOCKED_BY_HARD', 'hard conflicts cannot be approved; revise or skip');
        if (!why) throw new FolioError('OVERRIDE_REASON_REQUIRED', 'overriding a block needs a reason');
        current.overridden = question.blocking.map((finding) => ({ ...finding, overrideReason: why }));
        current.results[question.stepId] = { status: 'overridden', reason: why, source: 'user' };
        current.cursor = steps.findIndex((s) => s.id === question.stepId) + 1;
        const gate = steps.slice(current.cursor).find((s) => s.kind === 'gate');
        if (gate) current.approval = { stepId: gate.id, draftHash: current.draftHash, at: now(), feedback };
      } else {
        current.approval = { stepId: question.stepId, draftHash: current.draftHash, at: now(), feedback };
      }
    } else if (action === 'request_revision') {
      if (!String(feedback).trim()) throw new FolioError('FEEDBACK_REQUIRED', 'a revision request needs feedback');
      const revise = steps.find((s) => s.loop);
      if (!revise) throw new FolioError('REVISION_UNAVAILABLE', 'this pipeline has no revise step');
      current.userRevision = String(feedback).trim();
      current.cursor = steps.indexOf(revise);
      current.approval = null;
    } else if (action === 'skip') {
      if (!String(reason).trim()) throw new FolioError('SKIP_REASON_REQUIRED', 'skipping needs a reason');
      current.state = 'abandoned';
      current.pending = null;
      current.history.push({ at: now(), event: 'skipped', reason: String(reason).trim() });
      await runs.save(current);
      return { status: 'skipped', runId, nodeId: current.nodeId, reason: String(reason).trim() };
    } else {
      throw new FolioError('ACTION_INVALID', String(action));
    }
    current.pending = null;
    current.state = 'active';
    await runs.save(current);
    return settle(await drive(current));
  }

  return { run, resume, decide };
}
```

### Task 18: 구조 바꾸기(추가·이동·묶기)와 위층 재귀 — 큰 틀부터 / 바로 쓰기

**Files:**
- Create: `core/structure/naming.js`, `core/structure/ops.js`, `core/run/steps/children.js`
- Modify: `core/run/steps/generate.js` (임시 `childrenStep` 제거, `./children.js`에서 import)
- Test: `test/core/structure.test.js`, `test/run/hierarchy.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Work`, `levelIndex`, `serializeDoc`, `writeFileAtomic`, `ancestorsOf`, `NODE_FILE`, `TREE_DIR`, `updateNode`, `openWork`, `requestModel`, `takeAnswer`, `packPayload`, `parseJsonAnswer`
- Produces:
  - `addNode(root, work, { parentId?, level, id, title?, brief?, index? })` — status `draft`로 만든다. 폴더 이름은 `NN-<id>`(잎은 `.md`), 형제 번호를 다시 매긴다.
  - `wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds, level, id, title? })` — 이웃한 형제를 새 가지로 묶는다. 오류 `STRUCTURE_NOT_SIBLINGS`, `STRUCTURE_NOT_CONTIGUOUS`, `LEVEL_KIND_MISMATCH`, `LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID`
  - `moveNode(root, work, { nodeId, parentId?, index? })` — 오류 `STRUCTURE_CYCLE`, `LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID`, `STRUCTURE_PARENT_NOT_BRANCH`
  - 이 세 함수는 파일만 바꾼다. 호출자는 `openWork`를 다시 불러 새 위치를 얻는다(읽는 순서 위치는 매번 다시 계산됨).
  - `baseName(name)`, `entryName(index, base, width)`, `renumber(dir, entries: {name, base}[])`
  - `childrenStep` — open 모드: 자식 계획 요청 → 자식 id를 실행 파일에 먼저 고정 → 부모 `_node.md`에 계획을 쓰고 `status: planned` → 자식 노드 생성 → `waiting_children`. derive 모드(이미 자식이 있음): `skipped`, reason `children already exist`, source `core`.

`test/core/structure.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { compareNodes } from '../../core/store/position.js';
import { addNode, wrapNodes, moveNode } from '../../core/structure/ops.js';
import { copyFixture, emptyTreeCopy } from '../support/fixtures.js';

async function scenes(root, ids) {
  for (const id of ids) await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'scene', id, title: id });
  return openWork(root);
}

test('addNode creates branches and leaves in order and renumbers siblings', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'book', id: 'b1', title: '1권' });
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'book', id: 'b0', title: '0권', index: 0 });
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { parentId: 'b1', level: 'chapter', id: 'c1' });
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { parentId: 'c1', level: 'scene', id: 's1', brief: '짧은 계획' });
  const work = await openWork(root);
  assert.deepEqual(work.tree.rootIds, ['b0', 'b1']);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('b1').path, '02-b1/_node.md');
  const s1 = work.tree.nodes.get('s1');
  assert.deepEqual([s1.kind, s1.path, s1.status, s1.data.brief], ['leaf', '02-b1/01-c1/01-s1.md', 'draft', '짧은 계획']);
});

test('addNode refuses bad ids, duplicates, wrong depth and leaf parents', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'book', id: 'b1' });
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { parentId: 'b1', level: 'chapter', id: 'c1' });
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { parentId: 'c1', level: 'scene', id: 's1' });
  const work = await openWork(root);
  const tries = [
    [{ level: 'book', id: 'Bad Id' }, 'NODE_ID_INVALID'],
    [{ level: 'book', id: 'b1' }, 'DUPLICATE_ID'],
    [{ parentId: 'b1', level: 'book', id: 'bx' }, 'LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID'],
    [{ parentId: 's1', level: 'scene', id: 'sx' }, 'STRUCTURE_PARENT_NOT_BRANCH'],
    [{ level: 'ghost', id: 'gx' }, 'LEVEL_UNKNOWN'],
  ];
  for (const [input, code] of tries) await assert.rejects(addNode(root, work, input), { code });
});

test('wrapNodes groups neighbouring scenes under a new chapter, then a book', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await scenes(root, ['s1', 's2', 's3', 's4']);
  await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['s1', 's2'], level: 'chapter', id: 'c1', title: '첫 장' });
  await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['c1'], level: 'book', id: 'b1' });
  const work = await openWork(root);
  assert.deepEqual(work.tree.rootIds, ['b1', 's3', 's4']);
  assert.deepEqual(work.tree.nodes.get('c1').children, ['s1', 's2']);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('s2').path, '01-b1/01-c1/02-s2.md');
  assert.equal(compareNodes(work.positions, 's2', 's3'), 'before');
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('c1').title, '첫 장');
});

test('wrapNodes refuses gaps, mixed parents and wrong levels', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  let work = await scenes(root, ['s1', 's2', 's3']);
  await assert.rejects(wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds: ['s1', 's3'], level: 'chapter', id: 'cx' }), { code: 'STRUCTURE_NOT_CONTIGUOUS' });
  await assert.rejects(wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds: ['s1'], level: 'scene', id: 'cx' }), { code: 'LEVEL_KIND_MISMATCH' });
  await wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds: ['s1'], level: 'chapter', id: 'c1' });
  work = await openWork(root);
  await assert.rejects(wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds: ['s1', 's2'], level: 'chapter', id: 'cy' }), { code: 'STRUCTURE_NOT_SIBLINGS' });
  await assert.rejects(wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds: ['c1'], level: 'chapter', id: 'cz' }), { code: 'LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID' });
});

test('moveNode keeps ids and bodies and refuses cycles', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  const body = (await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.get('market').body;
  await moveNode(root, await openWork(root), { nodeId: 'market', parentId: 'the-fall', index: 0 });
  const work = await openWork(root);
  assert.deepEqual(work.tree.nodes.get('the-fall').children, ['market', 'rooftop-scene', 'stairwell']);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('market').path, '01-book-one/01-the-fall/01-market.md');
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('market').body, body);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('funeral').path, '02-book-two/01-after/01-funeral.md');
  await assert.rejects(moveNode(root, work, { nodeId: 'book-one', parentId: 'the-fall' }), { code: 'STRUCTURE_CYCLE' });
});
```

`test/run/hierarchy.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { updateNode } from '../../core/store/tree.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { addNode, wrapNodes } from '../../core/structure/ops.js';
import { pickNext } from '../../core/run/next.js';
import { createRunner } from '../../core/run/runner.js';
import { copyFixture, emptyTreeCopy, useFakePack, fakePacks } from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { createFakeHost, driveToEnd, label } from '../support/fake-host.js';

const doneOrder = (trail) => trail.filter((o) => o.status === 'done').map((o) => o.nodeId);
const receiptFor = async (root, nodeId) => (await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'receipt' && r.nodeId === nodeId).at(-1);
const statusOf = (receipt, id) => receipt.steps.find((s) => s.id === id);

test('pickNext descends into unfinished branches and returns finished ones', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  let work = await openWork(root);
  assert.equal(pickNext(work).id, 'rooftop-scene');
  assert.equal(pickNext(work, [{ nodeId: 'rooftop-scene', state: 'abandoned', createdAt: '1' }]).id, 'stairwell');
  for (const id of ['rooftop-scene', 'stairwell']) await updateNode(root, work.tree.nodes.get(id), { data: { status: 'committed' } });
  work = await openWork(root);
  assert.equal(pickNext(work).id, 'the-fall');
  const empty = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await addNode(empty, await openWork(empty), { level: 'book', id: 'b1' });
  assert.equal(pickNext(await openWork(empty)).id, 'b1');
});

test('top-down: a book plans its chapters, waits for them, then reviews and commits', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(root);
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'book', id: 'b1', title: '1권' });
  const host = createFakeHost({ children: { b1: [{ id: 'b1-c1', title: '가' }], 'b1-c1': [{ id: 'b1-c1-s1' }, { id: 'b1-c1-s2' }] } });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(() => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() }), host);
  assert.equal(trail.at(-1).status, 'idle');
  assert.deepEqual(doneOrder(trail), ['b1-c1-s1', 'b1-c1-s2', 'b1-c1', 'b1']);
  assert.deepEqual(trail.slice(0, 2).map(label), ['model:plan', 'user:approve']);
  const work = await openWork(root);
  for (const node of work.tree.nodes.values()) assert.equal(node.status, 'committed', node.id);
  assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.get('b1').body, '1권 계획');
  const book = await receiptFor(root, 'b1');
  assert.deepEqual(['fit', 'children', 'review'].map((id) => statusOf(book, id).status), ['not_applicable', 'passed', 'passed']);
  assert.equal(statusOf(await receiptFor(root, 'b1-c1'), 'fit').status, 'passed');
  const review = host.calls.find((c) => c.stepId === 'review' && c.nodeId === 'b1');
  assert.deepEqual(review.payload.children.map((c) => c.status), ['committed']);
});

test('bottom-up: scenes first, then wrap, then plans derived from the children', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(root);
  for (const id of ['s1', 's2']) await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'scene', id, title: id });
  const makeRunner = () => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() });
  const host = createFakeHost();
  assert.deepEqual(doneOrder(await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host)), ['s1', 's2']);
  await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['s1', 's2'], level: 'chapter', id: 'c1' });
  assert.deepEqual(doneOrder(await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host)), ['c1']);
  const chapter = await receiptFor(root, 'c1');
  assert.deepEqual(statusOf(chapter, 'children'), { id: 'children', kind: 'generate', status: 'skipped', reason: 'children already exist', source: 'core' });
  const plan = host.calls.find((c) => c.nodeId === 'c1' && c.stepId === 'plan');
  assert.equal(plan.payload.mode, 'derive');
  assert.deepEqual(plan.payload.children.map((c) => c.body), ['s1 본문', 's2 본문']);
  assert.deepEqual(await makeRunner().run({ node: 'next' }), { status: 'idle' });
});

test('a planned branch cannot be closed before its children finish', async () => {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(root);
  await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: 'book', id: 'b1' });
  const makeRunner = () => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() });
  const host = createFakeHost({ children: { b1: [{ id: 'b1-c1' }] } });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, host, { until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' && o.nodeId === 'b1-c1' });
  await makeRunner().decide({ runId: trail.at(-1).runId, action: 'skip', reason: '나중에' });
  await assert.rejects(makeRunner().run({ node: 'b1' }), { code: 'CHILDREN_PENDING' });
  assert.deepEqual(await makeRunner().run({ node: 'next' }), { status: 'idle' });
});
```

`core/structure/naming.js`:
```js
import { rename } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const PREFIX = /^\d+-/;

export const baseName = (name) => name.replace(PREFIX, '');
export const entryName = (index, base, width = 2) => `${String(index + 1).padStart(width, '0')}-${base}`;

/**
 * 형제 항목 이름의 번호를 순서대로 다시 매긴다. 이름이 겹치지 않게 임시 이름을 거친다.
 * 임시 이름은 '~'로 시작해 숨겨지지 않으므로, 중간에 죽어도 노드를 잃지 않는다(순서만 흐트러짐).
 */
export async function renumber(dir, entries) {
  const width = Math.max(2, String(entries.length).length);
  const plan = entries.map((entry, index) => ({ from: entry.name, to: entryName(index, entry.base, width) })).filter((p) => p.from !== p.to);
  for (const p of plan) await rename(join(dir, p.from), join(dir, `~renumber-${p.from}`));
  for (const p of plan) await rename(join(dir, `~renumber-${p.from}`), join(dir, p.to));
}
```

`core/structure/ops.js`:
```js
import { mkdir, rename } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { FolioError } from '../errors.js';
import { levelIndex } from '../work/config.js';
import { NODE_FILE, TREE_DIR, ancestorsOf } from '../store/tree.js';
import { serializeDoc } from '../store/frontmatter.js';
import { writeFileAtomic } from '../util/fs.js';
import { baseName, renumber } from './naming.js';

const ID = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/;

/** 노드가 부모 폴더 안에서 차지하는 항목(잎은 파일, 가지는 폴더). */
export const entryOf = (node) => (node.kind === 'leaf' ? basename(node.path) : basename(dirname(node.path)));
const containerDir = (root, parent) => (parent ? join(root, TREE_DIR, dirname(parent.path)) : join(root, TREE_DIR));
const siblingsOf = (work, parentId) => (parentId ? work.tree.nodes.get(parentId).children : work.tree.rootIds).map((id) => work.tree.nodes.get(id));
const entries = (nodes) => nodes.map((n) => ({ name: entryOf(n), base: baseName(entryOf(n)) }));

function assertNewId(work, id) {
  if (typeof id !== 'string' || !ID.test(id)) throw new FolioError('NODE_ID_INVALID', String(id));
  if (work.tree.nodes.has(id)) throw new FolioError('DUPLICATE_ID', id);
}

function levelOf(work, level) {
  const index = levelIndex(work.config, level);
  if (index < 0) throw new FolioError('LEVEL_UNKNOWN', String(level));
  return { index, ...work.config.levels[index] };
}

function assertDeeper(work, parentLevel, childLevel) {
  if (parentLevel === null) return;
  if (levelIndex(work.config, parentLevel) >= levelIndex(work.config, childLevel)) {
    throw new FolioError('LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID', `${childLevel} cannot sit under ${parentLevel}`);
  }
}

function branchOrNull(work, id) {
  if (!id) return null;
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(id);
  if (!node) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', id);
  if (node.kind !== 'branch') throw new FolioError('STRUCTURE_PARENT_NOT_BRANCH', id);
  return node;
}

const nodeDoc = ({ id, level, title, brief }) => serializeDoc({
  data: { id, level, ...(title ? { title } : {}), status: 'draft', ...(brief ? { brief } : {}) }, body: '',
});

export async function addNode(root, work, { parentId = null, level, id, title = '', brief = null, index = null }) {
  assertNewId(work, id);
  const info = levelOf(work, level);
  const parent = branchOrNull(work, parentId);
  assertDeeper(work, parent?.level ?? null, level);
  const dir = containerDir(root, parent);
  const base = info.leaf ? `${id}.md` : id;
  const temp = `~add-${base}`;
  await writeFileAtomic(info.leaf ? join(dir, temp) : join(dir, temp, NODE_FILE), nodeDoc({ id, level, title, brief }));
  const list = entries(siblingsOf(work, parentId));
  const at = index === null ? list.length : Math.max(0, Math.min(index, list.length));
  list.splice(at, 0, { name: temp, base });
  await renumber(dir, list);
}

export async function wrapNodes(root, work, { nodeIds, level, id, title = '' }) {
  assertNewId(work, id);
  if (!nodeIds?.length) throw new FolioError('STRUCTURE_EMPTY', 'nothing to wrap');
  const nodes = nodeIds.map((nodeId) => {
    const node = work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!node) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', nodeId);
    return node;
  });
  const parentId = nodes[0].parentId;
  if (nodes.some((n) => n.parentId !== parentId)) throw new FolioError('STRUCTURE_NOT_SIBLINGS', nodeIds.join(','));
  const siblings = siblingsOf(work, parentId);
  const indexes = nodes.map((n) => siblings.indexOf(n)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (indexes.some((value, i) => i > 0 && value !== indexes[i - 1] + 1)) throw new FolioError('STRUCTURE_NOT_CONTIGUOUS', nodeIds.join(','));
  const info = levelOf(work, level);
  if (info.leaf) throw new FolioError('LEVEL_KIND_MISMATCH', `${level} is a leaf level`);
  for (const node of nodes) assertDeeper(work, level, node.level);
  const parent = parentId ? work.tree.nodes.get(parentId) : null;
  assertDeeper(work, parent?.level ?? null, level);
  const dir = containerDir(root, parent);
  const temp = `~wrap-${id}`;
  await writeFileAtomic(join(dir, temp, NODE_FILE), nodeDoc({ id, level, title }));
  const wrapped = indexes.map((i) => siblings[i]);
  for (const node of wrapped) await rename(join(dir, entryOf(node)), join(dir, temp, entryOf(node)));
  await renumber(join(dir, temp), entries(wrapped));
  const list = entries(siblings);
  list.splice(indexes[0], wrapped.length, { name: temp, base: id });
  await renumber(dir, list);
}

export async function moveNode(root, work, { nodeId, parentId = null, index = null }) {
  const node = work.tree.nodes.get(nodeId);
  if (!node) throw new FolioError('NODE_NOT_FOUND', nodeId);
  const target = branchOrNull(work, parentId);
  if (target && (target.id === nodeId || ancestorsOf(work.tree, target.id).some((a) => a.id === nodeId))) {
    throw new FolioError('STRUCTURE_CYCLE', `${nodeId} cannot move inside itself`);
  }
  assertDeeper(work, target?.level ?? null, node.level);
  const fromParent = node.parentId ? work.tree.nodes.get(node.parentId) : null;
  const fromDir = containerDir(root, fromParent);
  const toDir = containerDir(root, target);
  const entry = entryOf(node);
  const temp = `~move-${baseName(entry)}`;
  await mkdir(toDir, { recursive: true });
  await rename(join(fromDir, entry), join(toDir, temp));
  const remaining = entries(siblingsOf(work, node.parentId).filter((n) => n.id !== nodeId));
  if (fromDir !== toDir) await renumber(fromDir, remaining);
  const list = fromDir === toDir ? remaining : entries(siblingsOf(work, parentId));
  const at = index === null ? list.length : Math.max(0, Math.min(index, list.length));
  list.splice(at, 0, { name: temp, base: baseName(entry) });
  await renumber(toDir, list);
}
```

`core/run/steps/children.js`:
```js
import { FolioError } from '../../errors.js';
import { levelIndex } from '../../work/config.js';
import { updateNode } from '../../store/tree.js';
import { openWork } from '../../store/work.js';
import { addNode } from '../../structure/ops.js';
import { parseJsonAnswer } from '../answers.js';
import { packPayload } from '../payload.js';
import { requestModel, takeAnswer } from './common.js';

const ID = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/;

function readChildren(answer, work, newId, childLevel) {
  let data;
  try { data = parseJsonAnswer(answer); } catch { return { error: 'CHILDREN_JSON_INVALID' }; }
  if (!Array.isArray(data?.children) || data.children.length === 0) return { error: 'CHILDREN_EMPTY' };
  const seen = new Set();
  const list = [];
  for (const child of data.children) {
    const id = child?.id ?? newId(childLevel);
    if (typeof id !== 'string' || !ID.test(id) || work.tree.nodes.has(id) || seen.has(id)) return { error: `CHILD_ID_INVALID:${id}` };
    seen.add(id);
    list.push({ id, title: typeof child.title === 'string' ? child.title : '', brief: typeof child.brief === 'string' ? child.brief : null });
  }
  return { value: list };
}

/** 위층 칸: 자식들의 짧은 계획으로 자식 노드를 만들고, 자식이 다 끝날 때까지 기다린다. */
export async function childrenStep({ ctx, run, step, env }) {
  if (run.mode === 'derive') {
    run.results[step.id] = { status: 'skipped', reason: 'children already exist', source: 'core' };
    run.cursor += 1;
    return null;
  }
  const node = ctx.work.tree.nodes.get(run.nodeId);
  const childLevel = ctx.work.config.levels[levelIndex(ctx.work.config, node.level) + 1];
  if (!childLevel) throw new FolioError('LEVEL_ORDER_INVALID', `${node.id} has no child level`);
  if (!run.outputs.children) {
    const request = (extra) => requestModel(run, env, [{ stepId: step.id, purpose: 'generate', produces: 'children', payload: packPayload(ctx, run, step, extra) }]);
    const entry = takeAnswer(run, step.id);
    if (entry === undefined) return request();
    const parsed = readChildren(entry.answer, ctx.work, env.newId, childLevel.name);
    if (parsed.error) return request({ retryReason: parsed.error });
    run.outputs.children = parsed.value;
    await env.runs.save(run); // 자식 id 를 먼저 고정해야 중간에 죽어도 같은 자식이 만들어진다
  }
  await updateNode(env.root, node, { data: { status: 'planned' }, body: run.outputs.draft ?? '' });
  let work = await openWork(env.root);
  for (const child of run.outputs.children) {
    if (work.tree.nodes.has(child.id)) continue;
    await addNode(env.root, work, { parentId: node.id, level: childLevel.name, id: child.id, title: child.title, brief: child.brief });
    work = await openWork(env.root);
  }
  run.results[step.id] = { status: 'passed' };
  run.state = 'waiting_children';
  run.cursor += 1;
  return { status: 'waiting_children', runId: run.runId, nodeId: run.nodeId };
}
```

`core/run/steps/generate.js` 수정: 임시 `childrenStep` 함수를 지우고 파일 머리에 추가한다.

### Task 19: 프로세스를 죽여도 재개

**Files:**
- Create: `test/support/child-runner.js`, `test/support/crash.js`
- Test: `test/run/resume.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createRunner`, `fakePacks`, `createFakeHost`, `driveToEnd`
- Produces: `runChild(root, scenario): { status, signal, stdout, stderr }` — 시나리오 `kill-after-first-request`(첫 needs_model을 stdout에 쓰고 SIGKILL), `kill-during-commit`(본문 파일을 쓴 직후 SIGKILL)

- [ ] **Step 1: 테스트 지원 코드 작성**

`test/support/child-runner.js`:
```js
import { writeSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createRunner } from '../../core/run/runner.js';
import { fakePacks } from './fake-pack.js';
import { createFakeHost, driveToEnd } from './fake-host.js';

const [root, scenario] = process.argv.slice(2);
const die = () => process.kill(process.pid, 'SIGKILL');

if (scenario === 'kill-after-first-request') {
  const outcome = await createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() }).run({ node: 'next' });
  writeSync(1, JSON.stringify(outcome));
  die();
} else if (scenario === 'kill-during-commit') {
  await driveToEnd(() => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks(), hooks: { afterBodyWrite: die } }), createFakeHost());
} else {
  throw new Error(`unknown scenario ${scenario}`);
}
```

`test/support/crash.js`:
```js
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const CHILD = join(import.meta.dirname, 'child-runner.js');

export function runChild(root, scenario) {
  return spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD, root, scenario], { encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 60_000 });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패하는 테스트 작성**

`test/run/resume.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createRunner } from '../../core/run/runner.js';
import { createRunStore } from '../../core/run/run-store.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { copyFixture, useFakePack, fakePacks } from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { createFakeHost, driveToEnd } from '../support/fake-host.js';
import { runChild } from '../support/crash.js';

test('a run killed while waiting for the model resumes with the same requests', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(root);
  const child = runChild(root, 'kill-after-first-request');
  assert.equal(child.signal, 'SIGKILL', child.stderr);
  const before = JSON.parse(child.stdout);
  const makeRunner = () => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() });
  assert.deepEqual(await makeRunner().run({ node: 'next' }), before);
  const trail = await driveToEnd(makeRunner, createFakeHost(), { from: before, until: (o) => o.status === 'done' });
  assert.equal(trail.at(-1).nodeId, 'rooftop-scene');
  assert.equal((await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'commit').length, 1);
});

test('a run killed in the middle of a commit finishes it exactly once', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(root);
  const child = runChild(root, 'kill-during-commit');
  assert.equal(child.signal, 'SIGKILL', child.stderr);
  const [run] = await createRunStore(root).list();
  assert.equal(run.state, 'committing');
  assert.deepEqual((await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).map((r) => r.type), ['receipt']);
  const outcome = await createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks() }).run({ node: 'next' });
  assert.deepEqual([outcome.status, outcome.nodeId, outcome.receiptId], ['done', 'rooftop-scene', run.receipt.receiptId]);
  const records = await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll();
  assert.deepEqual(records.map((r) => r.type), ['receipt', 'commit']);
  assert.deepEqual(await openLedger(root, 'receipts').verify(), { ok: true });
  const node = (await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.get('rooftop-scene');
  assert.deepEqual([node.status, node.body], ['committed', run.outputs.draft]);
});
```

`test/acceptance/m2.test.js`:
```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { openWork } from '../../core/store/work.js';
import { openLedger } from '../../core/ledger/ledger.js';
import { validateReceipt } from '../../core/ledger/receipts.js';
import { addNode, wrapNodes } from '../../core/structure/ops.js';
import { createRunner } from '../../core/run/runner.js';
import { createRunStore } from '../../core/run/run-store.js';
import { openWorkspace } from '../../core/run/workspace.js';
import { commitNode } from '../../core/run/commit.js';
import { workStatus } from '../../core/status.js';
import { copyFixture, emptyTreeCopy, seedEvents, useFakePack, fakePacks } from '../support/fixtures.js';
import { createFakeHost, driveToEnd, label } from '../support/fake-host.js';
import { makeReceipt } from '../support/receipts.js';
import { runChild } from '../support/crash.js';

const TOP = { 'novel-3level': 'book', 'knowledge-book': 'part' };
const runnerFor = (root, extra = {}) => () => createRunner({ root, packs: fakePacks(), ...extra });
const commits = async (root) => (await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'commit');
const receiptOf = async (root, nodeId) => (await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((r) => r.type === 'receipt' && r.nodeId === nodeId).at(-1);
const twoEach = (p) => [{ id: `${p.node.id}-1`, title: '가' }, { id: `${p.node.id}-2`, title: '나' }];

async function topDown(name, script = {}) {
  const root = await emptyTreeCopy(name);
  await useFakePack(root);
  for (const id of ['t1', 't2']) await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: TOP[name], id, title: id });
  const host = createFakeHost({ children: { '*': twoEach }, ...script });
  const trail = await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), host);
  return { root, host, trail };
}

for (const name of ['novel-3level', 'knowledge-book']) {
  test(`M2-1a top-down runs ${name} to the end`, async () => {
    const { root, trail } = await topDown(name);
    assert.equal(trail.at(-1).status, 'idle');
    const work = await openWork(root);
    assert.equal(work.tree.nodes.size, 14);
    for (const node of work.tree.nodes.values()) assert.equal(node.status, 'committed', node.id);
    const done = await commits(root);
    assert.equal(done.length, 14);
    assert.equal(new Set(done.map((c) => c.nodeId)).size, 14);
    assert.equal(trail.find((o) => o.status === 'needs_user').nodeId, 't1');
    for (const r of (await openLedger(root, 'receipts').readAll()).filter((x) => x.type === 'receipt')) {
      const { type: _t, seq: _s, at: _a, prev: _p, hash: _h, ...receipt } = r;
      assert.deepEqual(validateReceipt(receipt), [], r.nodeId);
    }
    assert.deepEqual(await openLedger(root, 'receipts').verify(), { ok: true });
  });

  test(`M2-1b bottom-up runs ${name} to the end`, async () => {
    const root = await emptyTreeCopy(name);
    await useFakePack(root);
    const [top, mid, leaf] = (await openWork(root)).config.levels.map((l) => l.name);
    for (const id of ['s1', 's2', 's3', 's4']) await addNode(root, await openWork(root), { level: leaf, id, title: id });
    const host = createFakeHost();
    await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), host);
    await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['s1', 's2'], level: mid, id: 'm1' });
    await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['s3', 's4'], level: mid, id: 'm2' });
    await wrapNodes(root, await openWork(root), { nodeIds: ['m1', 'm2'], level: top, id: 't1' });
    const trail = await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), host);
    assert.deepEqual(trail.filter((o) => o.status === 'done').map((o) => o.nodeId), ['m1', 'm2', 't1']);
    for (const node of (await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.values()) assert.equal(node.status, 'committed', node.id);
    for (const id of ['m1', 'm2', 't1']) {
      assert.equal((await receiptOf(root, id)).steps.find((s) => s.id === 'children').reason, 'children already exist');
    }
  });
}

test('M2-2 every lock bypass attempt fails', async (t) => {
  await t.test('locked steps and checks cannot be switched off, moved or unlocked', async () => {
    const attempts = [
      [{ overrides: { facts: { mode: 'off', reason: '빨리' } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { extract: { mode: 'off' } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { draft: { mode: 'off' } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { commit: { mode: 'off' } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { facts: { after: 'critic' } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { facts: { locked: false } } }, 'LOCKED_STEP_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ checks: { 'core.promise-order': { mode: 'off' } } }, 'LOCKED_CHECK_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ checks: { 'core.promise-order': { severity: 'advisory' } } }, 'LOCKED_CHECK_OVERRIDE'],
      [{ overrides: { revise: { max: 10 } } }, 'LOOP_MAX_EXCEEDED'],
    ];
    for (const [config, code] of attempts) {
      const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
      await useFakePack(root, config);
      await assert.rejects(openWorkspace(root, { packs: fakePacks() }), (e) => e.code === 'CONFIG_INVALID' && e.details.errors.some((x) => x.code === code), code);
    }
  });

  await t.test('commits without a receipt, with a changed text, a missing locked step, a hard conflict or a reused receipt fail', async () => {
    const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
    await useFakePack(root);
    const { work, pipelines } = await openWorkspace(root, { packs: fakePacks() });
    const ledgers = { receipts: openLedger(root, 'receipts'), events: openLedger(root, 'events') };
    const commit = (receipt, text) => commitNode({ root, work, ledgers, pipeline: pipelines.leaf, receipt, text });
    const good = () => makeReceipt({ nodeId: 'market', text: '검사한 원고', pipeline: pipelines.leaf });
    await assert.rejects(commit(null, '검사한 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_NO_RECEIPT' });
    await assert.rejects(commit(good(), '검사 뒤 바꾼 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH' });
    const noFacts = good(); noFacts.steps = noFacts.steps.filter((s) => s.id !== 'facts');
    await assert.rejects(commit(noFacts, '검사한 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_STEP_MISSING' });
    const hard = good(); hard.hard = 1;
    await assert.rejects(commit(hard, '검사한 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_HARD_VIOLATION' });
    const labelled = good(); labelled.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'critic').checks = [{ checkId: 'fake.critic', status: 'passed', findings: [], reviewer: { sameHost: true, independent: true } }];
    await assert.rejects(commit(labelled, '검사한 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_RECEIPT_INVALID' });
    const once = good();
    await commit(once, '검사한 원고');
    await assert.rejects(commit(once, '검사한 원고'), { code: 'LOCK_RECEIPT_CONSUMED' });
  });

  await t.test('changing the checked draft inside the run file cannot be committed', async () => {
    const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
    await useFakePack(root);
    const trail = await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), createFakeHost(), { until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' });
    const path = createRunStore(root).pathOf(trail.at(-1).runId);
    const run = JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));
    run.outputs.draft += ' 몰래 덧붙임';
    await writeFile(path, JSON.stringify(run));
    await assert.rejects(runnerFor(root)().decide({ runId: run.runId, action: 'approve' }), { code: 'LOCK_HASH_MISMATCH' });
    assert.deepEqual(await commits(root), []);
  });

  await t.test('hard conflicts cannot be approved; soft findings never trigger a revision', async () => {
    const root = await copyFixture('serial-2level');
    await seedEvents(root, 'serial-2level');
    await useFakePack(root);
    const host = createFakeHost({ critic: () => [{ checkId: 'fake.critic', code: 'SOFT', message: '참고' }] });
    const blocked = (await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), host, { start: { node: 'ep-004' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'needs_user' })).at(-1);
    assert.equal(blocked.question.canApprove, false);
    await assert.rejects(runnerFor(root)().decide({ runId: blocked.runId, action: 'approve', reason: '무시' }), { code: 'APPROVE_BLOCKED_BY_HARD' });
    for (const call of host.calls.filter((c) => c.purpose === 'revise')) {
      assert.deepEqual(call.payload.blocking.map((f) => f.severity), ['hard']);
      assert.equal(JSON.stringify(call.payload).includes('SOFT'), false);
    }
  });

  await t.test('an edited ledger is refused', async () => {
    const root = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
    await useFakePack(root, { overrides: { approve: { mode: 'auto' } } });
    await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), createFakeHost(), { until: (o) => o.status === 'done' });
    const path = openLedger(root, 'events').path;
    await writeFile(path, `${JSON.stringify({ type: 'event', seq: 1, prev: 'genesis', hash: 'x' })}\n`);
    await assert.rejects(runnerFor(root)().run({ node: 'next' }), { code: 'LEDGER_TAMPERED' });
  });
});

test('M2-3 runs resume after the process is killed', async () => {
  const waiting = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(waiting);
  const first = runChild(waiting, 'kill-after-first-request');
  assert.equal(first.signal, 'SIGKILL');
  assert.deepEqual(await runnerFor(waiting)().run({ node: 'next' }), JSON.parse(first.stdout));
  const committing = await copyFixture('novel-3level');
  await useFakePack(committing);
  assert.equal(runChild(committing, 'kill-during-commit').signal, 'SIGKILL');
  assert.equal((await runnerFor(committing)().run({ node: 'next' })).status, 'done');
  assert.equal((await commits(committing)).length, 1);
});

test('M2-4 fixing an event in book one marks book-two scenes using that character as needing a re-check', async () => {
  const drafts = {
    't1-1-1': '마라가 옥상에 섰다.', 't1-1-2': '준호가 기다렸다.', 't1-2-1': '마라가 편지를 읽었다.', 't1-2-2': '시장이 붐볐다.',
    't2-1-1': '준호는 마라를 떠올렸다.', 't2-1-2': '비가 왔다.', 't2-2-1': '마라의 편지가 도착했다.', 't2-2-2': '끝.',
  };
  const alive = { events: [{ entityId: 'mara', kind: 'state', data: { vital: 'alive' } }] };
  const { root } = await topDown('novel-3level', { drafts, events: { 't1-1-1': alive } });
  assert.deepEqual((await workStatus(root, { packs: fakePacks() })).stale, []);
  const dead = { events: [{ entityId: 'mara', kind: 'state', data: { vital: 'dead' } }] };
  await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), createFakeHost({ drafts, events: { 't1-1-1': dead } }), { start: { node: 't1-1-1' }, until: (o) => o.status === 'done' });
  const status = await workStatus(root, { packs: fakePacks() });
  assert.deepEqual(status.stale.map((s) => s.nodeId), ['t1-2-1', 't2-1-1', 't2-2-1']);
  for (const entry of status.stale) assert.deepEqual(entry.reasons.map((r) => `${r.kind}:${r.id}`), ['entity:mara']);
  assert.equal((await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.get('t2-1-1').body, drafts['t2-1-1']);
});

test('M2-5 a gamebook runs to the end with order checks recorded as not applicable', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('gamebook');
  await seedEvents(root, 'gamebook');
  await useFakePack(root);
  const trail = await driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), createFakeHost());
  assert.equal(trail.at(-1).status, 'idle');
  for (const node of (await openWork(root)).tree.nodes.values()) assert.equal(node.status, 'committed', node.id);
  const facts = (await receiptOf(root, 'gate')).steps.find((s) => s.id === 'facts');
  assert.equal(facts.status, 'not_applicable');
  assert.deepEqual(facts.checks.map((c) => [c.checkId, c.status]).sort(), [
    ['core.promise-order', 'not_applicable'], ['fake.dead-on-stage', 'not_applicable'], ['fake.defined-before-use', 'not_applicable'],
  ]);
});

test('M2-6 appendix nodes skip order checks; body nodes keep them', async () => {
  const root = await copyFixture('knowledge-book');
  await seedEvents(root, 'knowledge-book');
  await useFakePack(root, { overrides: { approve: { mode: 'auto' } } });
  const host = createFakeHost({
    events: {
      'atp-section': { events: [{ entityId: 'energy-detail', kind: 'promise.resolved' }] },
      membranes: { events: [{ entityId: 'atp', kind: 'term.used' }] },
    },
  });
  const one = (node, until = (o) => o.status === 'done') => driveToEnd(runnerFor(root), host, { start: { node }, until });
  await one('glossary');
  const glossary = (await receiptOf(root, 'glossary')).steps.find((s) => s.id === 'facts');
  assert.equal(glossary.status, 'not_applicable');
  await one('atp-section');
  const body = (await receiptOf(root, 'atp-section')).steps.find((s) => s.id === 'facts');
  assert.equal(body.checks.find((c) => c.checkId === 'core.promise-order').status, 'passed');
  const blocked = (await one('membranes', (o) => o.status === 'needs_user')).at(-1);
  assert.deepEqual(blocked.question.blocking.map((f) => f.code), ['USED_BEFORE_DEFINED']);
  assert.equal(blocked.question.canApprove, false);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패 확인**

Run: `node --test test/acceptance/m2.test.js`
Expected: FAIL — `Cannot find module '../../core/status.js'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현**

`core/status.js`:
```js
import { openWorkspace } from './run/workspace.js';
import { createRunStore, OPEN_STATES } from './run/run-store.js';
import { pickNext } from './run/next.js';
import { openLedger } from './ledger/ledger.js';
import { activeEvents } from './ledger/events.js';
import { findStale } from './scope/stale.js';
import { detectDrift } from './store/drift.js';

/** 지금 상태·다음 할 일·다시 확인 필요·손수정. 기록장이 깨졌으면 그것만 알린다. */
export async function workStatus(root, { packs }) {
  const { work } = await openWorkspace(root, { packs });
  const receiptsLedger = openLedger(root, 'receipts');
  const eventsLedger = openLedger(root, 'events');
  const ledger = { receipts: await receiptsLedger.verify(), events: await eventsLedger.verify() };
  if (!ledger.receipts.ok || !ledger.events.ok) {
    return { ledger, next: null, activeRun: null, waiting: [], stale: [], drift: [], warnings: work.warnings };
  }
  const receiptRecords = await receiptsLedger.readAll();
  const events = activeEvents(await eventsLedger.readAll());
  const runs = await createRunStore(root).list();
  const open = runs.find((r) => OPEN_STATES.has(r.state));
  const next = pickNext(work, runs);
  return {
    ledger,
    next: next ? { nodeId: next.id, level: next.level, kind: next.kind } : null,
    activeRun: open ? { runId: open.runId, nodeId: open.nodeId, state: open.state } : null,
    waiting: runs.filter((r) => r.state === 'waiting_children').map((r) => r.nodeId),
    stale: findStale({ work, receiptRecords, events }),
    drift: detectDrift(work, receiptRecords),
    warnings: work.warnings,
  };
}
```

---

## 범위 밖 (이 계획에서 하지 않는 것)

- MCP 서버·`folio_*` 도구·스킬 (M3). `workStatus`, `createRunner`, `structure/ops`가 M3 도구의 몸통이 된다.
- 소설 팩의 실제 프롬프트·vibelore 검사기 이식 (M3/M5). 여기서는 가짜 팩만 쓴다.
- 등장 위치 제출·인용문 대조·이름 바꾸기 (M4). M1의 `scanMentions`는 이름 일치 후보일 뿐이다.
- 요청 묶음 최적화(vibelore처럼 검사·검토를 한 왕복에 묶기). 실행기 계약은 이미 `requests[]` 배열이라 M3/M5에서 넣을 수 있다.
- `branching`/`none` 순서의 경로별 검사, 판본 `main` 외 값, 지면·내보내기.
- 기록장 성능(추가할 때마다 전체 읽기)과 찢어진 마지막 줄 복구. v0.1 규모에서는 문제없고, 필요해지면 따로 계획한다.

## 자기 점검 결과

- **계획서 대조:** M1 항목(설정 검증, 트리 왕복, 엔티티·약속·자료·그림, 추가만 되는 기록장, index.db 재생성, 읽는 순서 위치, 코어 자리 4개, 손수정 감지) → Task 3~11. M2 항목(단계 정의·덮어쓰기·잠금 거부, 저장·재개, needs_model/needs_user, 영수증·건너뜀·참고 버전, hash 대조, 위층 재귀·묶기, 앞쪽 범위·뒤쪽 전파, 검사 세트·사용자 검사 심각도, 가짜 팩) → Task 12~20. 비어 있는 항목 없음.
- **잠금 규칙 7개:** hash 일치(Task 16·17·20), 영수증 필수(16·20), hard 차단(16·17·20), 기록장 추가만(9·17·20), 자기검토 표기(14·16·17·20), soft 비자동수정(17·20), 모델 직접 호출 금지(Task 1 가드 + 실행기 계약).
- **이름 일관성:** `commitNode`/`buildReceipt`/`gatherScope`/`findStale`/`recheckStart`/`packPayload`/`OPEN_STATES`는 정의한 Task와 쓰는 Task에서 같은 이름·인자를 쓴다.
